# Zoho Desk (Enterprise edition): full feature inventory from the official docs

Built 1 Nov 2026 as the reference for the TAVI Desk go-live parity check. It is an itemised list of every user-visible Zoho Desk capability found in Zoho's official documentation, with the edition each needs.

## How it was built

- **Help docs:** the full English Zoho Desk help centre (help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk) was read through its public help-centre API. That is 207 categories and **897 articles**, each read in full, not summarised.
- **Edition data:** the edition comparison page (https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html) was parsed row by row. That is 360+ feature rows with values for Free, Express, Standard, Professional and Enterprise, plus each row's tooltip. It is the source of every "Ed:" value and per-edition limit unless an article says otherwise. The page redirects by IP (Egypt gets the Arabic page), so the English version was used.
- **API:** the REST API doc (https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument) gave 1,192 operation anchors. API-only capabilities are listed with `UI: API`, and section 18 has one line per resource group.
- **Earlier single-area inventories:** these were reused and condensed rather than redone. Their full detail stays in:
  - docs/desk-assignment-rules/reference/zoho-docs-inventory.md + zoho-live-notes.md
  - docs/desk-workflow-rules/reference/zoho-docs-inventory.md + zoho-live-notes.md
  - docs/desk-blueprint/reference/zoho-docs-inventory.md
  - docs/desk-modules-fields/reference/zoho-docs-inventory.md + zoho-desk-live-inventory.md
  - docs/desk-activity-lifecycle/reference/zoho-docs-inventory.md
  - docs/desk-ticket-replies/reference/zoho-docs-inventory.md
- **Web search and fetch:** not needed, because every page was reached directly. Nothing came from memory.

## How to read an item

`- **ID** Capability — options / limits / defaults — UI: where it appears — Ed: edition — [src](url)`

- **Ed:**
  - `All` means every edition, Free included.
  - `Express+`, `Standard+` and `Professional+` mean that edition and higher.
  - `Enterprise` means Enterprise only.
  - `n/d` means no edition is documented.
  - Per-edition limits are written as Free/Express/Standard/Professional/Enterprise, or as "Pro 15/dept, Ent 30/dept".
- **Enterprise baseline:** TAVI clones Enterprise, so **every item marked any edition applies**. An item matters most when it is marked `Enterprise`, or has an Enterprise-specific limit.
- **(inferred):** read from context, not stated by the source.
- **IDs:** stable. Add new items at the end of a sub-section with the next free number, and never renumber.
- **Gaps / thin docs:** each section ends with a list of what the docs did not cover or where sources conflict. Check those points on live Zoho, read-only.

## Enterprise-only capabilities at a glance (from the pricing page)

- **Automation:**
  - Custom functions (Deluge) in workflows and blueprints, and Zia Actions.
  - Schedules (10 per department) and contracts/support plans.
  - Deluge assistant bot.
  - Skills (30 per department) and skill-based assignment.
  - Blueprint strict mode.
- **Customisation:** layout rules and validation rules (50 per department each), multiple layouts (20 per department), custom modules (10), regular expressions.
- **Help center:**
  - Multi-brand help center, custom script, HTML customisation.
  - Auto-translation of KB articles, answer bot in ASAP.
  - Employee self-service.
  - Guided Conversations: 25 flows per department.
- **Channels:** live chat via SalesIQ in ASAP, chatbots in IM, multi-level IVR.
- **Analytics:** scheduled reports (100), Blueprint dashboard, Zia dashboard.
- **Security and data:** data sharing, field encryption and system field encryption, audit log, sandbox (3).
- **AI:** pre-built Zoho Desk AI agents.
- **Contacts:** Contacts and Accounts history.
- **Other:** on-demand mobile apps, department add-on (up to 450).
- **Raised limits on Enterprise:**
  - Tags per ticket: 50.
  - Email channels: 100.
  - Web forms: 50.
  - Business hours and holiday lists: 100 each.
  - Profiles: 50. Roles: 250.
  - Webhooks: 10 active.
  - API credits: 100,000 + 1,000 per user.
  - IM: 50 channels per platform.
  - ASAP: 50 widgets.
  - Social: 2 brands.
  - Departments: 50.
  - Light agents: 50 free.

## Conflicts between official sources (detail is in each section's Gaps list)

- **Field Update / "field watching" workflow trigger:** the article says Enterprise; pricing says Professional+.
- **Happiness rating:** pricing says Express+; the article says Standard+.
- **Answer Bot:** pricing says Standard+; a ChatGPT FAQ says Enterprise.
- **Live chat:** one pricing row says Enterprise only; the SalesIQ integration row says Standard+.
- **Light agents on Express:** the article says add-on; pricing says none.
- **Webhook "update specific fields" cap:** 5 in the help article, 20 in the API doc.
- **Round-robin threshold:** 1–200 in the FAQ, 1–500 in the API.
- **Automation Studio blocks:** 25 per rule in the article, 50 in the FAQ.
- **Merge limit:** contacts/accounts 3 in one article, 5 in another. Tickets: up to 5.
- **Import and export limits:**
  - Import is 30 MB / 10,000 rows in the article, but per-edition batches on the pricing page.
  - Export is given as 50,000, 3,000 or 1,000 in different places.
- **Audit-log retention:** given as 1 year, 2 years 20 days, or "coming soon".
- **Execution order of automations:** four different official sequences.
- **Deluge:** pricing says Enterprise only, but the API dashboard shows a Custom Function component in Professional.

## 1. Tickets (ZT)

Edition note: All editions (incl. Free) have email tickets, comments, spam, ticket history, resolution note, Peek, rich-text editor, drafts and advanced search. Express+ adds custom/starred views, Table view, merge, split, clone, timeline, read/unread, pinned conversations, custom statuses, keyboard shortcuts and Agent queue. Standard+ adds followers, time entry, manual timer, work modes, real-time list/detail updates and real-time starred-view counts. Professional+ adds approvals, team ownership, ticket sharing, Send as email, mass reply, scheduled reply, Teams queue, agent collision (detection, chat, reply avoidance), All-department view, ticket templates (600/dept), ticket ID customisation, automatic time tracking, billing and parent-child (early access). Tags per ticket: Free 10, Express 10, Standard 20, Pro 30, Ent 50. Nothing in this area is Enterprise-only; Enterprise differs only in limits (tags 50, layouts 20/dept, which carries over to layout-specific templates).

### 1.1 Ticket list: standard (system) views
- **ZT-01** Standard views are fixed — they cannot be edited or deleted. Some show only in All Departments, others only inside one department — UI: Tickets > left panel > Views / All Views — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-02** User-specific standard views: All Tickets, My Tickets, My Open Tickets, My On Hold Tickets, My Overdue Tickets, My Response Overdue Tickets, My Teams Open Tickets, Unassigned Open Tickets — UI: Views — Ed: All (My Teams needs Teams, Pro+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-03** Status-specific standard views: Closed, On Hold, Open, Overdue, Tickets for Review, Customer Responded, Response Overdue, Missed Chats, SLA Violated. The article counts "18 view options" plus archived — UI: Views — Ed: All (Tickets for Review needs Review ticket replies, Pro+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-04** On Hold Tickets / My On Hold Tickets views list tickets whose status maps to the On Hold state, sorted by Customer Response Time by default — UI: Views — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/on-hold-state-use-cases-and-behavior)
- **ZT-05** Overdue views (Overdue, My Overdue, Response Overdue, My Response Overdue) leave out On Hold tickets — UI: Views — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/on-hold-state-use-cases-and-behavior)
- **ZT-06** Missed Chats view: tickets created automatically from missed chats (agents busy or outside hours) — UI: Views > Missed Chats — Ed: All (needs chat channel) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-07** Spam Tickets view: holds tickets marked as spam; open one and click Not Spam to restore it — UI: Views > Spam — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-08** Shared Tickets / Shared Open Tickets views: the only place a receiving department sees tickets shared with it — UI: All Views > Shared — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/sharing-tickets-other-departments)
- **ZT-09** Blueprint views (department view only): Active Blueprint Tickets, All Transitions, My Team Transitions, My Transitions, Unassigned Transitions — UI: All Views (department selected) — Ed: Professional+ (blueprint) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-10** Archived Tickets view: closed tickets with no activity for 120 days are archived automatically; reached through All Views > ARCHIVED > Archived Tickets in a department — UI: All Views > ARCHIVED — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-11** Archive rules: closed (or a custom status mapped to Closed) for 120 days with no reply, task or transfer. No manual archiving, no bulk unarchive. Tickets with open activities or pending approvals are never archived. Archived tickets still appear in search and Analytics but not in default/custom views, All Departments or mobile apps. A new conversation or a property change (e.g. moving department) unarchives the ticket — UI: system — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-12** Archived custom views: custom views not opened for 60 days are archived, kept 30 days, then deleted permanently (not to Recycle Bin). Opening one within those 30 days restores it. Criteria can be viewed read-only (right-click > Show Criteria) but cannot be edited or renamed — UI: All Views > ARCHIVED > Archived View — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/archiving-custom-views)
- **ZT-13** All Departments view: shows tickets from every department on one screen. Custom views are not available in it (support can enable on request) — UI: department switcher > All Departments — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZT-14** Agent queue: open tickets grouped under each agent — UI: Tickets left side bar > Agent queue — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZT-15** Teams queue: open tickets grouped under each team. Agent and team queues also show On Hold tickets — UI: Tickets left side bar > Team Queues — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/on-hold-state-use-cases-and-behavior)

### 1.2 Custom views, filters and sorting
- **ZT-16** Create custom view: View Name, filter criteria, Visible to (Only me / All agents / Specific agents), optional "Show in all departments" — UI: Tickets > department > expand menu > + next to Views > Create New View — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/creating-custom-ticket-list-views)
- **ZT-17** Edit, delete or clone a custom view from its ⋯ menu. A clone gets the "clone" tag in its name unless renamed — UI: All Views > view ⋯ > Edit/Delete/Clone — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/creating-custom-ticket-list-views)
- **ZT-18** Custom view criteria can use "Age in Days" on Date/DateTime fields (e.g. created in the last 7 days) and happiness-rating conditions (Bad, link provided / not provided) — UI: Create New View > criteria — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-19** Status-group criteria: "Status is OPEN / ON HOLD / CLOSED" matches every status mapped to that state, in views, reports and automation — UI: criteria editor — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/understanding-the-onhold-ticket-state)
- **ZT-20** On Hold filters: Hours since On Hold and Ticket On Hold Time. These are not offered in SLA or Round Robin rules — UI: criteria editor — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/on-hold-state-use-cases-and-behavior)
- **ZT-21** Shared-ticket custom view: criteria that show tickets shared with other departments, combinable with e.g. Status is OPEN — UI: Create New View — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/sharing-tickets-other-departments)
- **ZT-22** Predefined views cannot be edited; add a custom view instead — UI: Views — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-23** Filter pane: ticket, customer and activity filters on standard and custom fields (Lookup, Date, Datetime, Picklist). Field search box. Max 250 filters, 50 values per field, org-wide. Fields the agent has no permission for are hidden. Filters apply only to the user who sets them and show an accurate ticket count — UI: Tickets > Filter icon beside views — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/creating-custom-ticket-list-views)
- **ZT-24** Saved filters: apply filters on a standard or custom view, then Save with a name. They appear under "My saved filter"; hover to rename or delete. Editing offers "Save changes" or "Save as new filter". Tickets module only — UI: Tickets > view > Filter > Save — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/creating-custom-ticket-list-views)
- **ZT-25** Display preferences: filter by agent, status, priority, channel or due date; Show Oldest First; sort by Created Time, Recent Thread or Due Date; "received in" period (last 15 days, 30 days, 3 months, All days); clickable total ticket count — UI: Ticket list > More (⋯) top right — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)
- **ZT-26** "Ticket received in" filter set below All days hides older tickets (listed as a common reason a ticket "can't be found") — UI: list display filter — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)

### 1.3 List layouts, Peek, star, export
- **ZT-27** Three list layouts: Classic (subject, customer, status, etc.), Compact and Table, in both All Departments and department views. Classic and Compact cannot be customised — UI: Tickets > Classic View drop-down — Ed: Classic/Compact All; Table Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-28** Table view default columns: Ticket ID, Subject, Contact Name, Account Name, Customer Responded Time, Email, Due Date, Status, Ticket Owner, Channel/Phone — UI: Table view — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)
- **ZT-29** Table columns: Select Columns icon (next to Ticket ID) to add standard or custom fields, uncheck to hide, drag to reorder. Max 15 columns. Ticket ID is fixed as the first column. Multi-line/text-area fields are not allowed. Blank values show "-". Settings are per department and shared with everyone the view is shared with — UI: Table view > Select Columns — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-30** Clip / Wrap text option for the subject column in Table view — UI: Table view — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-31** Move a ticket from the list: in All Departments Classic/Compact, click the department label on the ticket and pick another department — UI: list row > department label — Ed: Professional+ (multi-department) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)
- **ZT-32** Ticket Peek: hover a row and click the Peek icon to see all conversations, properties and related KB articles. You can reply from Peek, and it shows collision presence — UI: list row > Peek icon — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-33** Star a view: star icon next to the view name. Starred views sit at the top of the left panel with a ticket count. Max 15 per department and 15 in All Departments. Stars are per user — UI: view header > Star — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)
- **ZT-34** Real-time ticket count in starred views — UI: Starred Views — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZT-35** Real-time updates in the ticket list and detail view, without reload — UI: list/detail — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZT-36** Export View from a view's More menu: All Records, Specific Fields, or All Fields in this View (Table view only). Produces a CSV to download from Setup > Data Administration > Export, plus an in-app notification. Needs the Export Records profile permission. Lookups export as IDs. Not offered for empty views, blueprint views or custom-module list views — UI: view ⋯ > Export — Ed: All (Free/Express 1000/batch) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-37** Open in New Tab from a view's More menu — UI: view ⋯ > Open in New Tab — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-38** Read/unread indicator; mark a ticket read or unread from the list or detail view. Per user (other agents and the owner are not affected or notified). No bulk read/unread — UI: list row / detail — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/user-actions-in-the-ticket-detail-page)
- **ZT-39** Parent/child indicator next to the subject in the list view — UI: list row — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/linking-tickets/articles/understanding-parent-child-ticketing-system)

### 1.4 Work modes and Headquarters
- **ZT-40** Work modes: column (kanban-like) views per user and department; also work on custom views. Open tickets only (switch to Classic/Compact/Table for closed). Updates live without refresh — UI: Tickets > views drop-down > Work Mode — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/work-modes/articles/ticket-work-modes)
- **ZT-41** Status mode: one column per status. Add columns for existing statuses (including On Hold-mapped ones). "Other Status" collects the rest — UI: Work Mode > Status — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/work-modes/articles/ticket-work-modes)
- **ZT-42** Countdown mode: default columns Overdue, Due in Next 1 Hour, Due in 6 Hours or More, No Due Date, plus an On Hold column sorted by Customer Responded Time. Up to 7 columns. Custom columns between 5 min and 2 h. Overdue and No Due Date cannot be deleted — UI: Work Mode > Countdown — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/on-hold-state-use-cases-and-behavior)
- **ZT-43** Priority mode: High, Medium, Low, Other Priority. Open tickets are listed before On Hold ones; On Hold cards show the status and when they were put on hold — UI: Work Mode > Priority — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/work-modes/articles/ticket-work-modes)
- **ZT-44** Handshake mode (needs the Zoho CRM integration): system columns Contacts/Leads, Customers (won deals), Potentials (open deals), New (not in CRM). Columns cannot be edited, and empty ones are hidden — UI: Work Mode > Handshake — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/work-modes/articles/ticket-work-modes)
- **ZT-45** Add custom columns in Status/Countdown/Priority modes through ⋯ > + Add column. Default columns cannot be edited — UI: Work Mode ⋯ — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/work-modes/articles/ticket-work-modes)
- **ZT-46** Mass actions per work-mode column (up to 50): assign, update, reply, comment, delete, macro, revoke blueprint, update resolution. Merge up to 5 tickets within one column — UI: column header — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/work-modes/articles/ticket-work-modes)
- **ZT-47** Opening a ticket from a work mode lists the other tickets of that mode in the left panel — UI: detail left panel — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/work-modes/articles/ticket-work-modes)
- **ZT-48** Headquarters (HQ) dashboard, 8 live components: Incoming/Outgoing Bandwidth, Live Traffic (last hour), Unassigned (On hold / Open / Due in 1 h / Overdue), Online Agents, Offline Agents, Most Threaded Tickets (top 25, 30 days), Customer Happiness (25 days), Negative Ratings (25 days). Needs the Administrator Dashboard permission — UI: Tickets side bar > Headquarters — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/headquarters-dashboard)
- **ZT-49** HQ bandwidth filters: Today, Last 7/30 days, Current Week/Month, Custom; channel filter (email, chat, phone). Default is the last 24 h — UI: HQ > Bandwidth — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/headquarters-dashboard)
- **ZT-50** HQ agent components: per-channel online/offline with overdue, due in 1 h and pending counts; agents with the most overdue tickets listed first; assign or reassign from there — UI: HQ > Online/Offline Agents — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/headquarters-dashboard)

### 1.5 Ticket detail page
- **ZT-51** Default two-column detail layout: properties on the left, conversation on the right — UI: ticket detail — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-52** Three-column layout: pin the ticket list (hover the List icon in the left panel > Pin) to get list | properties | conversation. The list column defaults to All Views and can switch views. Unpin to go back — UI: detail > List icon > Pin — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-53** Conversation view: replies and comments in one stream with type icons; comments show private/public. Default on first open; can be turned off in Setup > Personalization > Preferences — UI: detail header toggle — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-54** Thread view: email exchanges grouped by thread in date order; comments live under a separate Comments tab — UI: detail header toggle — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-55** Private comments show on a light-yellow background, public ones on light-blue. Comment editor and reply details are covered in the Replies area (cross-ref) — UI: conversation — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/following-commenting-and-viewing-original-email-headers-in-tickets)
- **ZT-56** Ticket Timeline: Timeline icon in the left side bar lists the contact's previous tickets (Subject, Owner, Status) in date order, or only the current one if none. You can merge from it — UI: detail left side bar > Timeline — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
- **ZT-57** Ticket tabs (subtabs): Conversation, Resolution, Time Entry, Attachments, Activity, Approval, History, Parent-Child (when enabled) — UI: detail top tabs — Ed: varies per tab — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-58** History tab (audit trail): agent actions (reply, property edits, owner change, macro), customer actions (response, notification, property updates) and automated actions (workflow, assignment rule, SLA alert); also records cloning — UI: detail > History — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-59** Show Original: view, copy or download the full email header. Ticket threads only, not comments — UI: thread ⋯ > Show Original — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/actions-in-ticket-conversation)
- **ZT-60** Print a ticket with its email conversation. Internal comments print only with the Print Tickets extension — UI: detail > Print — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/actions-in-ticket-conversation)
- **ZT-61** Add to Prediction: manually add a ticket to Zia field-prediction training and pick the fields — UI: detail ⋯ > Add to prediction — Ed: n/d (Zia) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/actions-in-ticket-conversation)
- **ZT-62** CRM info in a ticket: the CRM icon next to customer info in the left pane shows CRM contact/account data (subscription, open deals, notes). Ticket Properties icon goes back; "View in CRM" opens Zoho CRM — UI: detail left pane > CRM icon — Ed: Standard+ (Zoho CRM integration) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/viewing-zoho-crm-information-inside-tickets)
- **ZT-63** Suggested KB articles shown next to the ticket (also in Peek) — UI: detail side panel — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZT-64** Ticket ID mentions in comments or feeds are hyperlinked automatically — UI: comments/feeds — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-customization/articles/customizing-your-ticket-id-starting-number)

### 1.6 Create and edit tickets
- **ZT-65** Add Ticket form: + in the top bar (or T + shortcut). Fields: Department (if more than one), Contact Name (suggestions), Account (auto-filled from contact), Email, Phone, Subject, other layout fields. Submit sends the customer an acknowledgement with the ticket ID — UI: top bar + > Ticket — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)
- **ZT-66** Manual ticket defaults: a new contact is created if none matches; the creating agent becomes the owner; channel defaults to Phone; status defaults to the department's default status — UI: Add Ticket — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-67** Attachments on create: up to 20 MB — UI: Add Ticket — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)
- **ZT-68** Contact is mandatory on every ticket. Changing a ticket's account relinks the contact (prompts to update existing tickets) when the contact has no account; it is blocked when the contact already has one — UI: detail/edit — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)
- **ZT-69** Add ticket for an account: Customers > Accounts > open account > Tickets tab > Add Ticket. Entering a new contact creates it and links it to the account — UI: Account detail > Tickets > Add Ticket — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/linking-tickets/articles/adding-and-submitting-a-ticket-for-an-account)
- **ZT-70** Contact's Tickets tab lists their tickets with status and owner, sortable by received order — UI: Contact detail > Tickets — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/linking-tickets/articles/associating-records-with-contacts)
- **ZT-71** Edit ticket: change subject, description and properties from the detail page — UI: detail > Edit / inline — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/user-actions-in-the-ticket-detail-page)
- **ZT-72** Change Layout: switch a ticket to another layout within the department. Blocked while a blueprint is running. Also available from the left-panel layout option — UI: detail ⋯ > Change Layout — Ed: Enterprise for multi-layout (20/dept) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/user-actions-in-the-ticket-detail-page)
- **ZT-73** Ticket templates: preset standard and custom field values (Name, Show to Contact in Help Center, Subject, Description, Status, Owner, other fields), per department and layout. Needs the Email and Ticket Templates permission — UI: Setup > Customization > Templates > Ticket Templates > New Ticket Template — Ed: Professional+ (600/dept) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/templates/articles/creating-ticket-templates)
- **ZT-74** Apply a template: Add Ticket > Choose Ticket Template. It overwrites field values, and switching templates loses typed data. Customers can also pick a template in the Help Center when it is marked Show to Contact — UI: Add Ticket header — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/templates/articles/using-ticket-and-email-templates-in-a-ticket-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZT-75** Hide tickets from the Help Center by moving them to a department not shown in the Help Center, then disabling "Display Tickets from private departments" — UI: Setup > Departments; Setup > Channels > Help Center > Access Settings — Ed: Professional+ (multi-department) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)
- **ZT-76** Only one product per ticket (Product is a lookup) — UI: Product field — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)
- **ZT-77** Standard ticket fields (Department, Contact, Account, Email, Phone, Subject, Description, Status, Product, Owner, Due Date, Priority, Channel, Category/Sub-Category, Attachment). Full field inventory in desk-modules-fields (cross-ref) — UI: layout — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)

### 1.7 Ticket actions (single ticket)
- **ZT-78** Assign or reassign owner: Avatar in the list row or detail left menu; pick agent or team, with search. Agents in several teams show their teams; email shown under the name. You can assign to a specific agent within a team. The assignee is notified by email, and every member is notified for a team assignment — UI: list avatar / detail owner — Ed: All (teams Pro+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/assignment-rules-notification/articles/assign-tickets-manually)
- **ZT-79** Self-pick unassigned tickets with the View Unassigned permission. There is no documented "Assign to me" button (see desk-assignment-rules) — UI: owner picker — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/assignment-rules-notification/articles/assign-tickets-manually)
- **ZT-80** Move to another department (needs Move Tickets permission): the ticket becomes new in the target, status resets to Open, and old automations/SLA are erased. Parent-child links are kept only if the target has the feature on — UI: detail ⋯ > Move; Peek > More Actions > Move — Ed: Professional+ (multi-department) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/user-actions-in-the-ticket-detail-page)
- **ZT-81** Share with departments (Support Administrator permission): up to 10 departments, each with Full (default), Restricted or Read-only access. Shared users can never change status or assignee. The receiving department cannot re-share; data stays synced — UI: detail ⋯ > Share, or iBar Share Ticket — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/sharing-tickets-other-departments)
- **ZT-82** Share access levels: Full = everything except status/owner; Restricted = private comments and internal forward, no customer reply; Read-only = view and private comments — UI: Share Ticket panel — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/sharing-tickets-other-departments)
- **ZT-83** Unshare: Shared badge in the left panel > Delete icon per department > Unshare. The child department cannot unshare or change permissions. The detail page shows the source department and access level — UI: detail left panel > Shared badge — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/sharing-tickets-other-departments)
- **ZT-84** "Receiving a shared ticket" notification per department (email/SMS template editable) — UI: Setup > Customization > Notifications > Department Notifications — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/sharing-tickets-other-departments)
- **ZT-85** Move vs Share: a move goes to one department and the agent loses access; a share goes to up to 10 departments with set access and the source keeps it — UI: n/a — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)
- **ZT-86** Follow / Unfollow (also Shift+W). Updates go to the Notification Center only: no email alerts, and no "followed tickets" view (Marketplace extension "My Followed Tickets") — UI: detail ⋯ > Follow — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/user-actions-in-the-ticket-detail-page)
- **ZT-87** Mark as spam (optionally mark the contact as spam too, so future mail goes to spam). Not Spam reverses it. Spam tickets are purged every 30 days — UI: detail ⋯ / list select > Mark as Spam — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/user-actions-in-the-ticket-detail-page)
- **ZT-88** Automatic spam detection setting, with "Do not auto-mark contact as spam if valid tickets exist" — UI: Setup > Customization > General Settings > Contacts — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-89** Clone: needs create permission. Adds "(Clone)" to the subject and copies properties and description only (no attachments, threads or comments). Not for shared or spam tickets. Send as Email is unavailable on clones. Logged in History — UI: detail ⋯ > Clone — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/user-actions-in-the-ticket-detail-page)
- **ZT-90** Delete a ticket: goes to the Recycle Bin for 60 days, then is purged — UI: detail ⋯ > Delete — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/user-actions-in-the-ticket-detail-page)
- **ZT-91** Restore deleted tickets: Recycle Bin (Details, Entity, Deleted By, Deleted Time) > Restore one or many; permanent delete; Empty Recycle Bin. Actions run in a queue. Restored tickets follow role/permission visibility. Needs the Recycle Bin permission — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Recycle Bin — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/recycle-bin/articles/using-the-recycle-bin)
- **ZT-92** Collision prompt when another agent deletes or moves the ticket you are on: restore and stay, or follow it to the new department — UI: detail prompt — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/avoiding-agent-collision-over-tickets-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZT-93** Merge: Find and Merge permission; same department only; up to 5 tickets; the oldest is master by default (can choose another); pick per field which ticket's value to keep (created time, time to respond and on-hold time always come from the master); confirm with "Yes, Merge". Cannot be undone. Merged-away tickets go to the Recycle Bin (restorable). All threads, comments and attachments are combined by time and visible to the master's contact in the Help Center — UI: list select > Merge; Timeline > Merge — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/merging-tickets)
- **ZT-94** Split: turn an incoming thread into a new ticket. Not offered on the description or agent replies, and only that one reply moves (later threads stay) — UI: thread ⋯ > Split as new ticket — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/actions-in-ticket-conversation)
- **ZT-95** Revoke Blueprint on a ticket (e.g. before moving it) — UI: detail ⋯ > Revoke Blueprint — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/user-actions-in-the-ticket-detail-page)
- **ZT-96** Send as Email (outbound email that becomes a ticket). Details in the Replies area (cross-ref) — UI: top bar + — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZT-97** Closing is never permanent: a customer reply reopens the ticket (status falls back to default) — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)
- **ZT-98** File ticket as bug/issue via extensions (Zoho BugTracker, Jira) — UI: detail ⋯ (extension) — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/actions-in-ticket-conversation)

### 1.8 Tags
- **ZT-99** Add a tag in the detail page Tags field (or Shift+T). Autocomplete suggests existing tags; a new name creates the tag. Tags are per department. Needs the Manage Tags permission — UI: detail left pane > Tags — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/working-with-ticket-tags)
- **ZT-100** Tag validation: max 50 characters; letters and numbers only (no special characters); at least 3 characters to save — UI: Tags field — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/working-with-ticket-tags)
- **ZT-101** Tags per ticket by edition: Free 10, Express 10, Standard 20, Professional 30, Enterprise 50 — UI: Tags field — Ed: All — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZT-102** Remove a tag from a ticket: hover the tag > X — UI: detail Tags — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)
- **ZT-103** Tags panel: Recent Tags (last 5) and All Tags (most used first, with ticket counts). Click a tag to list its tickets — UI: Tickets left panel > Tags — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/working-with-ticket-tags)
- **ZT-104** Search tickets by tag: search icon next to Tags — UI: Tags panel — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/working-with-ticket-tags)
- **ZT-105** Rename a tag: applies everywhere, including spam, archived and binned tickets, views, reports and rules. Renaming to an existing name offers to delete this tag and move its tickets to the existing one ("Transfer tickets…") — UI: Tags > tag ⋯ > Edit — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/working-with-ticket-tags)
- **ZT-106** Delete a tag: removed from every ticket. Views, reports and rules using it stop working — UI: Tags > tag ⋯ > Delete — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/working-with-ticket-tags)

### 1.9 Real-time collaboration and pins
- **ZT-107** Collision bubble: count of agents viewing the same ticket; glows red when others are present — UI: detail header (also Peek) — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/avoiding-agent-collision-over-tickets-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZT-108** User presence monitor: avatars of active viewers/repliers and faded recent viewers with the time they left; hover for name and status — UI: collision flag panel — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/avoiding-agent-collision-over-tickets-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZT-109** Collision activity feed: live list of changes since you opened the ticket; unread items on a light-blue background — UI: collision flag — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/avoiding-agent-collision-over-tickets-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZT-110** Floating alerts (fade after a few seconds) when another agent views, drafts a reply, replies, leaves, comments or edits a comment — UI: over the detail left menu — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/avoiding-agent-collision-over-tickets-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZT-111** Reply avoidance: clicking Reply while someone else is drafting warns both agents and asks whether to continue — UI: prompt box — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/avoiding-agent-collision-over-tickets-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZT-112** Collision chat: one-on-one or group chat titled with the ticket ID; Leave option — UI: Flag > agent > Chat / Group Chat — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/avoiding-agent-collision-over-tickets-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZT-113** Pin a comment or thread (private or public): max 6 per ticket = 3 "Visible only to me" (grey) + 3 "Visible to all Agents" (orange; needs the Pin conversation for all agents permission). Shown as a ribbon at the top; hover shows who pinned it and when — UI: thread/comment ⋯ > Pin — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/pinning-comments-and-threads-in-tickets)
- **ZT-114** Unpin: when pinned both ways, choose Only for me / For all agents / Both — UI: ⋯ > Unpin — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/pinning-comments-and-threads-in-tickets)
- **ZT-115** Team Feeds: internal feed of ticket updates on the Tickets home, different from comments — UI: Tickets side bar > Team Feeds — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)

### 1.10 Mass and bulk actions
- **ZT-116** Mass action covers up to 50 selected tickets; bulk action covers more than 50 — UI: list checkbox selection > top panel — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/performing-bulk-updates)
- **ZT-117** Action matrix (mass/bulk): Assign Y/Y, Update Y/Y, Reply Y/N, Comment Y/N, Merge Y/N, Close Y/N, Delete Y/Y, Move N/Y (only with more than one department), Mark as Spam Y/N, Macro Y/N, Revoke Blueprint Y/Y, Update Resolution Y/Y — UI: list top panel — Ed: n/d (mass reply Pro+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/performing-bulk-updates)
- **ZT-118** Mass Update: Field to Update + New Value > Save. May fire automations, whose alerts go to Notifications — UI: Update — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-119** Bulk assign needs the Change Ownership permission (all / unassigned only / none); Assign to > Team for teams — UI: Assign To — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-120** Bulk close (OK to confirm) does not fire notification rules — UI: Close — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/performing-bulk-updates)
- **ZT-121** Mass Update Resolution replaces any existing resolution — UI: Update Resolution — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/performing-bulk-updates)
- **ZT-122** Mass spam (max 50) with "Mark the associated contacts also as spam" — UI: Mark as Spam — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-123** Mass macro (per department) and mass Revoke Blueprint — UI: Macro / Revoke Blueprint — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/performing-bulk-updates)
- **ZT-124** Mass WhatsApp message (admins only, up to 50): template and language, Phone/Mobile/Both, primary contact only, de-duplicates contacts, can skip contacts with an ongoing chat, valid numbers only, queued — UI: Send WhatsApp Message — Ed: n/d (IM) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/performing-bulk-updates)
- **ZT-125** Mass reply and mass comment: see the Replies area (cross-ref) — UI: Reply / Comment — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/performing-bulk-updates)
- **ZT-126** Queue rules: max 11 bulk actions at once (1 running + 10 queued); one mass/bulk action per view at a time; a running bulk action cannot be discarded; up to 200,000 records per scheduler run. The initiator and admin get a completion email — UI: system — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/performing-bulk-updates)
- **ZT-127** Views where actions are blocked: no mass/bulk in Spam, Shared, Shared Open or Archived views; blueprint views allow mass but not bulk — UI: views — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/performing-bulk-updates)
- **ZT-128** Bulk Actions Log: Bulk tab (module, action, time, executor, view, department, status, failure reason) and Mass tab (Initiated/Completed, filter by department and action) — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Bulk Actions Log — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/performing-bulk-updates)

### 1.11 Parent-child tickets
- **ZT-129** Enable per department with preferences: auto-close children when the parent closes; OR allow closing the parent only when all children are closed (mutually exclusive); plus auto-close the parent when all children close — UI: Setup > Customization > General Settings > Tickets > Parent-Child Ticketing > Configure — Ed: Professional+ (early access) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/linking-tickets/articles/understanding-parent-child-ticketing-system)
- **ZT-130** Parent-Child subtab: create a new child/parent, associate existing tickets, clone the ticket as parent/child — UI: detail > Parent-Child — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/linking-tickets/articles/understanding-parent-child-ticketing-system)
- **ZT-131** Limits: one parent per child; 200 children per parent; no grandchildren; "association initiated" notice; only unlinked tickets can be picked; no archived, spam, binned or no-permission tickets — UI: Parent-Child — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/linking-tickets/articles/understanding-parent-child-ticketing-system)
- **ZT-132** Cross-department linking: feature on in both departments, the agent needs access (full-access share counts), and the parent department's close rules apply — UI: Parent-Child > department picker — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/linking-tickets/articles/understanding-parent-child-ticketing-system)
- **ZT-133** Reopening a child reopens the parent (in close-only-when-children-closed mode) but not the other children — UI: system — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/linking-tickets/articles/understanding-parent-child-ticketing-system)
- **ZT-134** Child mass actions in the subtab (max 50): dissociate, assign, update field, reply (department required), close, delete, macro, revoke blueprint, update resolution. Peer children are listed. Status and owner can be edited inline (not under blueprint) — UI: Parent-Child subtab — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/linking-tickets/articles/understanding-parent-child-ticketing-system)
- **ZT-135** Dissociate a link by hovering it > Dissociate icon > confirm — UI: Parent-Child — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/linking-tickets/articles/understanding-parent-child-ticketing-system)
- **ZT-136** Downgrading to Standard hides the UI but keeps the links in the back end; upgrading restores them — UI: n/a — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/linking-tickets/articles/understanding-parent-child-ticketing-system)

### 1.12 Statuses, On Hold, priority, channel
- **ZT-137** Default statuses: Open, On Hold, Escalated, Closed — UI: Status field — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/customizing-ticket-statuses)
- **ZT-138** Custom status: Name, Status Type (Open / On Hold / Closed), Fall-back to default. Per department, but the type mapping applies org-wide. Drag to reorder or "Sort Values Alphabetically" — UI: Setup > Customization > Layouts and Fields > Ticket Status > Add Status — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/customizing-ticket-statuses)
- **ZT-139** Set as default status: Open-type statuses only. Applies to incoming and Add Ticket form tickets and to reopened closed tickets — UI: Status list > ⋯ > Set as default — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/customizing-ticket-statuses)
- **ZT-140** Change type (status grouping): by default Open and Escalated map to Open — UI: Status list > ⋯ > Change type — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/customizing-ticket-statuses)
- **ZT-141** Sticky status: untick Fall-back to default (Open/Closed types) so a customer reply does not reset it. On Hold statuses always fall back — UI: Status list — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/customizing-ticket-statuses)
- **ZT-142** On Hold behaviour: SLA timers freeze and due times are hidden; the ticket leaves response/resolution-due views, rules and reports; no due emails or escalations — UI: system — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/on-hold-state-use-cases-and-behavior)
- **ZT-143** Resume from On Hold to Open recalculates due times (old due + hold time, using SLA business/calendar hours; a manually set resolution due uses calendar hours) and reschedules escalations. Closing from On Hold restores the old due times. Overdue tickets put on hold are not recalculated. Triggers: manual change, workflow, customer reply, feedback rating — UI: system — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/on-hold-state-use-cases-and-behavior)
- **ZT-144** On Hold statuses cannot be the default and the On Hold status is optional — UI: Status list — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/on-hold-state-use-cases-and-behavior)
- **ZT-145** Escalated status: set by SLA triggers or manually — UI: Status — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/customizing-ticket-statuses)
- **ZT-146** Priority values: High, Medium, Low, None; set manually, by automation or by priority SLAs — UI: Priority field — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-147** Channel field: Phone, Email, Web, Chat, Forums, Twitter, Facebook (plus IM channels); Phone by default for agent-created tickets — UI: Channel field — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-148** Ticket sources (channels) that create tickets: email, web form, Help Center, phone, chat, community, social, SMS, IM — UI: n/a — Ed: varies — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-on-tickets-management)

### 1.13 Ticket ID customisation
- **ZT-149** Ticket ID = prefix + starting number + suffix, shown with #. The first ticket is 101 by default. The next number can be raised (must be above the current highest; max 9 digits). Changes do not affect existing tickets. Needs Manage General Settings — UI: Setup > Customization > General Settings > Organization-wide Ticket ID — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-customization/articles/customizing-your-ticket-id-starting-number)
- **ZT-150** Prefix + suffix ≤ 16 characters. Not allowed: ` ~ # % ^ & * = + [ ] \ | : ; " < >, spaces and emojis. Live preview — UI: Ticket ID editor — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-customization/articles/customizing-your-ticket-id-starting-number)
- **ZT-151** Dynamic placeholders: {{DD}} {{MM}} {{YY}} {{YYYY}}, ${email} ${phone} ${subject} ${status} ${dueDate} ${priority} ${channel} ${category} ${subCategory} ${classification} ${language}, plus custom fields (not lookup, multi-lookup, text area or encrypted). Can mix with static text. Values are frozen at creation — UI: curly-brace picker — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-customization/articles/customizing-your-ticket-id-starting-number)
- **ZT-152** Trimming: 8 characters each when both affixes are long; a short one gives its unused room to the other — UI: system — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-customization/articles/customizing-your-ticket-id-starting-number)
- **ZT-153** Department-specific ticket ID (department, start number, affixes). Number series must not overlap other departments or the org series. Deleting one falls back to the org setting. Org ID goes inactive if every department has its own — UI: General Settings > Add department-specific Ticket ID — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-customization/articles/customizing-your-ticket-id-starting-number)
- **ZT-154** Hide Ticket ID in Outgoing Replies (per department); Show Ticket ID in Forwarded Tickets (per department, hidden by default) — UI: General Settings > Edit Configuration — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-customization/articles/customizing-your-ticket-id-starting-number)

### 1.14 Time tracking and time entry
- **ZT-155** Ticket timer: HH:MM:SS stopwatch at the top of the ticket with pause, resume, reset and submit to timesheet. Several can run at once — UI: detail top — Ed: Standard+ (manual) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZT-156** Tracking mode per department: Manual (default) or Automatic; can be disabled. Switching mode makes you add or discard running timers. Needs Manage Time Tracking — UI: Setup > Customization > Time Tracking — Ed: Automatic Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZT-157** Automatic tracking actions: Reply (mandatory, default), Draft (on Save Draft), Field update, Approval, Comment, Attachment, Resolution, Remote assistance. Workflow and AI-agent actions are not tracked — UI: Time Tracking settings — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZT-158** Automatic options: pause after 30 s idle; hide timer from agents; let agents edit their time (10-second review window) — UI: Time Tracking settings — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZT-159** Time Entry tab: add (time spent + cost), edit or delete entries for reports and billing — UI: detail > Time Entry — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-160** My Active Timers: pause, stop or reset running ticket/activity timers — UI: bottom bar > Pending Activities > My Active Timers — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZT-161** Billing preferences: Fixed cost per agent, Specific cost per agent, Specific cost per profile, Fixed cost per ticket; Billing History link — UI: Time Tracking page — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)

### 1.15 Approvals, resolution, attachments, activities
- **ZT-162** Approval: Approval tab > Submit For Approval (To = approver, Subject, Description). The approver gets an email link and approves or declines from the ticket; it shows Pending until then. Pending approvals stop archiving — UI: detail > Approval — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-163** Resolution: one per ticket. Save, or Save and Add Solution (opens Add Article). Edit/delete via ⋯. "Notify Contact" sends it to the customer — UI: detail > Resolution — Ed: All (KB article Express+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-164** Attachments tab: add one or many files (20 MB each). New files are Private; ⋯ > make Public for the Help Center; ⋯ > Delete — UI: detail > Attachments — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZT-165** Activity tab: add a Call, Task or Event linked to the ticket (owner = creator, reminders); tick the checkbox to complete. Open activities stop archiving. Details in the Activities area — UI: detail > Activity — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)

### Gaps / thin docs
- Split ticket: the article's step list is empty. The exact menu label ("Split as a new ticket") and which fields copy to the new ticket are not documented.
- Kanban: work modes are the only column/kanban view. No free-form kanban is documented.
- Keyboard shortcuts: only Shift+?, T+, Shift+W and Shift+T are in text. The full shortcut list lives in an in-app overlay, not the help text.
- Custom view limits (how many per department/user) and sort options inside a custom view are not documented. The custom view criteria operator list is not itemised.
- Classic vs Compact field differences are not described (Compact has no description in the article).
- Edition for HQ dashboard, Peek-reply, three-column layout, saved filters, custom buttons, print, add-to-prediction and archiving is not in pricing rows (marked n/d).
- Approvals: no approval-flow setup (multiple approvers, approver list rules) is documented beyond the FAQ.
- Mark read/unread UI steps are headings only (no menu path given).
- The FAQ says the ticket ID start must have ≥3 digits and "cannot include character prefixes", which contradicts the newer prefix/suffix article (Pro+). The newer article was taken as authoritative.
- Deleting a ticket from the detail page: confirm dialog text and permission name are not documented.
- Ticket history event list is only in API articles (developer-space get-ticket-history), not itemised here.
- Translation (multilingual ticket translation) article not itemised — belongs to Replies/AI.

## 2. Replies and conversations (ZR)

Edition note: Comments (private/public), private threads, the rich-text reply editor, response drafts, reply-article auto-suggestion, Default Send Behaviour, reply editor font and reply button behaviour are in all editions (Free+). Pinned conversations and suggested articles are Express+. Snippets and email templates in reply are Standard+ (Free/Express use default templates only). Review ticket replies, Send as Email, Mass Reply, Scheduled Reply, Approvals and Secondary contacts (CCs) are Professional+. Light agents (private comments only, no replies) are a paid add-on from Standard, 50 free on Enterprise. Nothing in this area is Enterprise-only except the free light-agent allowance.

### 2.1 Reply, Reply All, Forward
- **ZR-01** Reply — opens the editor addressed only to the ticket contact (To) — UI: ticket detail > Reply — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/actions-in-ticket-conversation)
- **ZR-02** Reply All — sends to everyone in the conversation; secondary contacts auto-added to CC; agent can add/remove CCs — UI: ticket detail > Reply drop-down — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/actions-in-ticket-conversation)
- **ZR-03** Removing a secondary contact from CC on Reply All does not remove their Help Center access; access ends only when removed from the ticket's Secondary Contact/CC field — UI: reply editor CC / ticket properties — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-replies/articles/enabling-and-adding-cc-users-on-tickets)
- **ZR-04** Forward — share ticket/thread with someone outside the conversation (e.g. dev team) — UI: ticket detail > Reply drop-down / More; thread More menu — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/actions-in-ticket-conversation)
- **ZR-05** Forwarded threads are always private, regardless of recipient — API `isPrivate` note "Forwarded threads are always private" — UI: thread label — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-replies/articles/public-and-private-threads-in-tickets)
- **ZR-06** Agent signature is not applied to forwarded emails — UI: forward editor — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/updating-user-preferences)
- **ZR-07** Ticket ID hidden in forwarded threads by default; "Show Ticket ID in Forwarded Tickets" per department — UI: Setup > Customization > General Settings > Tickets — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-customization/articles/customizing-your-ticket-id-starting-number)
- **ZR-08** Reply from a specific thread — each thread has its own More menu (reply, forward, split, pin, show original, print); API `inReplyToThreadId` — UI: thread hover > More (...) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/replying-to-tickets)
- **ZR-09** Multiple recipients in To — all except the original requestor are moved to CC automatically — UI: reply editor — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-replies/articles/enabling-and-adding-cc-users-on-tickets)
- **ZR-10** Reply in IM / Reply via Email — IM-channel tickets (WhatsApp, Telegram, Facebook…) answered in IM or switched to email; To auto-filled if contact has email, else agent types it — UI: ticket detail reply bar — Ed: Standard+ (IM channels: Std 1, Pro 10, Ent 50) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/actions-in-ticket-conversation)
- **ZR-11** WhatsApp 24-hour session window — after expiry agent must send an approved WhatsApp template to restart — UI: IM reply box — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/instant-messaging/articles/faqs-instant-messaging)
- **ZR-12** Social/forum/custom-channel replies — `sendReply` with `channel` EMAIL, FACEBOOK, TWITTER, FORUMS or integrated custom channel code; custom channel must handle the reply or it fails — UI: API (UI reply on social tickets) — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Threads_SendReplyviaIntegratedCustomChannels)
- **ZR-13** Resolution shared with customer — Resolution tab with "Notify Contact" checkbox instead of a reply; one resolution per ticket — UI: ticket detail > Resolution tab — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)

### 2.2 Private vs public threads
- **ZR-14** Reply visibility auto-set — reply is public when recipient is primary/secondary contact; replying/forwarding to a non-contact can be marked private — UI: reply editor — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-replies/articles/public-and-private-threads-in-tickets)
- **ZR-15** Setting "Automatically set Ticket threads as private, if requestor is not the recipient" — UI: Setup > Channels > Email > Advanced Configuration — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-replies/articles/public-and-private-threads-in-tickets)
- **ZR-16** Public thread visible in Help Center and contact notified; private thread not visible and no contact notification — UI: thread badge "Private" — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-replies/articles/public-and-private-threads-in-tickets)
- **ZR-17** Private threads count toward response time; workflows can trigger on private threads (incoming/outgoing/both) — UI: Setup > Automation > Workflows (Private Thread trigger) — Ed: All (workflows Standard+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-replies/articles/public-and-private-threads-in-tickets)

### 2.3 Comments (private / public)
- **ZR-18** Add comment — "Comment" button or Shift+C; private by default; drop-down next to Comment switches to Public — UI: ticket detail > Comment — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-replies/articles/private-and-public-comments-in-tickets)
- **ZR-19** Private comment visibility — internal users with ticket access only (admin, peers, other-department agents with access) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-replies/articles/private-and-public-comments-in-tickets)

- **ZR-104** Attachments — 20 MB per file on tickets; multiple files; private by default, can be made public; reply attachments via Uploads `attachmentIds` (other user's upload ID rejected) — UI: reply editor attach icon — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZR-105** Inline images — Insert icon documented in signature editor; inline images in reply body not explicitly documented (inferred) — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/updating-user-preferences)
- **ZR-106** Happiness rating link in replies — "To every response sent" or "When a ticket is closed"; placeholder `${Cases.Customer Happiness Rating}` — UI: Setup > Customer Happiness — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)

### 2.17 Send behaviour and reply button
- **ZR-107** Send and Send & Close; Default Send Behaviour preference (Send or Send & Close) — UI: Setup > Personalization > Preferences — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/updating-user-preferences)
- **ZR-108** Send and update status — pick a status at send (e.g. On Hold, Waiting for customer reply); on by default; toggle via Send drop-down > Open preferences — UI: reply editor Send drop-down — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/actions-available-while-replying-to-a-ticket)

### 2.18 Thread view and thread actions
- **ZR-111** Threads and comments as one conversation or separate tabs (preference); API `threadCommentViewType` recentTop/recentBottom — UI: Preferences — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/updating-user-preferences)

- **ZR-114** Split thread as new ticket — incoming replies only, moves that thread — UI: thread ... > Split — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/actions-in-ticket-conversation)
- **ZR-115** Failed sends / bounces — bounce added to conversation; Email Failure Alerts in notifications panel with resend; API list/clear alerts — UI: top-bar notifications — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)

### 2.19 Effects, permissions and automation
- **ZR-117** Customer reply reopens a closed ticket — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/tickets/articles/faqs-working-on-tickets)
- **ZR-118** First response time — from creation to first agent reply; only the first of consecutive replies counts; auto-acks excluded — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/response-and-resolution-times/articles/response-time-cases-example-and-important-notes)

- **ZR-120** Workflow triggers Customer Reply / Agent Response / Private Thread; custom actions Send Email Reply, Add Comment (public toggle), Reply via IM (max 5 custom actions per rule) — UI: Setup > Automation > Workflows — Ed: Standard+ (Std 5, Pro 15, Ent 30 rules per module per dept) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/workflows/articles/custom-actions-gallery)
- **ZR-121** Webhooks Ticket_Thread_Add (direction filter), Ticket_Comment_Add, Ticket_Comment_Update — UI: Setup > Developer Space > Webhooks — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/support/WebhookDocument.do)

### Gaps / thin docs
- Undo-send window (community report only, 5–30 s).
- Quoted previous thread / "include previous threads" behaviour and any "reply all by default" org setting (only per-user Reply Button Defaults documented).
- Editor toolbar contents (bold, lists, tables, spell check, emoji) not itemised; inline images in reply body undocumented.
- Allowed attachment file types, attachments per reply and total reply size; community cites 40 MB for paid editions.
- Mass comment and read receipts have no row in the pricing comparison; edition `n/d`.
- @mention UI and notification details only from API and community.
- Send as Email note says BCC cannot be added but the steps list BCC — contradictory in source.
- Owner auto-assignment on reply and whether a reply clears pending escalations — community only / undocumented.
- Department-level default From address (community says in rollout).

## 3. Contacts, Accounts and Products (ZC)

Edition note: Contact/account records, private notes and dedicated owners are in all editions. Merge contacts/accounts and custom customer views are Express+. Insights, deduplication, follow, contact-product association and the whole Products module (product-based ticket tracking, product owner, product custom fields and views) are Standard+. Secondary contacts (CCs) and contacts to multiple accounts are Professional+. Contacts and Accounts **History is Enterprise-only** (and admin-profile only). Custom fields for contacts/accounts: Free 0, Express 10, Standard 50, Professional 150, Enterprise 230 (products: Std 50, Pro 150, Ent 230). Layouts for Contacts, Accounts and Products are org-wide, not per department.

### 3.1 Contacts CRUD
- **ZC-01** Auto-create contact from first ticket — further tickets from same email attach to that contact — UI: automatic — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/contacts-and-accounts/articles/add-edit-and-delete-contacts)
- **ZC-02** Add contact manually — Last Name mandatory; creator becomes Contact Owner (changeable) — UI: Customers > Contacts > Add (+) > Add Contact > Submit — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/contacts-and-accounts/articles/add-edit-and-delete-contacts)
- **ZC-03** Create contact while sending a WhatsApp message — name + phone/mobile, channel + template; contact auto-saved; needs create-contact permission (message still sends without it); option shown only with View contact; hidden number field shows restricted-field alert; shortcut w+ — UI: Add (+) drop-down > Send Whatsapp Message — Ed: Standard+ (IM) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/contacts-and-accounts/articles/add-edit-and-delete-contacts)
- **ZC-04** Edit contact — detail > More Actions > Edit, or hover Edit icon in list view — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/contacts-and-accounts/articles/add-edit-and-delete-contacts)
- **ZC-05** Delete contact — More Actions > Delete > confirm; its tickets, activities and time entries go to Recycle Bin; contacts added as end users cannot be deleted — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/contacts-and-accounts/articles/add-edit-and-delete-contacts)
- **ZC-06** Add ticket for a contact — search contact > open > Tickets tab > Add; contact info pre-filled — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/contacts-and-accounts/articles/adding-a-ticket-for-a-contact)
- **ZC-07** Contact photo — delete contact photo — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Contacts_Deletecontactphoto)
- **ZC-08** Contacts API — Create, Get, List, List by IDs, Update, Update many, Delete, count, statistics, profiles, list tickets/accounts/products by contact — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Contacts_CreateContact)

### 3.2 Accounts CRUD
- **ZC-09** Ways to add accounts — manual, CSV/XLS import with contact mapping, Zoho CRM sync — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/contacts-and-accounts/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-accounts)
- **ZC-10** Add account — Account Name mandatory; creator becomes Account Owner (changeable) — UI: Customers > Accounts tab (lower-left) > Add Account — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/contacts-and-accounts/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-accounts)

- **ZC-12** Delete account — contacts, tickets, calls, tasks, events, time entries, contracts move to Recycle Bin; account dissociated from contacts; if primary for a contact, a secondary becomes primary; contacts only linked to it are deleted — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/contacts-and-accounts/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-accounts)
- **ZC-13** Restore / permanently delete account — Setup > Data Administration > Recycle Bin > Restore / Delete > Proceed — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/contacts-and-accounts/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-accounts)
- **ZC-14** Accounts API — Create, Get, List, count, Update, Update many, Delete, Merge, statistics, history, list contacts/products/tickets, contracts, associate/dissociate SLA — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Accounts_CreateAccount)

- **ZC-22** Primary (direct) vs secondary (indirect) accounts — Account Name field = primary; account can be primary for many contacts; secondary needs a primary (no orphan) — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/contacts-and-accounts/articles/associating-contacts-with-multiple-accounts)

### Gaps / thin docs
- Merge limit inconsistent (3 vs 5 records).
- Account follow UI and private-notes UI not described in KB (API only plus pricing row).
- Department-specific products: products added under a department; whether visibility is department-restricted is not stated.
- Contact & account insights page beyond the Overview tab not documented.
- Anonymous contacts view exists but anonymous contact behaviour undocumented here.
- CRM integration edition limits not in the pricing rows.
- Account deletion recovery steps say "select Tasks" in the Recycle Bin drop-down (likely doc error).
