## 4. Activities: tasks, calls, events (ZA)

Edition note: Pricing rows: Tasks Standard+, Events Professional+, Calls Professional+; Free and Express have no Activities module. Activity time tracking is Professional+ (manual ticket timer Standard+). Workflow rules on activity modules need Standard+ (Std 5, Pro 15, Ent 30 per module per department); the Field Update trigger, layout rules and validation rules are Enterprise-only. Webhooks: Pro 5, Ent 10. Import batch: Std 10,000, Pro 20,000, Ent 30,000.

### 4.1 Module and creation
- **ZA-01** Three activity types (Task, Call, Event) in one Activities module plus per-type modules; department-specific; recycle bin — UI: Activities module left panel — Ed: Tasks Standard+, Calls/Events Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/working-with-activities-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZA-02** Create from Activities module — Add icon: Add Task / Add Event / Log a Call / schedule call > Submit — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/add-edit-and-delete-tasks)
- **ZA-03** Create from a ticket — Activity tab > Add Activity > Task/Call/Event; ticket pre-mapped — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/working-with-tasks-zoho-desk)
- **ZA-04** Ticket lookup on Add form (calls: "Ticket Subject" auto-fills Contact Name) — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-calls)
- **ZA-05** Activities sub-tab on Tickets, Contacts and Accounts; calls loggable from contact/account — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/marking-an-activity-as-spam)
- **ZA-06** Tasks created by ticket workflow Task action and by macros — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/workflows/articles/workflow-automations)
- **ZA-07** Import tasks and calls from XLS/CSV with ticket mapping — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Import — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/add-edit-and-delete-tasks)
- **ZA-08** Mobile apps create/edit/delete tasks, events, calls (record/schedule only, no dialling) — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/accessing-activities-module-in-android)

### 4.2 Task fields and features
- **ZA-09** Task fields — Department (req), Task Owner (agent or team, default creator), Subject (req, UI 50 / API 300), Due Date, Ticket, Contact, Status, Priority (Highest…Lowest), Category (max 120), reminder, Description (32,000), layout, team, custom fields — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/standard-modules-and-fields-zoho-desk)
- **ZA-10** Task statuses — Not Started, Deferred, In Progress, Waiting on Someone, Canceled, Completed — UI: Task Details inline Status — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/setting-task-status-due-date-and-priority)
- **ZA-11** Inline edit of Status, Due Date (calendar > Set), Priority, Task Owner on Task Details — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/setting-task-status-due-date-and-priority)
- **ZA-12** Assign task owner — agent or team; only admins/authorised agents; owner list limited to department agents — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/assigning-and-managing-task-owner)
- **ZA-13** Task attachments — cloud (WorkDrive, Google Drive, Dropbox, Evernote, OneDrive, Box) or Browse Files — UI: Task Details > Attachments tab — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/working-with-tasks-zoho-desk)
- **ZA-14** Task comments (with mentions) — UI: Task Details; API Tasks comments CRUD — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#TasksComments_Createataskcomment)
- **ZA-15** Task time entries and task timer — add/list/update/delete time entries, summation, timer actions — UI: Task Details > Time Entry — Ed: Professional+ (activity time tracking) — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#TaskTimer_PerformsTaskTimeractions)

### 4.3 Calls
- **ZA-16** Call fields — Department, Call Owner, Subject (req), Direction Inbound/Outbound (default Outbound), Ticket, Call Status, Start Time (default now), Duration (min:sec; live counter), Priority (default High), Contact Name (req), Remind me, Description; API recordingUrl (max 2500) — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-calls)
- **ZA-17** Call types — Current call, Completed (log after), Scheduled; API enum Completed, Scheduled, Missed, Canceled, In Progress — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-calls)
- **ZA-18** Call interface — Call Started only for contacts with phone; PhoneBridge click-to-call icon; results Answered / Call Ended / Unanswered; activity recorded regardless — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-calls)
- **ZA-19** Call Ended links call to current ticket and adds call notes as a ticket conversation — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-calls)
- **ZA-20** Completed call is read-only (no edit) — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-calls)
- **ZA-21** Missed call — unanswered scheduled call prompts reschedule; otherwise status Missed; reschedule creates a missed-call activity and moves the start time — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-calls)
- **ZA-22** Cancel scheduled call — article says "Mark as Completed" though Canceled status exists (conflict) — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-calls)
- **ZA-23** Clear live call mapping (API) — UI: API — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Calls_Clearlivecallmappingfromanactivity)

### 4.4 Events
- **ZA-24** Event fields — Department, Event Owner, Subject (req), Category, Start Time (req), Duration (req), Ticket, Status, Priority, Contact Name (req), Remind me, Description; no location/end time/participants — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-events)
- **ZA-25** Event does not auto-close at end of duration; closes only when Status = Completed — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-events)

### 4.5 Life cycle
- **ZA-27** Only system Status = Completed closes an activity; statusType Open/Closed derived; custom "Completed" field does nothing — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/closing-and-reopening-tasks)

- **ZA-34** Edit — detail More > Edit or header icon > Save; list hover Edit icon — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-events)
- **ZA-35** Delete to Recycle Bin; restore/permanent delete in Setup > Data Administration > Recycle Bin (module picker); restore brings back linked ticket/contact for calls; deleting a call keeps its ticket — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-calls)
- **ZA-36** Spam inherited from linked contact/ticket (no direct mark); Spam views; Activity tab spam filter; API deleteSpam/emptySpam — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/marking-an-activity-as-spam)
- **ZA-37** Recurring activities — not documented (treat as unsupported) (inferred) — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/activities/articles/adding-editing-and-deleting-events)

- **ZA-65** Activities REST APIs — Tasks, Calls, Events CRUD, count, moveToTrash, updateMany, attachments, comments; Recycle Bin restore/delete — UI: API — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Tasks_Createtask)
- **ZA-66** Radar app Activities widget/card — Ed: All (Radar) — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)

### Gaps / thin docs
- Default Status/Priority for new tasks and events; event status list.
- Cancel-call semantics (Completed vs Canceled) and call/event owner at creation conflict.
- Ticket close blocked by open tasks (mobile-only statement).
- Whether task create/complete appears in ticket History.
- Event invitations to other agents: pricing mentions, no KB steps.
- Data-sharing options per activity module not described in KB.
- Recurring activities, calendar view: undocumented.


---

## 5. Knowledge Base (ZK)

Edition note: A private KB for agents and article versioning are in all editions (Free+). "Add resolution as KB" and Zia article generation are Express+. Public KB, KB article templates, the KB dashboard and user groups are Standard+ (groups are not in Free). Limits: 700 sections per category including the root, 699 below the root, 4 levels. Multilingual KB (40+ languages), 301 redirection, Google Analytics and themes are Professional+. Auto-translation of articles is Enterprise only.

### 5.1 Articles: create, edit, publish
- **ZK-01** Add article — Title and content are mandatory; you choose the category, section and sub-section; the article goes straight under the category when there are no sections — UI: Knowledge Base module > Articles home > Add article (+) icon; or the Add icon in the right panel of a section — Ed: All (agent KB); Standard+ (public) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/editor/articles/adding-editing-cloning-unpublishing-and-deleting-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-02** Article owner — whoever creates the article owns it by default; owners get notified of customer feedback; owner can be changed by bulk update or quick edit — UI: article editor / list mass action — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/editor/articles/adding-editing-cloning-unpublishing-and-deleting-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-03** Display permission per article — Agents / All users / Registered users; the choices depend on the category visibility — UI: article editor > Display Permission — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/editor/articles/adding-editing-cloning-unpublishing-and-deleting-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-04** Tags on articles — used for suggested articles in the help center and to bring up solutions while agents reply to tickets; search tags API — UI: article editor > Tags — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/editor/articles/adding-editing-cloning-unpublishing-and-deleting-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-05** Article expiry date and time — once it expires the article leaves the help center and appears in the Expired view; the owner gets emails at 7 days before, 1 day before and on expiry (template under Setup > Templates > Email Templates) — UI: article editor > Article Expiry — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/user-actions/articles/sort-article-by-status-expiry-date-visibility)
- **ZK-06** Per-article SEO — SEO Title, Meta description, Meta keywords, Noindex and Nofollow checkboxes — UI: article editor > SEO section — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/user-actions/articles/optimize-your-knowledge-base-for-search-engines)
- **ZK-07** Publish / Save as Draft / Send for Review — Send for Review shows only when the category has review on; the editor auto-saves drafts while you edit — UI: article editor top bar — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/user-actions/articles/sort-article-by-status-expiry-date-visibility)
- **ZK-08** Edit article — available from the list (hover > Edit icon) or the detail view; while you edit, the changes are kept as a draft until you publish — UI: KB list hover / detail > Edit — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/editor/articles/adding-editing-cloning-unpublishing-and-deleting-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-09** Quick Edit — edits the published version without touching an existing draft. It is shown only when a published article also has a draft (My Articles). An alert warns that another user has a draft (Continue). Changes publish immediately (no draft) and are not recorded in version history. Every property can be changed: title, content, owner, permission, expiry, permalink, category, tags, SEO — UI: Quick Edit icon next to Edit — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/knowledge-base/articles/faqs-quick-edit)
- **ZK-10** Concurrent-edit notification and compare — users with edit rights are notified that someone is editing; a Compare button highlights removed text in red, added in green, modified in blue; the draft author sees a pop-up about a recent quick edit before publishing — UI: in-editor notification — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/editor/articles/adding-editing-cloning-unpublishing-and-deleting-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-11** Discard draft — a draft cannot be deleted directly; the Discard Draft icon (next to Cancel) reverts the article to its earlier version — UI: article editor top-right — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/knowledge-base/articles/faqs-quick-edit)
- **ZK-12** Clone article — "Clone in same category" or "Clone in different category/section" (pick the category and sub-sections). Creates a draft that keeps the title, content, SEO, tags, attachments, permalink and folder structure. Limited to 5 consecutive clones. Agents of associated departments cannot clone — UI: detail > Clone icon; list hover > More > Clone — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/editor/articles/adding-editing-cloning-unpublishing-and-deleting-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-13** Unpublish — Continue confirmation; status becomes Unpublished; the help center shows "page doesn't exist" and search engines stop crawling it; it can be republished with the same or a new permalink. Unpublishing without a draft creates no version; with a draft it creates a minor version and removes the article from Drafts. No bulk unpublish — UI: list hover > More > Unpublish — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/editor/articles/adding-editing-cloning-unpublishing-and-deleting-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-14** Delete article — confirmation dialog; the article moves to the KB Recycle Bin — UI: list hover > More > Delete; detail view — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/editor/articles/adding-editing-cloning-unpublishing-and-deleting-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-15** Hide/Show list — collapses or expands the article list panel in the agent detail view — UI: top panel Hide/Show list — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/editor/articles/adding-editing-cloning-unpublishing-and-deleting-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-16** No storage or article-count limit for the KB — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/knowledge-base/articles/faqs-knowledge-base)
- **ZK-17** Import KB articles — the FAQ lists XLS and CSV; the editor also has an "Import Article" option for documents from cloud or computer (the format list is cut off in the source) — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Import; editor > Import Article — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/knowledge-base/articles/faqs-knowledge-base)
- **ZK-18** Preview article (API) and Validate permalink (API) — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Articles_Previewarticle)

### 5.2 WYSIWYG editor and attachments
- **ZK-19** Rich-text formatting — bold, italic, underline; font and background colour; headings (Normal, H1–H3…); alignment left/right/center/justify; LTR/RTL direction; numbered and bulleted lists; indent; line spacing 1.0/1.2/1.5/2.0; clear formatting; format painter — UI: KB editor toolbar — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/editor/articles/formatting-knowledge-base-articles-using-wysiwyg-editor)

- **ZK-26** Table of contents (TOC) — built from H1–H3 (the title and paragraphs are left out); a TOC tab in the editor's right panel; shown at the top or in the sidebar; turned on or off with "Display table of contents (TOC) for Articles" — UI: Manage KB settings; editor right panel > TOC — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/editor/articles/formatting-knowledge-base-articles-using-wysiwyg-editor)

### 5.3 Permalinks, 301 redirects, article numbers
- **ZK-31** Permalink auto-built from category, sub-category and title. If another article (in any state, including the Recycle Bin) has the same title, the date is appended (e.g. -13-12-2024) and an alert is shown; the date can be removed only after the duplicate is deleted or renamed — UI: editor title > Link icon — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/user-actions/articles/setting-up-301-redirects-for-your-kbase-articles-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZK-32** Edit permalink + "Save and add 301 redirection" — the permalink can be edited any number of times, but an article keeps at most 3 redirections; remove one before adding a 4th. Redirections are listed on the Update Permalink page and can be deleted. Edit permission covers your own articles, Edit All covers all — UI: Edit Article > Link icon in title > Edit — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/user-actions/articles/setting-up-301-redirects-for-your-kbase-articles-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZK-33** Without a redirect, old links return 404 and SEO ranking suffers (documented behaviour) — UI: help center — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/user-actions/articles/setting-up-301-redirects-for-your-kbase-articles-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZK-34** Article numbers — one numeric sequence for the whole organization across all categories. A number is assigned on creation even to drafts; discarded or deleted numbers are never reused; trashed and restored articles keep their number; all translations share it; numbers cannot be edited; templates have none; numeric only, max 9 digits, no prefix. Existing articles are numbered by creation date when the feature is enabled. KB Admin only — UI: Setup > Knowledge Base > Manage KB > Access Settings > Article Number toggle + Next Starting Number > Enable — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/setting-up-and-permission/articles/configuring-article-numbers-for-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-35** Change the article-number start — must be higher than the highest existing number and applies only to new articles — UI: Access Settings > Article Number > Edit > Save — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/setting-up-and-permission/articles/configuring-article-numbers-for-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-36** Article number shown in the list view, detail page, search results, help center, suggested articles, moderation, recycle bin and followed articles; you can search by number in the KB and the help center — UI: these places — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/setting-up-and-permission/articles/configuring-article-numbers-for-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-37** Permalink structure — Default (title) / Number only / Number + title; existing URLs redirect automatically, without a 301 rule — UI: Manage KB > Access Settings > Permalink structure — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/setting-up-and-permission/articles/configuring-article-numbers-for-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-38** Category and section permalinks (API) — create, update and delete root-category permalinks; section permalinks; get the category tree by permalink — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#KBCategory_CreateRootCategoryPermalink)

### 5.4 Categories, sections, department association, visibility
- **ZK-39** Default category per department — on sign-up a category named after the portal is created; each new department adds its own category (it can be hidden from the help center or renamed) — UI: Manage KB > Organize Category — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/editor/articles/adding-editing-cloning-unpublishing-and-deleting-knowledge-base-articles)
- **ZK-40** Create category — display image (library or upload), language, name, description, primary department (required), associated departments, visibility, article view, feedback-ticket agent, auto-create tickets from feedback, review articles plus reviewers, moderation plus moderators — UI: Knowledge Base > Manage KB > Organize Category > Create Category (side panel) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/setting-up-and-permission/articles/organizing-your-knowledge-base-content-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZK-41** Associated (secondary) departments — their agents can only view and download articles, not create or edit; feedback tickets go only to the primary department; review cannot be turned on while an associated department is set — UI: Category panel > Associated Departments — Ed: Professional+ (multi-department, inferred) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/setting-up-and-permission/articles/organizing-your-knowledge-base-content-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZK-42** Category visibility — None (open; the article chooses Agents, Registered or All), Groups (registered members of the groups only), Public (Registered or All; no agent-only articles), Agents (internal only), Custom IP (registered users from the listed IPs, comma-separated). Changing it later resets article permissions to Registered users — UI: Category panel > Visibility — Ed: All (Groups: Standard+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/setting-up-and-permission/articles/organizing-your-knowledge-base-content-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZK-43** Article view mode per category — List (in the configured sort order) or Quick navigation (ordered previous/next reading path) — UI: Category panel > Article View — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/setting-up-and-permission/articles/organizing-your-knowledge-base-content-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZK-44** Category show/hide toggle in the help center — UI: Organize Category > toggle per category — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/knowledge-base/articles/faqs-knowledge-base)
- **ZK-45** Hierarchy and limits — Category > Section > Sub-section > Sub-subsection (3 levels under a category, 4 in total); 700 sections per category including the root (699 below it). Once a section has sub-sections, articles cannot sit directly under it — UI: Organize Category — Ed: Standard+ (public KB row) — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZK-46** Add, edit or delete a section — image, name, description, visibility (chosen only when the category is None, otherwise inherited). The first section created takes over the category's existing articles. Deleting a section with articles prompts you to move or delete them; child sections must be deleted first — UI: Organize Category > category > Add (Section); More > Edit/Delete — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/setting-up-and-permission/articles/organizing-your-knowledge-base-content-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZK-47** Move a section — the section becomes a sub-section of the target, articles included — UI: section hover > More > Move > pick category/section > Move — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/setting-up-and-permission/articles/organizing-your-knowledge-base-content-in-zoho-desk)

### 5.9 Review / approval

Translates the title, content, tags, SEO title, meta description and keywords (not categories). "Publish and translate" from the master article; the result is a translated draft to publish — UI: Help Center > Languages > Translation Type Automatic; editor > Publish and translate — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/guide-to-building-multilingual-help-center)
- **ZK-104** Translation status per language — Published, Draft, Review, Auto-translation in progress, Auto-translation failed (Restart Translation), Outdated, Updated; you cannot request a new translation while one is in progress; stop translation for one article or in bulk — UI: article languages drop-down — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/guide-to-building-multilingual-help-center)
- **ZK-105** Mark translations outdated or up-to-date — prompted on Publish; Mark as up-to-date from the translation or in bulk from the list (manual languages only) — UI: Publish confirmation > Mark as outdated; list > Outdated — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/guide-to-building-multilingual-help-center)
- **ZK-106** Translation API surface — add, update, clone, trash translations; translation versions, diff and restore; permalinks per translation; likes and dislikes per translation — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Articles_Addtranslation)

### Gaps / thin docs
- Recycle bin retention for articles (as opposed to comments and templates) is not stated in the KB articles; 60 days is assumed from the comment and template text.
- The editor's "Import Article" supported formats list is cut off in the local text; the FAQ only says XLS/CSV (bulk import).
- There is no help article for "Add resolution as KB" (pricing row only); the exact UI entry point is inferred.
- PDF download, 301 redirect add/delete and metric-reset step lists are image-only in the source (steps missing).
- The difference table between Manage KB and Admin Access is missing from the local text.
- No article on the KB's own sitemap or SEO beyond per-article fields (help center SEO is covered in ZH).
- Articles' "In Review" status view and translation-status filter appear only in the mobile article, so web availability is inferred.
- Edition for comments, moderation, follow, PDF, versions compare: not in the pricing table (n/d).

## 6. Help Center, Community, ASAP, Guided Conversations and web forms (ZH)

Edition note: The help center itself is in all editions. A custom domain is Express+. Community forums and ASAP are Standard+ (10 widgets on Standard/Pro, 50 on Enterprise). Themes gallery, CSS customization, custom widgets, Google Analytics, multilingual KB and community gamification are Professional+. HTML customization, custom script (JS), multi-brand help centers, the Employee Self-Service portal, Answer Bot in ASAP, Live Chat and SMS OTP sign-in are Enterprise only. Guided Conversations is Enterprise only: 25 flows per department, 500 blocks per flow, 100 global, session and local variables, 1 agent widget and 5 customer widgets per department. Advanced web forms: 1/1/5/10/50 by edition. Feedback widget: 1 per department on Pro/Ent, 1 in total below that.

### 6.1 Help Center setup, domains, branding
- **ZH-01** Help center portal auto-created at sign-up; its URL contains the company name; copy it from Access Settings — UI: Setup > Channels > Help Center > Access Setting — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/help-center/articles/faq-help-center)
- **ZH-02** Help center branding — logo (max 20 KB, best 200:40 px), favicon, logo link-back URL, help center name. With no logo, the Zoho Desk logo appears; emails then use the org logo — UI: Setup > Channels > Help Center > (HC) > Rebrand — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/providing-multi-brand-customer-support)
- **ZH-03** Custom domain mapping — Map Domain; Set as Primary — UI: Help Center > Rebrand > Domain — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZH-04** Primary and secondary domains — up to 3 secondary (old) domains with a server-side 301 to the primary; branded help centers only — UI: Rebrand > Domain > hover > Set as Primary > Save — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/renaming-your-help-center-sub-domain)
- **ZH-05** Switch to the new default domain URL format (security; permanent redirect from old URLs; forced after the deadline); needs Manage Help Center permission — UI: Help Center > Switch Now — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/switching-to-new-help-center-domain-url)
- **ZH-06** Multi-brand help centers — enable Multi-branding (common user base by default), then New Help Center per department (department, logo, favicon, link-back, name, primary URL) — UI: Setup > Organization > Rebranding > Multi-branding > Enable; Setup > Channels > Help Center > New Help Center — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/providing-multi-brand-customer-support)
- **ZH-07** Common vs Unique user base — common: one login across brands; unique: separate sign-up per brand. Unique needs at least 2 branded HCs, can invite existing users, and cannot be rolled back by the admin (support only) — UI: Multi-branding > Manage > Enable Unique User — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/providing-multi-brand-customer-support)
- **ZH-08** Disable multi-brand — with unique users the branded HCs are deleted (option to migrate users to the default HC; migrated users are re-invited); with common users you must delete the branded HCs first — UI: Multi-branding > Manage > Disable — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/providing-multi-brand-customer-support)
- **ZH-09** Help center email templates — Invitation, activation and deactivation templates with a selectable From address — UI: Setup > Channels > Help Center > (HC) > Email Templates — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/help-center/articles/faq-help-center)
- **ZH-10** Help center CRUD API — create, get, update, delete help centers; sitemaps generate, download, delete — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#HelpCenter_CreateHelpCenter)

Drag widgets in, reorder or remove them (Header and Footer cannot be removed); Preview, then Publish — UI: Customize > Widgets > Pages — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/customizing-help-center-home-page)

- **ZH-24** XML sitemap — Generate Sitemap (link opens in a new tab); refreshes every 5 days; domain-mapped HCs only; created automatically for sign-ups after 13 Jun 2019; hidden when "Customers must register" is on. Leaves out agent-only and registered-only articles, drafts, pending-review and expired articles — UI: Help Center > SEO > XML Sitemap — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/creating-a-sitemap-for-the-help-center)

Goal basics, metrics over time, day and hour, source and visitor type, top pages, devices, countries. - **ZH-51** SMS OTP sign-in — Indian numbers only (DLT/TRAI). Set up company info, then sender ID (sender name, PEID, entity, unique ID, status, registered-by), add Zoho as telemarketer, buy credits (1 credit = $1 = 500 SMS; admin emailed when credits run out), upload DLT invitation and verification templates. Needs a single HC or a unique user base; once on, it applies to all HCs and replaces SAML/JWT/federated sign-in. Disabling wipes the DLT data but keeps the credits — UI: Setup > Channels > Help Center > User Authentication > SMS Based Authentication — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/help-center-auth-sms-otp)

- **ZH-65** Community structure — Category > Forum (sub-category) > Topics; one default category per department; uncategorized posts move into the first forum created — UI: Setup > Channels > Community > Categories — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/community/articles/setting-up-community-forums-in-zoho-desk)

Up to 10 on Pro/Ent; categories cannot be deleted, only renamed — UI: Community > Categories > + Create Category — Ed: Professional+ (independent) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/community/articles/setting-up-community-forums-in-zoho-desk)

- **ZH-69** Group-based private community category (Visibility Private + Groups; groups on top-level categories only; followers outside the group are removed) — UI: Community > Categories > Edit > Visibility — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/setting-up-and-permission/articles/adding-and-managing-user-groups)
- **ZH-70** Community preferences — turn topic types on or off (Discussion, Question [default], Announcement, Idea, Problem; only Question, Idea or Problem can be the default); posts of a disabled type can be moved or deleted; landing page shows All Category or Recent Topics — UI: Setup > Channels > Community > Preferences — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/community/articles/setting-up-community-forums-in-zoho-desk)

Validity window max 10 minutes (nbf/iat in seconds, not_before/not_after/exp in ms); new users auto-created; config-error debugging (domain, secret format) — UI: ASAP setup — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/asap/general/articles/debugging-jwt-related-errors-while-configuring-the-asap-add-on)

### Gaps / thin docs
- The SAML, JWT, OIDC and MFA help-center articles were cited by path but not read in depth (the security area covers them); the UI entry point "User Authentication" comes from the SMS article.
- No dedicated "themes gallery" article; the theme list comes from the customization FAQ. Theme-specific widget and page availability is only partly documented.
- No Advanced Web Form API resource found in api_ops.txt (only FeedbackWidget).
- Business Messaging setup, the Answer Bot in ASAP and per-SDK (Android, iOS, Flutter, RN) configuration articles were not itemised one by one (55+ SDK articles exist under developer-space/asap/*).
- GC block-level limits were taken from grep extracts; per-channel support tables (which block works on which channel) were not transcribed in full.
- Community gamification is labelled "Community gamification" (Pro+) in pricing, but the Gamescope article sits under Setup > Organization (it also covers agents); the overlap is not clarified.
- Help center sign-up form fields, password policy and the "forgot password" email OTP are not documented in the read articles.
- Edition for many access-setting toggles, My Area features and community moderation is not in the pricing table (n/d, or the parent row).
