## 7. Channels (ZN)

Edition note: Email channel is in every edition, but the number of support addresses differs: Free 1, Express 2, Standard 5, Professional 10, Enterprise 100. Social (Facebook, X/Twitter, Instagram) starts at Express with 1 brand, and Enterprise gets 2 brands. Instant Messaging (IM) starts at Standard. The per-platform channel limit is Standard 1, Professional 10, Enterprise 50. Round-robin routing, IM workflows, IM Blueprint and IM webhooks need Professional or higher. IM chatbots are Enterprise-only. Telephony starts at Professional, and Multi-Level IVR is Enterprise-only. Live chat powered by SalesIQ is listed as Enterprise-only. The SMS add-on starts at Standard.

### 7.1 Email – support addresses and forwarding
- **ZN-01** Default support mailbox — `support@<portal>.zohodesk.com` is created at signup. It is mapped to the default department and has a friendly name. Its portal or domain part can't be changed later (FAQ says a portal rename needs Zoho support) — UI: Setup > Channels > Email > Support Email Address — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-02** Support email address limit per edition — Free 1, Express 2, Standard 5, Professional 10, Enterprise 100 — UI: Setup > Channels > Email — Ed: All (limits) — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZN-03** Add a support email alias — click New Support Email Address and enter Address (prefix only, e.g. `sales` gives sales@<portal>.zohodesk.com) and Friendly Name (shown as "Zylker Sales <sales@…>"). Click Save — UI: Setup > Channels > Email > Support Email Address > New Support Email Address — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-04** Edit or delete an alias — Delete asks for an OK confirmation. At least one support email address must remain. The FAQ advises removing the external forwarding rule before deleting — UI: Support Email Address list > Edit / Delete — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/channels/articles/faqs-email-channel-set-up)
- **ZN-05** Start or stop mail fetch per address — a toggle on each address with an OK confirmation. While it is OFF, mail to that alias is not turned into tickets. The alias itself is kept, unlike delete — UI: Support Email Address list toggle — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-06** Map an alias to a department — Edit the alias and choose a department from the drop-down. This option only appears when the portal has more than one department — UI: Support Email Address > Edit — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-07** Email-to-ticket conversion — each incoming email to a support address (or forwarded to it) becomes a ticket with a unique ticket ID in the mapped department — UI: Tickets module — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/channels/articles/faqs-email-channel-set-up)
- **ZN-08** Forward from an external mailbox — the external address (e.g. support@company.com) forwards to its matching Zoho Desk alias. The provider's verification email arrives as a ticket in the Open Tickets view — UI: external mail provider + Open Tickets view — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/forwarding-your-support-emails-to-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-09** Provider forwarding guides — Zoho Mail (Settings > Email Forwarding), Exchange/Active Directory (mail-enabled contact or Global Distribution Group), Office 365 (Exchange or Global admin, Manage email forwarding, Outlook redirect rule for several addresses), Gmail (the confirmation code is read from the "Gmail Forwarding Confirmation" ticket), Google Workspace Default Routing, Yahoo (needs Yahoo Mail Plus) — UI: external — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/forwarding-your-support-emails-to-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-10** Google Workspace routing header — add a custom `X-Orig-To` header with the original address and change the envelope recipient to the Desk alias, so agents can see the email was routed. Repeat the rule for each address — UI: Google Admin Console — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/channels/articles/faqs-email-channel-set-up)
- **ZN-11** Permission to configure email — needs the Support Administrator permission. Users without it can't open Setup > Channels > Email — UI: Setup > Channels > Email — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/channels/articles/faqs-email-channel-set-up)

### 7.2 Email – outgoing (From address, SMTP, authentication)
- **ZN-12** Add a From address — click New From Address and fill in Department (only shown when there is more than one), From Address and Friendly Name. An authentication email is sent, and the address can be used only after it is verified. A Resend link is available. The default support address is pre-listed as a From address — UI: Setup > Channels > Email > From Address — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-13** Manage From addresses — Edit, Delete, Mark as default (per department), Active/Inactive, Disable (hidden from the reply From field). The default zohodesk.com address can also be disabled or deleted — UI: From Address list — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-14** Template From field — a new From address must be set in each email template's From field before templates can use it — UI: Setup > Customization > Templates (pointer) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-15** Custom SMTP for a From address — turn on "Use my SMTP" and enter Username, Password (app password if 2FA is on), Server Name, Port (25/465/587) and Connection Type (Plain/SSL/TLS). No verification email or SPF record is needed. Credentials are stored in Desk — UI: New From Address > Use my SMTP — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-16** OAuth mail providers for From addresses — choose Microsoft Outlook, Microsoft Office 365 or Google under "Choose Email Account Provider". This redirects to the provider's sign-in, and the address is then listed with an "authenticated" tag. Desk never asks for the password. To drop stored SMTP credentials, delete the address and re-add it through OAuth — UI: New From Address > Use My SMTP Server — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-17** Re-authenticate a From address — when credentials or server details change, the status shows "failed" and a Re-authenticate button opens the detail view — UI: From Address list — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-18** Remove the "via Zoho Desk" text — sending replies through custom SMTP removes it from replies. Notification emails keep "via/on behalf of Zoho Desk" because of DKIM signing, unless Zoho support moves the account to ZeptoMail — UI: From Address + support request — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/email/articles/faqs-email)
- **ZN-19** Recommended SPF record — `v=spf1 mx include:zoho.com ~all` for From-address domains — UI: external DNS — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-20** DKIM authentication page — lists From-address domains. Verify opens a window with Copy buttons for the selector and TXT values. After you publish them in DNS (24–48 h), click Verify and the status changes to "Verified". Desk also re-checks the DKIM TXT record every two days. Not available for addresses that send through their own SMTP — UI: Setup > Channels > Email > DKIM Authentication — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/configuring-spf-and-dkim-for-your-email-domain)
- **ZN-21** Domain verification troubleshooting checklist — From address verified, values copied for the right domain, DNS published, propagation time allowed, and the address is not on custom SMTP — UI: DKIM Authentication — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/channels/articles/faqs-email-channel-set-up)
- **ZN-22** Mail reply addresses — API resource with Add, Delete, Get, List, Update and SendVerificationMail operations. These appear to be the From addresses (inferred) — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#MailReplyAddress_AddMailReplyAddress)

### 7.3 Email – advanced configuration and delivery
- **ZN-23** Auto Cc replies to the support mailbox — every reply Ccs the original support address. It is an org-level setting that departments can override — UI: Setup > Channels > Email > Advanced Configuration — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-24** Create contacts from the "Reply To" address — creates the contact from Reply-To instead of From. If there are several Reply-To addresses, the first is used. Org-level with department override — UI: Advanced Configuration — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-25** Show agent name in ticket replies and outgoing emails — shows "From: First Last <support alias>" — UI: Advanced Configuration — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-26** Use the original sender as contact for agent-forwarded emails — Desk reads the sender from the forwarded header, or agents can add a `#original_sender someone@example.com` tag in the body. Works only for emails forwarded by active agents. Org-level with department override — UI: Advanced Configuration — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-27** Set threads private automatically by recipient — a reply is marked private when the requester is not a recipient. Forwarded threads are always private. Org-wide. When this is OFF, agents can't make email replies private — UI: Advanced Configuration — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-28** Email threading by message-id — the default. Replies are threaded to the ticket through the message-id header, and a new email without it creates a new ticket — UI: (behaviour) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-29** Add threads using the ticket ID — off by default. The subject must contain `[##444##]` or `[## 444 ##]` (a bare number doesn't work). Choose who may thread: "Active help desk agents excluding light agents" and/or "Ticket contact and others (cc, bcc) who've participated" (spam guard) — UI: Advanced Configuration — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-30** Bounce handling — a hard bounce blocks the address permanently. Soft bounces are allowed up to 5 consecutive times and then become hard. Unblocking needs a request to support@zohodesk.com. A high bounce rate can block notification emails, but not ticket replies — UI: ticket history / conversation — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/setting-up-your-email-channel)
- **ZN-31** Automatic spam detection — on by default and can be toggled — UI: Setup > Customization > General Settings > Contacts > Automatic Spam Detection — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/general-settings/articles/managing-spam-detection-settings)
- **ZN-32** Spam detection options — (a) mark only suspicious tickets as spam; (b) also mark the contacts as spam, so their future tickets go straight to the Spam view; (c) mark tickets but don't mark contacts that have other valid tickets — UI: same toggle — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/general-settings/articles/managing-spam-detection-settings)
- **ZN-33** Email delivery failure alerts — list and clear the alerts — UI: API (in-app alert UI not documented) — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Notification_Listemaildeliveryfailurealerts)
- **ZN-34** Email headers for troubleshooting — support may ask for incoming or outgoing headers to look into delivery failures (header viewing is in the ticket area) — UI: ticket > original email headers — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/troubleshooting-tips/articles/getting-email-headers-to-troubleshoot-email-failures)
- **ZN-35** Auto-acknowledgement (pointer) — notification rules send "Acknowledging a ticket reply" to the requester and to CC'd users. Details are in the Notifications area — UI: Setup > Customization > Notifications > Notification Rules — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/notifications/articles/managing-notification-rules-triggers-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-36** Department email templates and cloning — templates are created per department. The Broadcast Email Template extension copies templates across departments or sets a default — UI: Setup > Marketplace > Broadcast Email Template — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/email/articles/faqs-email)
- **ZN-37** Support email address API — Add, Delete, Get, List, Update — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#SupportEmailAddress_AddSupportEmailAddress)
- **ZN-38** Support email sub-domain API — Get and Update the zohodesk.com sub-domain — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#SupportEmailDomain_UpdateSupportEmailAddressSubDomain)
- **ZN-39** Email threads API — SendEmailReply, DraftEmailReply, UpdateDraft, GetOriginalMailContent, Listconversations — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Threads_SendEmailReply)

### 7.4 Web forms and feedback widget (cross-reference)
- **ZN-40** Advanced web forms and feedback widget — web forms: Free 1, Express 1, Standard 5, Professional 10, Enterprise 50. Feedback widget: 1 in Free/Express/Standard, 1 per department in Professional and Enterprise. Details are in the Help Center area (FeedbackWidget API: Create/Get/Update/Delete) — UI: Setup > Channels — Ed: All (limits) — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)

### 7.5 Social (Facebook, X/Twitter, Instagram) and app-review channels
- **ZN-41** Brand model — a brand groups 1 Facebook page, 1 Instagram account and 1 X/Twitter handle. A portal can have several brands. Brands are created in Desk or imported from Zoho Social — UI: Setup > Channels > Social — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-42** Brand limits — Express, Standard and Professional get 1 brand, Enterprise gets 2 (pricing row per platform; more "on request"). Extra brands cost $5 per brand per month or $50 per brand per year. Free: none — UI: Setup > Channels > Social — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZN-43** Social permission — only users with the Administrator profile can link social accounts — UI: Setup > Channels > Social — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/channels/articles/faqs-social-module)
- **ZN-44** Create a brand — Get Started > Create, then Brand Logo, Name, Department (one department or all departments) and Description — UI: Setup > Channels > Social — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-45** Edit or delete a brand — hover to see the Edit Brand and Delete Brand icons. Deleting removes a native brand permanently, while an imported brand is only delinked and stays in Zoho Social — UI: Social brand list — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-46** Link a Facebook page — "+" next to Facebook, log in, pick a page the account administers, Link Page. A "?" tooltip explains why a page can't be linked — UI: brand panel — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-47** Link an X/Twitter handle — "+" next to Twitter, Authorize app, Done — UI: brand panel — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-48** Link an Instagram account — "+" next to Instagram, log in through Facebook, "+" on the account, Add Account — UI: brand panel — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-49** Disconnect one social account — hover over it, Delete, Disconnect. The brand is kept — UI: brand panel — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/channels/articles/faqs-social-module)
- **ZN-50** Import brands from Zoho Social — Get Started > Import the first time, Import Brand later. Tick the brands and click Import — UI: Setup > Channels > Social — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-51** Facebook feed — shows page posts and visitor posts. Actions: convert to ticket, like/dislike, comment, reply to comments, delete post, New Post. The post panel has a Profile tab and can add the user as a contact — UI: Social module > Facebook Feed — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-52** X/Twitter feed — shows tweets and @mentions. Actions: reply, retweet, like/unlike, convert a mention, delete, Post Tweet, follow/unfollow the user. Includes search of public tweets with Twitter's advanced operators — UI: Social module > Twitter Feed — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-53** Instagram feed — shows posts (stories and DMs through View Feed). Actions: convert to ticket, comment, reply to comments. The post panel shows comments, linked tickets and the profile — UI: Social module > Instagram Feed — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-54** Feed filters — All, Converted or Not converted, and bulk actions (delete tickets, mark as spam) — UI: feed > Filter icon — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-55** Private and direct messages — Facebook private messages and X/Instagram DMs sync in real time. Facebook and X show an Interactions history, profile, add-contact and follow options — UI: Social module > message tabs — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/channels/articles/faqs-social-module)
- **ZN-56** Manual conversion — the "Convert into Ticket" icon is replaced by the ticket number, with a pop-up to assign an agent or team and open Ticket Peek. For messages, the latest 15 messages are captured, and at least one must be incoming — UI: post/message window — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-57** Follow-up behaviour — later comments on a converted post are added as threads to the same ticket. New private/direct messages are NOT appended to a manually converted ticket — UI: (behaviour) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-58** Auto-conversion settings per handle — Facebook: page posts, visitor posts, messages. X: @mentions, messages. Instagram: posts, DMs. Choose All, or only items with specific keywords (each keyword set can go to a different department; All allows only one department). An auto-converted message brings in 1 message — UI: Setup > Channels > Social > handle > Settings icon — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-59** Append interval for DMs — the period during which a user's later messages are added to the same ticket (e.g. 3 days). Only for the "All messages" option — UI: Auto-Conversion Settings — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-60** Related Tickets tab — lists all tickets linked to a post or message, for Facebook, X and Instagram — UI: post/message window > Tickets tab — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/social/articles/setting-up-the-social-module)
- **ZN-61** Social reply API — SendFacebookReply, DraftFacebookReply, SendTwitterReply — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Threads_SendFacebookReply)
- **ZN-62** YouTube extension — enter a channel ID and pick up to 10 videos per department. Each video gets one ticket with every comment as a conversation, and the video description becomes the ticket description. Imports 100 comments every 4 minutes. One YouTube channel at a time. Agents reply from Desk, but replies are not nested and mentions don't notify — UI: Setup > Integrations > Marketplace > YouTube for Zoho Desk — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/channels/articles/youtube-for-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-63** Google Play Reviews extension — needs an OAuth client ID/secret from Google Cloud (redirect https://flow.zoho.com/oAuth/callBack) and the app's package name. Choose a department and "App as Ticket" or "Reviews as Tickets". Agents can reply from Desk — UI: Marketplace > Google Play Reviews — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/channels/articles/google-play-reviews-for-zoho-desk-extension)
- **ZN-64** App Store Reviews extension — search for the app and choose a department, plus App as Ticket or Reviews as Tickets. View only: agents can't reply from Desk and use the "via App Store Reviews" link instead — UI: Marketplace > App Store Reviews — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/channels/articles/app-store-reviews-for-zoho-desk-extension)

### 7.6 Live chat (Zoho SalesIQ) and Chat module
- **ZN-65** Live chat powered by SalesIQ — the pricing row lists it as Enterprise-only, while the "Zoho SalesIQ" integration row says Standard+. Needs the Support Channels admin permission — UI: Setup > Channels > Chat — Ed: Enterprise (per pricing) — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZN-66** Enable chat — Enable Chat creates a SalesIQ portal named after the Desk portal, or lets existing SalesIQ customers pick their portal. It can also be enabled per department under Setup > Departments > [dept] > Channels > Chat — UI: Setup > Channels > Chat — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/chat/articles/setting-up-live-chat-using-zoho-salesiq-integration)
- **ZN-67** Embed and Help Center chat — choose the embed (Name), copy the snippet for websites, and "Include live chat in Help Center" (default on, no script needed). One embed applies to all unbranded departments. Appearance is customised in SalesIQ (Advanced Settings) — UI: Chat Settings — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/chat/articles/setting-up-live-chat-using-zoho-salesiq-integration)
- **ZN-68** Chat departments and agents — public departments only. Each needs at least one chat agent. "All Public Departments" and "All Agents" are shortcuts, and an agent can serve several departments — UI: Chat Settings — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/chat/articles/setting-up-live-chat-using-zoho-salesiq-integration)
- **ZN-69** Included SalesIQ features — operators equal to chat agents, unlimited chats, 25 departments, translation, unlimited history, unlimited embeds, mobile SDK, IP blocking, concurrent chat limit, chat from email signature. Not included: screen sharing, remote access, visitor tracking and routing — UI: SalesIQ — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/chat/articles/setting-up-live-chat-using-zoho-salesiq-integration)
- **ZN-70** Disable chat — the toggle removes the Help Center widget, and the SalesIQ subscription drops to the Free plan — UI: Chat Settings toggle — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/chat/articles/setting-up-live-chat-using-zoho-salesiq-integration)
- **ZN-71** Chat module home — My Chat tab (Active/Missed/Closed) and Chats tab (Active, Missed, Reopened, Closed, Monitored) — UI: Chat module — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/chat/articles/working-with-chat-module-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-72** Chat window and side panel — header shows chat number, brand, department and agent, with several sessions in tabs. The right panel has Info (browser, location, OS, landing page), Recent Chats, Notes, Activity and Zoho Desk (linked tickets) — UI: Chat module — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/chat/articles/working-with-chat-module-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-73** Chat-to-ticket — manual from the right panel, or automatic transcript conversion configured in SalesIQ — UI: Chat module right panel — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/chat/articles/working-with-chat-module-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-74** Agent chat status — Online/Offline and Mute Sounds, from the account photo. Shown only to agents with chat access — UI: top-right profile menu — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/chat/articles/working-with-chat-module-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-75** Chat canned replies — type "/" to list them, Enter to insert, Esc to dismiss. New replies take a department, category and tags. Existing ones can only be edited in SalesIQ — UI: chat window > canned replies icon — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/chat/articles/working-with-chat-module-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-76** Transfer and invite — Transfer chat picks an operator and department with an optional note. The transfer completes only when accepted, and the customer is not notified. Invite Operators adds a colleague to the chat — UI: chat window > More Actions — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/chat/articles/working-with-chat-module-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-77** Other chat actions — Block visitor IP (synced to SalesIQ, doesn't block the Desk contact), Save as PDF, Print, Copy chat URL, Close (immediately or after a delay) — UI: chat window > More Actions — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/chat/articles/working-with-chat-module-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-78** Agent channel availability API — get the current agent's mail, phone and chat preference, and update overall availability and online preference — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#AgentChannelPreference_Getmailphoneandchatpreferenceofcurrentagent)

### 7.7 Instant Messaging – platform, setup, billing and limits
- **ZN-79** IM module and platforms — WhatsApp, Telegram, LINE, WeChat, WeCom, Facebook Messenger, Instagram, Business Messaging. Pay-as-you-go. Supported plans: Standard, Professional, Enterprise, CRM Plus, Zoho One — UI: IM module; Setup > Channels > Instant Messaging — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/introduction-to-instant-messaging)
- **ZN-80** Channels per platform — Business Messaging, WhatsApp, Telegram, WeChat, LINE, Messenger and Instagram each allow Standard 1, Professional 10, Enterprise 50 channels. WhatsApp also has usage-based charges — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > [platform] > Create Channel — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZN-81** Channels capped by departments — "The number of channels you create cannot exceed the departments in your account". Channel icon is at most 1 MB (.jpg/.png/.jpeg) — UI: Create Channel form — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/facebook-messenger/articles/integrating-facebook-messenger-with-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-82** IM permission and module visibility — needs the IM permission in the profile, and IM must be in the selected modules (Customization > Modules), otherwise users see "Permission Denied" — UI: Setup > Profiles; Customization > Modules — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/instant-messaging/articles/faqs-instant-messaging)
- **ZN-83** Associate agents with channels — Setup > Channels > IM > platform > channel > Associate Agents, or Preferences > Agents > Associate. Only agents associated with a channel show in the assign list, and associations must be redone when departments change — UI: IM Preferences > Agents — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/setting-up-agents-for-zoho-desk-s-im-user-gudie)
- **ZN-84** IM credits — $2 per credit plus a 3% fee. Buy Credits in IM setup, or as a non-recurring add-on on the Subscription page. The Available Credits balance is visible to all users. Credits are per portal (they survive channel deletion) and can't be transferred between Zoho products. A zero balance blocks chargeable messages. Credits can't be bought while the subscription is scheduled for cancellation — UI: Setup > Channels > Instant Messaging > Buy Credits — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/managing-payments-for-im-through-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-85** Auto-recharge credits — set the credits to add and the minimum-balance trigger. Needs a saved payment source and is not supported for partner-billed portals (partners buy through the partner portal) — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > WhatsApp > Auto-Recharge — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/purchasing-im-credits-partner-s-user-guide)
- **ZN-86** WhatsApp charging model — per delivered template message since 1 Jul 2025 (Marketing, Utility, Authentication). Customer Service Window is 24 h. Free Entry Point window is 72 h after a Click-to-WhatsApp ad or Facebook CTA. From 1 Oct 2026, service replies are free only for the first 1,000 per number per month and utility templates in the window are charged. Price depends on the recipient's country code — UI: (billing) — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/whatsapp-business-platform-comprehensive-pricing-user-education-guide-2024-2025)
- **ZN-87** Incoming attachment limit — up to 50 MB for any media type on all platforms — UI: IM Inbox — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/im-inbox-message-composer-to-manage-customer-conversations)
- **ZN-88** Outgoing attachment limits per platform — WhatsApp: image 5 MB, document 50 MB (FAQ says 20 MB), video and audio 16 MB. Telegram: image 10 MB, PDF 20 MB, video and audio 20 MB. LINE: image 10 MB, video and audio 20 MB, no documents. WeChat: image and video 10 MB, audio 2 MB. Messenger: image 8 MB, PDF, video and audio 20 MB. Instagram: image 8 MB, video and audio 20 MB — UI: composer — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/im-inbox-message-composer-to-manage-customer-conversations)
- **ZN-89** Session windows per platform — WhatsApp 24 h from the customer's last message (templates only after that). Messenger and Instagram 24 h, with message tags or a 7-day exception. WeChat 48 h. Telegram has no limit. LINE depends on the bot. Business Messaging depends on the session type — UI: composer — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/im-inbox-message-composer-to-manage-customer-conversations)
- **ZN-90** IM count limits — up to 300 canned messages and 200 template messages per business — UI: Setup > Channels > IM — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/instant-messaging/articles/faqs-instant-messaging)
- **ZN-91** Data-centre availability — the FAQ has a per-channel DC matrix (US/IN/EU/AU/CN/JP/CA). WeChat setup works only in some DCs, and WeCom only on Mainland China infrastructure — UI: (availability) — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/instant-messaging/articles/faqs-instant-messaging)
- **ZN-92** Fall back to the default status — a customer reply in IM resets the ticket or conversation to the default status (usually Open). Set up in Ticket Status customization — UI: Setup > Customization > Ticket Status — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/instant-messaging/articles/faqs-what-is-fall-back-to-default-in-im-and-how-does-it-work)
- **ZN-93** IM notification preferences — agents turn incoming-IM notifications on or off under Profile > My Availability. Real-time notifications are a Standard+ pricing row — UI: profile > My Availability — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/instant-messaging/articles/faqs-instant-messaging)

### 7.8 Instant Messaging – IM Inbox
- **ZN-94** IM Inbox layout — conversation list on the left, chat window in the centre, customer info on the right, composer at the bottom. Covers all connected platforms — UI: IM module > Inbox — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/getting-started-with-im-inbox)
- **ZN-95** Left-panel tabs — My Conversations (sub-filters Responded / Yet to Respond / All), Unassigned, Blocked, Ended, All Conversations (same sub-filters), Bot Conversations (filter by bot) — UI: IM Inbox left panel — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/left-hand-side-lhs-panel-in-im-inbox)
- **ZN-96** Conversation row, filters and search — each row shows name, channel icon, unread count, last-message preview, timestamp, bot, assignee and department. Filter by channel and department. Search by customer name, text, conversation ID, agent, bot or contact info. The panel remembers if it was collapsed — UI: IM Inbox left panel — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/left-hand-side-lhs-panel-in-im-inbox)
- **ZN-97** Message status ticks — Sent ✓, Delivered ✓✓, Read (blue ✓✓), Failed (¡). WhatsApp shows all of them. Messenger and Instagram show read status depending on privacy settings. Telegram and WeChat don't show read status — UI: chat window — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/center-panel-chat-window-to-manage-customer-conversations)
- **ZN-98** System messages — assignment, accepted, reopened, ended, "Ticket Id #… created from chat", auto-close, user blocked/unblocked, bot handover. Shown in muted grey and can't be edited. Sent messages can't be edited or deleted, and there is no typing indicator — UI: chat window — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/center-panel-chat-window-to-manage-customer-conversations)
- **ZN-99** Composer actions and resulting status — Pickup sets Yet to Respond, Send sets Responded, Send & Close sets Closed, End sets Closed, Reopen sets Open, Block/Unblock. Only the assigned agent can close. Replying to an unassigned chat takes ownership — UI: composer action bar — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/im-inbox-message-composer-to-manage-customer-conversations)
- **ZN-100** Assign or reassign a conversation — to an agent or a bot, from the agent/bot name or "+" in the conversation — UI: chat window top-right — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/setting-up-agents-for-zoho-desk-s-im-user-gudie)
- **ZN-101** Message-level actions — reply to a specific message (WhatsApp, Messenger), create a ticket from a message bubble, jump to the linked ticket ID. Chats created only from emoji reactions can't be picked up — UI: chat window message hover — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/center-panel-chat-window-to-manage-customer-conversations)
- **ZN-102** Manual ticket from IM — the Create Ticket icon opens a form with Department, Contact Name (suggestions), Account, Email, Phone, Subject and Description. Attachments up to 20 MB each. The customer gets an acknowledgement email, and the creator becomes the owner — UI: IM module message hover > Create Ticket — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/how-to-manually-create-a-ticket-in-the-instant-messaging-module)
- **ZN-103** Move IM tickets between departments — single or bulk. Needs the Move Tickets permission. The ticket becomes a new ticket in the target department with status Open, and the old department's automations and SLA no longer apply — UI: ticket Classic/Compact view department, Ticket Peek > More > Move — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/how-to-manually-create-a-ticket-in-the-instant-messaging-module)
- **ZN-104** Right-hand info panel — Contact Info (name, country, email, phone, owner), Conversation Details (channel, department, conversation ID, session created, last seen), Tickets (auto-created only), Attachments, Zia Insights. Panels can be reordered by dragging (saved per agent) and collapsed, with at least one panel kept visible — UI: IM Inbox right panel — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/right-hand-side-rhs-info-panel-in-im-inbox)
- **ZN-105** Composer tools — emoji picker, drag-and-drop attachments, "#" for canned messages, Knowledge Base article search and share — UI: composer — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/im-inbox-message-composer-to-manage-customer-conversations)
- **ZN-106** Voice notes — record up to 3 minutes (stops automatically), sent as compressed .mp3. Not available on Instagram. Can be deleted before sending, not after. Shows microphone permission and error messages — UI: composer mic — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/voicenote-in-im)
- **ZN-107** IM canned messages — admin only. Setup > Channels > Instant Messaging > Canned Message: Name, Tags, Content (up to 1600 chars, emojis, "$" placeholders). Department-scoped. Enable/disable toggle, edit, delete (permanent). No attachments. Placeholders need auto-ticket conversion turned on — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > Canned Message; composer "#" — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/canned-messages-for-instant-messaging-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-108** Channel disabled or deleted — agents can't reply and no new messages arrive. Old chats stay read-only and searchable, and agents can't transfer or block in them. An expired token needs the admin to reconnect, and hitting platform limits shows "Channel temporarily unavailable" — UI: IM Inbox — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/im-inbox/articles/getting-started-with-im-inbox)

### 7.9 Instant Messaging – preferences and automation
- **ZN-109** Chat-to-ticket timer — set per department and channel under Preferences > Tickets. "None" means no automatic ticket. A duration starts a new ticket when the customer replies to a closed conversation after that time. "Always" reopens and appends to the same ticket. Only portal owners and admins can change it. Anonymous Business Messaging visitors are not converted — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > Preferences > Tickets — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/preferences/articles/configuring-the-timer-for-chat-to-ticket-conversion)
- **ZN-110** Ticket conversion — turns WhatsApp and other IM conversations into tickets (Standard+ pricing row) — UI: IM Preferences — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZN-111** Auto-close conversations — per-channel agent timer (e.g. 5 min, 1 h, 24 h, None) and bot timer (5 min, 10 min, None). Applies to Waiting for Customer or Pending. A customer reply reopens the chat and notifies agents. The timer pauses outside business hours or when agents are offline, and doesn't run while the agent hasn't replied — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > Preferences > Conversation Rules > Auto-close Conversations — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/preferences/articles/auto-close-conversations-bot-agent-26-3-2025)
- **ZN-112** IM business hours per channel — pick a Desk business-hours schedule for each channel, or None for 24x7. Deleting a schedule switches channels to 24x7. SLA starts only after a ticket is created and follows the department SLA's hours — UI: Preferences > Conversation Rules > Business Hours — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/preferences/articles/set-up-business-hours-for-instant-messaging-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-113** Welcome message — set per department, up to 1600 chars, with "$" placeholders (ticket placeholders when the session has a ticket). Sent when an agent picks up a customer-started chat or a reopened chat. Not sent when agents are offline or for agent-initiated chats. One language only — UI: Preferences > Automation > Enable Welcome Message — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/preferences/articles/configure-welcome-messages-in-instant-messaging-inbox)
- **ZN-114** Offline message — set per department, up to 1600 chars, with placeholders. Sent outside business hours or when no agent is Online (including a failed bot handover). Not sent when business hours aren't configured. The chat stays open in Unassigned — UI: Preferences > Automation > Enable Offline Message — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/preferences/articles/configure-offline-messages-in-instant-messaging-channels)
- **ZN-115** Auto-responses — welcome and offline messages can be turned on or off (Standard+ pricing row) — UI: Preferences > Automation — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/preferences/articles/how-to-enable-auto-response-messages-for-your-business)
- **ZN-116** Chat routing (round robin) — sequential cyclic assignment only (no load-based or skill-based in IM), toggled per channel. Assigns only to agents Online for IM. Queues chats outside business hours. Downgrading makes assignment manual — UI: Preferences > Automation > Chat Routing — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/preferences/articles/chat-routing-in-instant-messaging)
- **ZN-117** Chat transfer — reassign conversations to the right agent. Agent-to-agent transfer is outside the routing rules — UI: chat window > Assign — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZN-118** Block users — detect abusive language and block or unblock a user. Blocked chats go to the Blocked tab — UI: chat window > Block User — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZN-119** IM workflow actions — "Notify via Instant Messaging" (template message, not tied to a ticket) and "Reply via Instant Messaging" (appended to the ticket). Pick the IM account and template. Telegram, LINE and WeChat can only reply to an existing ticket — UI: Setup > Automation > Workflows > Actions — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/im-workflows-custom-actions-gallery)
- **ZN-120** Blueprint in IM — flowcharts with automated transitions for IM tickets — UI: Blueprints (pointer) — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZN-121** Embeddable chat options — per channel: chat link, QR code, or message button snippet (button size, colour, text, corner radius, live preview). WeChat offers a QR code only. There is no single multi-channel IM widget — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > [channel] > embed — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/gudie-to-setup-an-embeddable-whatsapp-message-button)
- **ZN-122** IM dashboard — monitoring (new and existing contacts), summary counts (incoming and outgoing messages), supervising (attended, unassigned, total), metrics drill-down by channel, session metrics (average pickup, response and first response time), agent metrics table. Time filters run from Last 24 hours to Custom, and channel filters include All Channels — UI: IM > Dashboard — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/getting-started-with-the-im-dashboard)
- **ZN-123** Zoho Analytics sync for IM — IM Conversations and IM Messages tables (direction, status, bot type, channel) with incremental sync. Portal-level only, one workspace per portal, admin only. Business Messaging contacts are not synced, contact-less chats are skipped, and history is synced only at setup — UI: Setup > Marketplace > Zoho Analytics — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/analyze-instant-messaging-data-in-zoho-analytics)
- **ZN-124** IM webhooks — IM_Message_Add (incoming and outgoing messages, direction IN/OUT), IM_Message_Status (old and new status, e.g. SENT to DELIVERED), IM_Session_Status (sessionStatus such as ON_HOLD or ENDED, replyStatus, old status) — UI: Setup > Developer Space > Webhooks (inferred) — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/webhooks/articles/im-session-status-webhook)
- **ZN-125** IM happiness rating — CSAT on IM channels (detail in ZY) — UI: Setup > Customer Happiness — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/measuring-customer-happiness-in-instant-messaging)

### 7.10 Instant Messaging – bots and Zia
- **ZN-126** Chatbots in IM — associate a Guided Conversations or Answer Bot with a channel. Audience defaults to All Visitors and availability is Always. Give each bot its own audience. Supported on WhatsApp, Instagram, Telegram, Business Messaging, LINE and Messenger — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > Preferences > Bots > Let's Get Started — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/blended-conversations/articles/how-to-add-a-chat-bot-to-your-whatsapp-channel)
- **ZN-127** Guided Conversation block support per channel — e.g. WhatsApp: text up to 4096 chars, images jpg/png, video mp4/3gpp, PDF files, up to 10 buttons of 20 chars each, single-select only. Separate matrices exist for Messenger, Instagram, LINE, Telegram and Business Messaging — UI: GC builder — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/blended-conversations/articles/supported-gc-blocks-in-the-whatsapp-channel)
- **ZN-128** Bot handover and bot timers — the bot hands over to an agent, falls back to the offline message if nobody is online, and restarts its flow when a customer returns after auto-close. Bots run 24x7 unless the flow has time conditions — UI: (behaviour) — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/preferences/articles/set-up-business-hours-for-instant-messaging-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-129** Zia insights in IM — sentiment and tone shown as emoji, conversation summary (Last 1 Hour, Last 24 Hours, Today…), suggested replies from the Knowledge Base, one-click insert. Needs Zia enabled under Admin > Zia > Permissions. Enterprise per the right-panel FAQ — UI: IM Inbox right panel > Zia Insights — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/zia-ai-in-im/articles/enhancing-communication-with-zia-in-your-im-inbox)
- **ZN-130** Message suggestions — hover a customer message, then Preview or Add to Composer — UI: chat window message hover — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/zia-ai-in-im/articles/message-suggestions-in-im)
- **ZN-131** Zia composer rewrite — full or partial text, tones Formal, Informal, Diplomatic, Assertive or Humorous, lengths Short or Long — UI: composer > Writing Assistance — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/zia-ai-in-im/articles/zia-powered-composer-in-inbox)
- **ZN-132** Translate outgoing messages — 15 languages, chosen by hand, for typed or canned text. Review before sending — UI: composer > Translate — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/zia-ai-in-im/articles/translating-outgoing-messages-in-composer)
- **ZN-133** Zia reply assistance — ask Zia follow-up questions and get suggested replies with sources. Uses ChatGPT or Gemini if configured — UI: right panel > Reply Assistance — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/zia-ai-in-im/articles/zia-reply-assistance-in-zoho-desk-im)

### 7.11 Instant Messaging – WhatsApp
- **ZN-134** WhatsApp prerequisites — Facebook Business Manager, a WhatsApp Business Account (WABA) and a phone number that is owned, has a country code, can take voice/SMS, isn't a short code, and isn't on the WhatsApp app or another provider. Embedded sign-up. OTP verification is valid 14 days. Wait 1 h after creating a new Business Manager — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > WhatsApp > Configure — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/instant-messaging/articles/faqs-whatsapp-business)
- **ZN-135** Business profile — category, description, icon (up to 1 MB). A display-name change needs Meta approval, and re-registration currently needs Zoho support — UI: WhatsApp channel setup — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/integrating-whatsapp-with-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-136** WhatsApp sandbox (unverified trial) — business-initiated messages to 2 numbers and up to 10 customer-initiated conversations before the business is verified — UI: WhatsApp channel — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/integrating-whatsapp-with-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-137** Messaging tiers and number counts — Tier 1 1k, Tier 2 10k, Tier 3 100k, Tier 4 unlimited business-initiated conversations per 24 h. 2 numbers before verification, 25 after. The green-tick application takes 1–7 days — UI: Meta Business Manager — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/applying-for-a-whatsapp-official-business-account)
- **ZN-138** Multiple numbers per WABA — Add Number and Change number on the channel (needs an approved name and certificate). The UI lists why a number can't be chosen. After a change, customers see a new contact and the chat link must be regenerated — UI: WhatsApp > Channel Info > Change number — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/multi-number-support-by-whatsapp-business-platform)
- **ZN-139** Multiple WABAs — Change WABA on a channel. Templates are scoped to the selected WABA — UI: WhatsApp > Channel Info > Change WABA — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/whatsapp-multi-waba-support-by-instant-messaging)
- **ZN-140** Move a number from another provider (BSP migration) — both WABAs must be in the same Facebook Business. Messages are not migrated. Name, quality rating, limits and official-account status are kept. Old templates can't be used and must be recreated — UI: Embedded Signup > Add a new number — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/bsp-migration)
- **ZN-141** WhatsApp Co-Existence — connect a WhatsApp Business App number. Syncs up to 6 months of history (up to 24 h), with no replies during the sync. Only new messages create tickets. The number can't be edited, and the channel can't be deleted during the sync — UI: WhatsApp > Co-Existence — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/whatsapp-co-existence-use-whatsapp-business-app-with-instant-messaging)
- **ZN-142** Shared WhatsApp number across Zoho bundle services — one channel is shared and the first service becomes the parent. Pick-up locking works per service. Bundle users only — UI: WhatsApp setup — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/manage-whatsapp-conversations-using-a-shared-phone-number)
- **ZN-143** WhatsApp Cloud API — migrated from on-premise. The same number can't be on both. Template create/read/update/delete goes through the Cloud API — UI: (platform) — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/zoho-desk-s-im-migration-to-the-whatsapp-cloud-api)
- **ZN-144** Message templates — Templates sub-tab > Create Template. Needs Meta approval, and Pending/Failed templates can't be sent. Header up to 60 chars with 1 placeholder. Body (required) up to 1024 chars with up to 10 placeholders, not at the start or end. Footer up to 60 chars with no placeholders. Buttons: up to 10 Quick Reply, Call to Action (static or dynamic URL, call button). Categories: Utility and Marketing. Add Sample. Opt-in required — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > WhatsApp > Templates — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/whatsapp-message-templates-user-guide)
- **ZN-145** Interactive templates — CTA buttons are limited to 2 (one call, one URL). The call number must differ from the API number. Quick replies are preset options — UI: Templates — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/send-interactive-message-templates)
- **ZN-146** Dynamic URL in templates — "$" placeholder (e.g. `$Contacts.Email`) only at the end of the URL. Don't put sensitive data in it — UI: Templates > Buttons > Call to action — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/dynamic-url-setup-for-whatsapp-templates-in-im)
- **ZN-147** Multilingual templates (setup) — a default language plus translations. The customer's preferred language is picked automatically, with the default as fallback. Deleting the default language deletes all translations — UI: Templates — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/configuring-multilingual-whatsapp-templates-in-im)
- **ZN-148** Multilingual templates (inbox) — the preferred language is preselected and agents can change it. Messages include "Template is not available in customer's preferred language. Sending in the default language." and "Selected template is pending WhatsApp approval." — UI: IM Inbox composer — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/using-multilingual-templates-in-im-inbox)
- **ZN-149** Delete a WhatsApp channel — after deletion nothing can be sent or received and old chats are read-only. Zoho is a WhatsApp Business Solution Provider — UI: channel ellipsis > Delete Channel — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/whatsapp/articles/integrating-whatsapp-with-zoho-desk)

### 7.12 Instant Messaging – other platforms
- **ZN-150** Facebook Messenger — link Meta business pages as an admin. 1600-char message limit, 7-day reply window. Messages sent in the native Messenger app don't sync. An expired OAuth token needs re-authentication. API: StartaFacebookMessengerSession — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > Facebook Messenger — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/facebook-messenger/articles/integrating-facebook-messenger-with-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-151** Instagram DM — linked through a Facebook admin login (Linked Accounts). 1600-char limit, 7-day reply window. GC Quick Reply buttons show only in the mobile app. No voice notes — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > Instagram — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/instagram/articles/integrating-instagram-with-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-152** Telegram — needs a bot token from @BotFather. Every message creates a contact (without a phone number). No session limit. Deactivate the channel with a toggle and confirm — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > Telegram > Create Channel — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/telegram/articles/integrating-telegram-with-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-153** LINE — needs the Messaging API Channel ID and Channel Secret. In LINE response settings: chat off, webhook on, greeting and auto-response off. Users add the account as a friend (QR or URL). LINE plans: free 500, light 15,000, standard 45,000 messages/month — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > LINE — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/instant-messaging/articles/faqs-line)
- **ZN-154** WeChat — needs Original ID, App ID, App Secret, Token and EncodingAESKey, plus region IP whitelisting. Limited to some DCs. Agents can't start conversations. 48-hour window, then 4 pushes per month. Embed by QR code only — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > WeChat — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/wechat/articles/integrating-wechat-with-zoho-desk)
- **ZN-155** WeCom (WeChat Enterprise) — Mainland China only. A WeCom owner or admin authorises Zoho IM as a third-party app and links a Customer Service Account. Desk then gives a chat link, QR code and button — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > WeCom — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/wecom/articles/wecom-integration-in-instant-messaging)
- **ZN-156** Business Messaging web widget — Desk's own live chat. Customise mode, colour, wallpaper (5 presets), size and launcher icon (6 styles). Icon up to 1 MB. Embed as a chat bubble. Business Messaging in ASAP is Standard+ — UI: Setup > Channels > IM > Business Messaging — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/business-messaging/articles/adding-a-business-messaging-widget-to-your-website)
- **ZN-157** Business Messaging JavaScript API — `window.ZOHOIM` properties (widgetContainerId, widgetLayout.showWidget, needWidgetBubble, needCloseIcon…). Widget script from im.zoho.com. Custom actions, events and contact info — UI: website code — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/business-messaging/articles/integrating-custom-actions-in-the-business-messaging-widget)
- **ZN-158** Business Messaging session variables — pass user details (name, email, phone) when the widget first renders or later — UI: website code / SDK — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/business-messaging/articles/session-variables-in-business-messaging)
- **ZN-159** Business Messaging mobile SDK — Android, iOS and Flutter SDKs: integrate, customise the widget UI, set contact info and session variables (brief) — UI: mobile app code — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/business-messaging/android-sdk/articles/how-to-integrate-the-business-messaging-sdk-with-your-android-app)
- **ZN-160** ASAP mobile SDK (pointer) — ASAP add-ons for Android, iOS, React Native and Flutter bring the help widget, tickets, KB, community and chat into apps. Covered in the Help Center area — UI: mobile app code — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/asap/general/articles/working-with-the-asap-add-ons-for-mobile-platforms)
- **ZN-161** WhatsApp number rules (FAQ) — a number used in Desk can't also be used in the WhatsApp Business mobile app (except through Co-Existence, ZN-141). It can't be moved between Zoho products. A number already linked to another device doesn't appear in the channel drop-down. Template length is 1024 chars — UI: WhatsApp channel setup — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/instant-messaging/articles/faqs-whatsapp-business)

### 7.13 IM and Channels API
- **ZN-162** Channels list — Listconfiguredchannels returns the channels configured in the portal — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Channels_Listconfiguredchannels)

### 7.14 Telephony – capabilities (pricing rows)

### Gaps / thin docs
- DMARC is not covered anywhere in the local help text. Only SPF (one recommended record) and DKIM are documented.
- No documented size limit for email attachments (inbound or outbound). Only the 20 MB per-file limit for tickets created manually is found.
- The UI for email delivery-failure alerts is not described; only the Notification API operations exist. The MailConfigurations API resource lists no operations. MailReplyAddress has no help article (its mapping to From Address is inferred).
- Arattai, Viber and Apple Messages are not documented as IM platforms (Viber and Apple Messages show up only in the Business Messaging vs SalesIQ comparison text).
- Live chat edition conflict: the pricing row "Live chat (powered by SalesIQ)" says Enterprise-only, while the "Zoho SalesIQ" integration row says Standard+.
- WhatsApp outgoing document size conflict: 50 MB in the composer article vs 20 MB in the IM FAQ.
- The Telephony setup path and provider-neutral settings (greeting, fallback number, spam call handling, call summary) are known only from pricing rows. Only the Twilio, RingCentral, Amazon Connect, babelforce and PhoneBridge articles give details.
- Edition gating for the YouTube, Google Play, App Store, 3CX and Vonage extensions is not stated.
- The IM webhook setup UI path is not in the webhook articles (marked inferred). The Blueprint-in-IM detail is only in pricing.
- The GC block matrices for channels other than WhatsApp were not itemised individually (one article per channel exists under blended-conversations).

## 8. Automation (ZU)

Edition note: Notification rules are on every edition; macros run from Free (2) to Ent (30/dept); workflow rules start at Express (1/module, tickets only), Std 5/module, Pro 15/dept/module, Ent 30/dept/module. Supervise rules Std 5 / Pro 15/dept / Ent 30/dept. Direct assignment Express 2 / Std 5 / Pro 15 / Ent 30; round robin (load 10/dept Pro, 15/dept Ent; sequential, agent threshold) is Pro+; skills and skill-based routing are Enterprise (30 active skills/dept). Blueprints are Pro (1/dept, 20 transitions) and Ent (20/dept, 100 transitions); strict mode, blueprint custom functions and the blueprint dashboard are Ent only. SLAs: Free/Express default, Std 4, Pro 10/dept, Ent 20/dept; customer-based SLA and field watching Pro+; contracts, support plans and SLA contract management Ent only. Business hours / holiday lists: Express-Pro 1, Ent 100. Custom functions (Deluge), schedules (10/dept, 250/account), Zia Actions and the Deluge assistant bot are Ent only. Webhooks Pro 5 active, Ent 10 active (20 configured max). Custom actions gallery Pro+. Automation Studio is Early Access on request for Pro and Ent.

Sources reused (not re-researched): docs/desk-assignment-rules, docs/desk-workflow-rules and docs/desk-blueprint `reference/zoho-docs-inventory.md` in the repo. Their items are condensed below with their original URLs.

### 8.1 Automation order, counts and cross-cutting rules
- **ZU-01** Order of execution (current doc) — Direct Assignment > Workflows > SLAs > Round Robin > Blueprints > Notification rules > Automation Studio (last, async; may override earlier changes; order among several Studio rules not guaranteed) — UI: n/a (engine) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/automation-studio/articles/automation-studio-an-overview)
- **ZU-02** Order of execution (SLA article variant) — Direct assignment > workflow > SLA > Round robin (sequential > load balancing > skill based) > Blueprint > Notifications — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/escalate-sla/articles/understanding-and-working-with-slas)
- **ZU-03** Order of execution (older FAQ variant) — Skills-based assignment > Direct assignment > Workflow > SLA > Round robin > Blueprint > Supervise (periodic) > Macros (manual); conflicts with ZU-01/02 — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/automation/articles/faqs-automation)
- **ZU-04** Practical order effects — round robin does not reassign a ticket already given an owner by workflow, direct rule or macro; workflow field updates can change which SLA applies; blueprint is evaluated after workflow changes — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/automation/articles/faqs-workflow-rules)
- **ZU-05** Automation feature count — counts per feature (businesshours, holidaylist, Skills, SkillType, workflows, blueprints, automationAlerts, fieldUpdates, automationTasks, customFunctions) return maxTotalCount / maxActiveCount / usedTotalCount / usedActiveCount; module + departmentId mandatory for workflows; 50 credits — UI: API — Ed: All (limits per edition) — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#AutomationFeatureCount_GetAutomationFeatureCount)
- **ZU-06** Tags as automation criteria — is / isn't; up to 20 comma-separated tags per condition; each 3–50 chars; no special characters; spaces collapsed; case-insensitive; duplicates removed; usable in assignment, workflow, blueprint, SLA and supervise; FAQ says automation cannot add tags (conflicts with gallery Add Tags) — UI: rule criteria — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/automation/articles/faqs-automation)
- **ZU-07** Profile permission "Help Desk Automation" — gates workflows, macros, assignment rules, supervise, schedules, webhooks; API attribute `automation`; some articles say Support Administrator instead — UI: Setup > User Management > Profiles — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/workflows/articles/workflow-automations)
- **ZU-08** Teams across automations — Team usable as criterion (with AND/OR) in workflows, direct and round-robin rules, supervise, macros; SLA targets per team and escalation to tiered teams; prebuilt team notifications are not editable (use a workflow) — UI: rule criteria — Ed: Pro+ (team ownership) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/actions/articles/using-teams-in-automation)
- **ZU-09** Ticket history evidence of automation — History tab / API `eventFilter` values WorkflowHistory, SuperviseHistory, MacroHistory, AssignmentRuleHistory, NotificationRuleHistory, SLAHistory, BlueprintHistory, ApprovalHistory; actor types DirectAssignment, RoundRobinAssignment, Workflow, Macro, Supervise, Blueprint, SLA, NotificationRule — UI: ticket detail > History — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/rest-apis/articles/get-ticket-history-api-explained-in-detail)
- **ZU-10** Audit log of automation config — entities Direct Assignment Rule, Round Robin Rule, Skills, Workflow, Workflow Alerts, Field Updates, Tasks, Custom Functions, Escalate (SLA), Supervisor Rules, Schedules, Notification Rules, ActionMapping; add/edit/delete with old/new values; 2-year retention — UI: Setup > Privacy and Security > Audit Log — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/audit-log/articles/monitoring-audit-log-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZU-11** Sandbox deploy of automation — assignment rules, notify, workflows, skills, blueprint, macros, supervise, schedules can be deployed from sandbox to production; workflow notifications in sandbox go to primary recipient only — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Sandbox — Ed: Ent (3 sandboxes) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/sandbox/articles/working-with-sandbox-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZU-12** Downgrade / expiry behaviour — workflow rules disabled on downgrade or expiry; must be re-enabled manually — UI: Setup > Automation > Workflows — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/workflows/articles/workflow-automations)
- **ZU-13** Agent deactivation cascade — replacement agent takes over round-robin and direct rule assignees, owner field updates in workflows/macros/supervise, notify fields, SLA escalation fields and workflow task assignees; API schedules reassignment — UI: Setup > Agents > deactivate; API — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZU-14** On Hold exclusion — On Hold tickets are excluded from Is overdue / Is Response Overdue criteria in automations — UI: rule criteria — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-status/articles/on-hold-state-use-cases-and-behavior)
- **ZU-15** Execute action API — run an automation action on records (`entityIds`, `moduleId`, SYNC/ASYNC, `actionParamMappings`); returns skippedFieldUpdates — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#GenericActions_ExecuteAction)

### 8.3 Assignment rules: round robin and routing preferences
- **ZU-23** Round-robin rule — per department; name, description, execute on Create / Update / both; up to 5 targets; criteria up to 25; Assign To agents or teams; Exclude agents checkbox; no limit on agents — UI: Setup > Automation > Assignment Rules > Round-robin > New Round Robin Assignment — Ed: Pro 10/dept, Ent 15/dept — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/assignment-rules-notification/articles/assigning-tickets-using-workflows-assignment-rule)

- **ZU-25** Sequential method — order agents were added (alphabetical when all agents or a team chosen); identical names by user ID; editing the agent list resets sequence; offline / at-threshold agents marked SKIPPED and moved to end — UI: target > method — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/assignment-rules-notification/articles/assigning-tickets-using-workflows-assignment-rule)
- **ZU-26** Round-robin rule and target order — chronological default; hover > Reorder icon > drag > Save order; per-rule Deactivate/Activate icons with Inactive list; global Round Robin Rules toggle — UI: Round-robin list — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/assignment-rules-notification/articles/assigning-tickets-using-workflows-assignment-rule)
- **ZU-27** Round-robin API — Create/Get/Update rule and target, criteria fields, reorder rules, reorder targets, execution status (the earlier inventory missed these; they are in the API op list) — UI: API — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#RoundRobin_CreateRoundRobinRule)
- **ZU-28** Online requirement — only agents online on the email channel get tickets unless Assign tickets to offline agents is on (API `assignToOfflineAgents`, default off) — UI: Round Robin > More > Round Robin Preferences — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#RoutingPreference_UpdateRoutingPreferences)
- **ZU-29** Department threshold — toggle + max open tickets per agent; FAQ 1–200 vs API 1–500 (conflict) — UI: Round Robin Preferences — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/automation/articles/faqs-assignment-rules)
- **ZU-30** Agent-level threshold — rows of agents + count, overrides department value, up to 1500 agents — UI: Round Robin Preferences — Ed: Pro+ (Early Access) — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
