- **ZU-205** Action block — alerts, update record, assign ticket, send email reply, add comment, custom function, IM, Generate Content (once per rule); runs in configured order — UI: + > Action — Ed: Pro/Ent EA — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/automation-studio/articles/building-blocks-using-automation-studio)
- **ZU-206** Action chaining + dot walking — action outputs prefixed with block id feed later actions; related-module fields usable in conditions and actions — UI: action config — Ed: Pro/Ent EA — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/automation/articles/faqs-automation-studio)
- **ZU-207** If block — true path only (no else); must be followed by an action; not after another If; time comparators "age in" and "due in" — UI: + > If — Ed: Pro/Ent EA — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/automation-studio/articles/building-blocks-using-automation-studio)
- **ZU-208** Fork block — up to 5 parallel branches; no fork directly under fork — UI: + > Fork — Ed: Pro/Ent EA — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/automation-studio/articles/building-blocks-using-automation-studio)
- **ZU-209** Delay block — fixed duration or date/time with before/on/after offset; 10 min – 120 days; not last in path; one per path; re-update restarts timer; department move skips pending delays >10 min; deleting block/rule deletes jobs; recycle bin pauses, restore resumes — UI: + > Delay — Ed: Pro/Ent EA — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/automation-studio/articles/building-blocks-using-automation-studio)
- **ZU-210** Block cap — 25 blocks per rule (building-blocks article) vs 50 (FAQ) — conflict — UI: n/a — Ed: Pro/Ent EA — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/automation/articles/faqs-automation-studio)

### 8.21 Automation in Instant Messaging
- **ZU-211** IM chat routing — per-channel toggle; sequential to agents online for IM and linked to channel; queue outside business hours; direct transfer not routed — UI: Setup > Channels > Instant Messaging > Preferences > Automation > Chat Routing — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/preferences/articles/chat-routing-in-instant-messaging)
- **ZU-212** IM welcome / offline auto-response — toggles with message, placeholders ($CustomerName), preview — UI: IM > Preferences > Automation — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/preferences/articles/how-to-enable-auto-response-messages-for-your-business)
- **ZU-213** IM auto-close conversations — per channel timer for agent chats (e.g. 5 min–24 h or None) and bot chats (5 min, 10 min, None); paused outside business hours / agents offline; reopen on customer reply — UI: IM > Preferences > Conversation Rules > Auto-close Conversations — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/preferences/articles/auto-close-conversations-bot-agent-26-3-2025)
- **ZU-214** IM business hours — business hours for IM channels (used by routing and SLA) — UI: IM > Preferences — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/preferences/articles/set-up-business-hours-for-instant-messaging-in-zoho-desk)

### 8.22 Zia-assisted assignment (cross-ref)
- **ZU-215** Zia field predictions incl. ticket owner — predicted value auto-updated at accuracy score so assignment/workflow rules route; max 20 per department — UI: Setup > Zia > Field Predictions — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/prediction/articles/zia-field-predictions)
- **ZU-216** Zia agents as assignees — Support Specialist / Resolution Expert selectable in assignment rules and workflows; Specialist un-assigns itself when no KB match — UI: assignment targets — Ed: Enterprise (prebuilt agents) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/agentic-ai/articles/agentic-ai-in-desk-support-specialist-and-resolution-expert)

### Gaps / thin docs
- Execution order: four different official sequences (ZU-01..03 plus the workflow FAQ order). Where skill stamping, Supervise, Macros and Automation Studio sit is unclear.
- Field Update trigger / field watching: the workflow article says Enterprise only, but pricing lists "Field watching" as Pro+.
- Round-robin threshold range: FAQ says 1–200, API says 1–500. Agent-level threshold is marked Early Access.
- Round-robin API ops exist in the API op list (ZU-27). The earlier assignment inventory said they were missing; the field-level payload is not confirmed.
- Automation Studio block cap: 25 per rule in the article, 50 in the FAQ. The full action list per module and any per-edition rule limits are not published.
- Schedules: whether the 10/department cap counts active schedules only, and the meaning of the "Custom" repeat options, are not documented.
- Workflow rules: clone, reorder, list-page columns/search, firing on import/API/bulk updates, and per-day alert quotas are not documented.
- Custom functions: the article lists custom functions in workflows as Ent only. The Functions UI path also differs by article (Developer Space > Functions vs Automation > Workflows > Custom Functions). Connections edition is n/d.
- Blueprint state SLA: the maximum escalation levels and the before/after semantics are known from API JSON only. The checklist operation is mentioned only in a community post.
- Webhooks: the retry policy is not documented. The "Update specific fields" cap is 5 in the help article and 20 in the API doc.
- Support plans: the KB states that customers see their credit balance, but no portal location is documented.
- Contracts: the help says Manage Help Desk Automation, while plans say Manage Automation. The permission naming is inconsistent.
- Notification rules: the FAQ says Department "Creating a new ticket" notifies the assignee, which conflicts with the rule description (it notifies all agents about unassigned tickets).
- Macros: there is no documented clone or reorder, and no documented way for an agent to search the macro list.
- Edition data comes from pricing_rows.txt. Where it conflicts with an article, the conflict is noted in the item.

## 9. Customer happiness ratings and surveys (ZY)

Edition note: The pricing page lists Customer Happiness Ratings and the Customer happiness dashboard from Express up. The setup article says "Standard Edition and above", so the two sources disagree. The Free edition has neither. IM happiness ratings need Standard or higher, the same as IM itself. Survey tools (Zoho Survey, Qualtrics, SurveyGizmo) are Marketplace extensions, and their editions are not documented.

### 9.1 Rating setup
- **ZY-01** Customer Happiness feature and edition — ratings are requested on ticket closure or after every reply, and the results are tracked. Pricing says Express+ and the article says Standard+ — UI: Setup > Organization > Customer Happiness — Ed: Express+ (pricing) — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZY-02** Permissions — the admin profile permission "Customer Happiness Ratings" manages the feature. "Agent Overview" lets a user see other users' Happiness Rating on their profile page — UI: Setup > Profiles — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZY-03** Enable ratings — Get Started opens the Rating Configuration page. In IM, Get Started turns it on for all departments by default — UI: Setup > Organization > Customer Happiness > Get Started — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-04** Rating question — free text, e.g. "How would you rate our customer service?" — UI: Rating Configuration — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-05** Rating scale — three options: Good, Okay, Bad. Okay can be disabled to remove the neutral choice — UI: Rating Configuration > Ratings — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-06** When the rating link is sent — "To every response sent" (reply level) or "When a ticket is closed" (closure level, sent in the Ticket Closed notification) — UI: Rating Configuration > Add Rating Link — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-07** Rating link style — "rating question alone" or "rating question with smileys" — UI: Rating Configuration > Rating Link — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-08** Who can use the rating link — Anyone on the internet (default), Requesters and cc'd users (email only, not in the Help Center), or End users (requester, CCs and the requester's organisation; others are asked to sign up) — UI: Rating Configuration > Rating link accessible to — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-09** Help Center rating widget — shows the overall % of Good, Okay and Bad plus the last 100 ratings — UI: Rating Configuration > Display Widget in the Help Center — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-10** Agent photo on the rating form — on or off — UI: Rating Configuration > Show Agent Photo — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-11** Reopen ticket on rating — Don't reopen, For all ratings, or For bad ratings only — UI: Rating Configuration > Reopen Ticket — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-12** Departments where ratings are on — choose one or more departments — UI: Rating Configuration > Enable Survey — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-13** Rating notifications — tell the ticket owner about bad ratings only or all ratings. Supervisors or managers can also be notified of negative ratings — UI: Rating Configuration > Notify Ticket Owner For / other users — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-14** Rating rules — customers can't opt out. Ratings are per response or per ticket, not per customer. The link works only once, so a rating can't be changed later — UI: (behaviour) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-15** Customise rating options and the thank-you text — per option you can change the smiley (or upload an image), the label, the message shown after rating and the message shown after feedback — UI: Customer Happiness > Edit icon on a rating — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-16** Multilingual rating form — 27 languages besides English. Language order: ticket language, then browser language, then English. Translate by exporting "Displayed for customers – Happiness Rating", editing the key=value pairs and importing — UI: Setup > Customization > Languages — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-17** Rating placeholder and where it goes — `${Cases.Customer Happiness Rating}` renders the question plus Good/Okay/Bad links. Reply-level: added below the agent signature and can be moved in the editor. Closure-level: above the signature in the "Closing a Ticket" notification, which can be edited — UI: reply editor; Setup > Notifications — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-18** Feedback page — the customer picks a rating and adds a comment. The page shows the agent photo and business logo — UI: public rating page — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-19** Rating for phone tickets — workflow on Tickets/Create with Status contains Closed and Channel contains Phone, sending an email alert with the happiness template to the Contact — UI: Setup > Automation > Workflows — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-20** CC'd users can rate — after each reply or on closure, depending on the setting — UI: (behaviour) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/ticket-replies/articles/enabling-and-adding-cc-users-on-tickets)
- **ZY-21** Turn ratings off — a toggle at the top of the Customer Happiness page — UI: Setup > Organization > Customer Happiness — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)

### 9.2 Viewing scores, reports and dashboard
- **ZY-22** Ratings on the ticket — each rating and its comment is added as a thread on the ticket — UI: ticket detail conversation — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZY-23** Requester score on a ticket — overall score under the contact info, with a breakdown for this department and for all departments — UI: ticket detail > contact score — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/viewing-happiness-rating-score)

The detail page has a Happiness Rating tab with positive/neutral/negative counts and feedback (ticket ID, subject, agent) in date order — UI: Customers > Contact > Happiness Rating tab — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/viewing-happiness-rating-score)
- **ZY-25** Account happiness — same as contacts, combining all contacts of the account — UI: Customers > Account > Happiness Rating tab — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/viewing-happiness-rating-score)
- **ZY-26** Customer happiness dashboard — Happiness rate, ratings by agent, ratings over time — UI: Analytics > Dashboards > Customer Happiness — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/standard-dashboards-in-desk)
- **ZY-27** Survey dashboard component — top ten customers who gave positive or negative ratings in a period — UI: dashboard component — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/dashboard-components)
- **ZY-28** Happiness in the agent performance report — counts of Good, OK and Bad ratings on public agent responses and tickets — UI: Reports > Agent Performance — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/reports/articles/understanding-the-agent-performance-report)
- **ZY-29** Predefined customer happiness reports — UI: Reports > Predefined — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/reports/articles/using-predefined-reports-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZY-30** Custom views by rating — e.g. Status is Closed AND Happiness Rating is Bad. The view can be shared — UI: Tickets > Custom View criteria — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/creating-custom-ticket-list-views)
- **ZY-31** Rating fields in email templates — merge fields "Customer Happiness Rating" and "Bad Rating", plus a notification when a ticket gets a rating — UI: Setup > Customization > Templates — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/templates/articles/creating-and-managing-email-templates)

### 9.3 Automation on ratings
- **ZY-32** Workflow trigger "Happiness Ratings" — the rule runs when a rating is received, and you pick which rating value. Tickets module only — UI: Setup > Automation > Workflows > Execute On — Ed: n/d (workflows) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/workflows/articles/workflow-automations)
- **ZY-33** Follow-up on a bad rating — workflow on Happiness Rating = Bad with Status is Closed sends an email alert to the Contact. It runs immediately and can't be undone. Other actions (assign, field update, webhook, custom function) can be added — UI: Setup > Automation > Workflows — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/workflows/articles/configuring-automation-rules-for-happiness-ratings)
- **ZY-34** Delayed rating request (supervise rule) — Calendar Hours. Criteria: Hours since closed is 24 AND Happiness Rating "Link not provided" AND Status is Closed. Sends an alert with a template containing the rating placeholder — UI: Setup > Automation > Supervise — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/workflows/articles/configuring-automation-rules-for-happiness-ratings)
- **ZY-35** Rules around reopening — if the ticket reopens before the supervise rule fires, the rule doesn't run. The "Link not provided" condition prevents duplicate rating emails — UI: (behaviour) — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/automation/articles/faqs-configuring-automation-rules-for-happiness-ratings)

### 9.4 Happiness in chat and IM
- **ZY-36** SalesIQ chat ratings — 1–5 stars map to Bad (1–2), Okay (3) and Good (4–5). Turning off Desk happiness doesn't turn off SalesIQ visitor feedback. Departments with chat show rating options at the end of the chat by default — UI: SalesIQ chat window — Ed: Enterprise (live chat) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/chat/articles/setting-up-live-chat-using-zoho-salesiq-integration)
- **ZY-37** IM happiness (CSAT) — asks "How was your support experience?" with Good, Okay (can be turned off) or Bad. Sent when an agent closes the conversation or it auto-closes. One request per session. The rating can't be changed. The question can be customised — UI: Setup > Customer Happiness — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/measuring-customer-happiness-in-instant-messaging)
- **ZY-38** IM feedback recognition and late replies — counted as feedback only when the customer taps a button or types exactly Good, Okay or Bad (any case). The feedback window is 30 minutes. A late reply creates a new ticket and notifies agents. Any other text is not counted. No agent notification for feedback-only replies — UI: (behaviour) — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/measuring-customer-happiness-in-instant-messaging)
- **ZY-39** IM rating button limits per channel — WhatsApp: 3 buttons, 20 chars each, question up to 1024. Telegram: 3, 40 chars, 1024. Messenger and Instagram: 3, 20 chars, 640. LINE: 3, 13 chars, 60 — UI: (channel constraint) — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/measuring-customer-happiness-in-instant-messaging)
- **ZY-40** IM limitation: reopen setting ignored — submitting a rating reopens a closed IM ticket and moves it to Unassigned, even when Reopen Ticket is "Don't reopen". Ratings are sent in the customer's preferred language and fall back to the primary language — UI: (limitation) — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/measuring-customer-happiness-in-instant-messaging)
- **ZY-41** IM customer happiness pricing row — "Collect customer feedback … on your messaging apps through ratings" — UI: IM — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)

### 9.5 API, surveys and related
- **ZY-42** CustomerFeedback API — Listallcustomerfeedback, Getthecustomerfeedbackcontent, Getcustomerfeedbackcount, Getthecustomerfeedbackplaceholderlink — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#CustomerFeedback_Listallcustomerfeedback)

A survey link can go into email templates. Responses show on the ticket, with department-wise reports — UI: Setup > Marketplace > Zoho Survey > Preference — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/general/articles/zoho-survey-extension-for-zoho-desk)

Covered in the Knowledge Base area — UI: Knowledge Base — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#ArticleFeedback_CreateArticleFeedback)

### Gaps / thin docs
- Edition conflict: the pricing page says Express+ and the setup article says Standard+.
- No setting is documented for the rating scale beyond the three fixed options (no 5-star or NPS scale in native Desk ratings).
- The thank-you page is only partly documented: per-option "response shown on selecting a rating and on providing feedback". There is no redirect-URL option.
- The workflow FAQ talks about a customer "updating" a rating, but the setup article says the link works only once. That conflict is unresolved.
- No native Zoho Survey "integration" beyond the Marketplace extension. NPS/CES survey types are not documented.
- The API field-level schema for CustomerFeedback (rating values, filters) was not checked; only operation names are known.
- The happiness dashboard is listed only as three components. Filters and drill-downs are not documented in the local text.

## 10. Reports and dashboards (ZD)

Edition note: Agent dashboard is in all editions (incl. Free). Predefined reports, Overview, Ticket status and Customer happiness dashboards are Express+. Custom reports (Std 50, Pro/Ent unlimited), custom dashboards (Std 10, Pro/Ent unlimited), CSV/XLS/PDF export, Headquarters, Response/Resolution/FCR, KB and Community dashboards are Standard+. Lifecycle, Agent availability, Agent performance, Calls/Telephony, SLA dashboards and global (all-department) reports/dashboards are Professional+. Scheduled reports (max 100), Blueprint dashboard and Zia dashboard are Enterprise only. Zoho Analytics integration is Standard+ (not on Free).

Common sources: pricing = https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html (ANALYTICS section).

### 10.1 Analytics module, access and permissions
- **ZD-01** Analytics module (top bar) with left panel Overview / Dashboards / Reports; Dashboards opens on the Overview dashboard by default — UI: top bar > Analytics — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/overview-dashboard-desk)
- **ZD-02** Profile module permission "Reports and Dashboards" with levels View / Update / Create / Delete; enables Analytics access — UI: Setup > User Management > Profiles > Module Permissions — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/reports-and-dashboards/articles/faqs-lifecycle-report)
- **ZD-03** Creating custom reports needs admin, or Reports and Dashboards permission with at least View + Create — UI: Profiles — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/reports/articles/creating-custom-reports-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZD-04** Separate "Global Reports and Dashboard" profile permission gates all-department reports — UI: Profiles — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/reports/articles/creating-global-cross-departmental-reports)
- **ZD-05** "Administrator Dashboard" profile permission: needed to see other agents' data in Agent Performance report and to open the Headquarters dashboard — UI: Profiles — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/reports/articles/understanding-the-agent-performance-report)
- **ZD-06** Standard time-frame picker used across static reports and standard dashboards: Last 24 hours (default), Today, Yesterday, Last 7 days, Last 30 days, Current week (Mon start), Last week (Mon–Sun), Current month, Last month, Custom (start/end) — UI: report/dashboard header, upper-right — Ed: All with reports — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/reports-and-dashboards/articles/faqs-lifecycle-report)

### 10.2 Predefined reports
- **ZD-07** Predefined reports per module (Tickets, Articles, Accounts/Contacts, Products, Activities, Customer happiness, Static) — ready-made, no build needed; needs Reports and Dashboards permission — UI: Analytics > Reports — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/reports/articles/using-predefined-reports-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZD-08** Ticket predefined reports (16): Frequently Reopened, Most Threaded, Not Responded Open, On Hold, Open-Overdue, Tickets By Account / Agents / Channels / Contact / FCR / Priority / SLA / SLA Violated / Status / Time Entry, Unresolved Tickets — UI: Analytics > Reports > Tickets folder — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/reports/articles/using-predefined-reports-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZD-09** Article predefined reports: Frequently Suggested, Most Disliked, Most Liked, Popular Articles — UI: Analytics > Reports — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/reports/articles/using-predefined-reports-in-zoho-desk)

### 10.10 Reporting formulas and time definitions
- **ZD-57** Formulas: ticket assignment time, Average Handling Time, System response / First system response, System resolution, Agent resolution, Agent response / First agent response, Team resolution, Response/Resolution violation, Response/Resolution SLA success time; all exclude time in Closed status; agent metrics also exclude On Hold — UI: n/a (calculation) — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/reports/articles/reporting-formulas)
- **ZD-58** Glossary: first response time, business/non-business/24x7/calendar/custom hours, holiday list (skipped by SLA, blueprint and time-based rules), Response SLA = SLA execution time + allowed time, Resolution SLA, ticket overdue, due date, modified time, assigned time — UI: n/a — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/response-and-resolution-times/articles/response-and-resolution-times-glossary)
- **ZD-59** Response-time rules: SLA tickets count business hours only; multiple customer emails / consecutive agent replies; assigned or created outside business hours → response counted from next business minute (1 min); reply to unassigned ticket = immediate (1 min); merge with different owners resets assignment time; system response includes On Hold, agent response excludes On Hold; auto-acknowledgements not counted — UI: n/a — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/response-and-resolution-times/articles/response-time-cases-example-and-important-notes)
- **ZD-60** Resolution-time rules: closure outside business hours counted at next business-hour start; reassignment when agent unavailable; merge resets assigned time; overall resolution includes On Hold, agent resolution excludes it — UI: n/a — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/response-and-resolution-times/articles/resolution-time-cases-example-and-important-notes)

### 10.11 Standard dashboards
- **ZD-61** Standard dashboard folders: Popular, Public, Telephony, Time Based, Overview, Customer Happiness, Zia, Global; cannot be deleted/edited or have components added; viewable by all Desk users — UI: Analytics > Dashboards — Ed: per dashboard — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/standard-dashboards-in-desk)
- **ZD-62** Overview dashboard (default on opening Dashboards): KPI tiles Open, On-Hold, Overdue, Due Today, Unassigned Due in 1 hour, Unassigned; widgets Ticket Stats (New/On Hold/Closed/Backlog, clickable legends), Traffic Analysis by channel (donut/table), Average Handling Time (FRT, response, resolution; table toggle), Happiness rate; one duration filter for all widgets (not KPI tiles, no per-widget filter) — UI: Analytics > Dashboards > Popular > Overview — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/overview-dashboard-desk)
- **ZD-63** Ticket Status dashboard: Open tickets by status, Tickets by state, Reopened tickets, Avg handling time by status, Avg time taken by agents; filters teams, agents, duration; auto-refresh every 15 min in full-screen — UI: Dashboards > Popular > Ticket Status — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/ticket-status-dashboard)
- **ZD-64** Customer Happiness dashboard: customer feedback, happiness rate, ratings by agent, ratings over time — UI: Dashboards > Customer Happiness — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/standard-dashboards-in-desk)
- **ZD-65** Public dashboards: Accounts by industry; Requests by priority / by status; Products by category — UI: Dashboards > Public — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/standard-dashboards-in-desk)
- **ZD-66** Time-based dashboards: First response time (overview, FCR, channel-wise, time frame, agent-wise; with telephony only calls linked to tickets count), Response time (overview, number of responses, channel-wise, time frame, agent-wise), Resolution time (overview, closed trend, time frame, channel-wise, agent-wise) — UI: Dashboards > Time Based — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/standard-dashboards-in-desk)
- **ZD-67** SLA dashboard: tiles SLA violated tickets, SLA violations (instances), avg residual time, avg violation time; 7 components (violated tickets by hour, adherence vs violated tickets, adherence vs violated instances, violations by SLA and agent, by time, by status, by channel); filters SLA name, violation type (first response/response/resolution), teams, agents, duration — UI: Dashboards > Popular > SLA — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/sla-dashboard)
- **ZD-68** Blueprint dashboard (department-specific): active blueprint tickets, SLA violated tickets, all transitions; components active vs completed records, SLA violated states, violated vs adherence, avg time per blueprint, avg time per state, transition occurrence (per agent via All agents dropdown); filter blueprint name + duration — UI: Dashboards > Popular > Blueprint — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/blueprints-dashboard)
- **ZD-69** Telephony / Calls dashboard (when telephony enabled): call metrics (inbound answered/missed, outbound answered/unanswered), inbound answered vs missed, outbound answered vs unanswered, inbound vs outbound, agent call stats (attended/initiated/missed, agent search), call metrics by hour; filters agent + duration — UI: Dashboards > Telephony — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/telephony-dashboard)
- **ZD-70** Telephony agent availability tracking (phone availability) — UI: n/d — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZD-71** Headquarters (HQ) dashboard: 8 real-time components — incoming/outgoing bandwidth (default 24h, channel filter), live traffic (last 1 hour), unassigned (on hold / open / due in 1h / overdue), online agents and offline agents per channel (sorted by most overdue), most threaded tickets (top 25, 30 days), customer happiness (25 days), negative ratings (25 days); needs Administrator Dashboard permission — UI: Tickets > Headquarters — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/headquarters-dashboard)
- **ZD-72** Knowledge Base dashboard: summary (views, likes, dislikes, feedback, usage), keyword search stats (popular / failed), article views by user type and channel (help center, ASAP web, ASAP SDK), likes/dislikes, most referenced articles, trending articles, article effectiveness; durations 24h/7d/30d/365d/custom; needs KB View permission — UI: Knowledge Base > dashboard — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/performance-monitoring/articles/knowledge-base-dashboard)
- **ZD-73** Community dashboard (customer interaction/behaviour in community) — UI: Community module (inferred) — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZD-74** Zia dashboard folder (needs prediction enabled for the department): 30-day trend vs incoming / outgoing responses, trending auto tags, sentiment analysis, sentiment trend — see ZZ for detail — UI: Dashboards > Zia — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/standard-dashboards-in-desk)
- **ZD-75** IM dashboard for instant-messaging conversation data — UI: IM module — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/instant-messaging/general/articles/getting-started-with-the-im-dashboard)

- **ZD-87** Dashboard actions: export Excel/CSV (KPI dashboard to PDF), print dashboard or component, set as default for whole org, edit/delete (creator or admin), manual refresh, zoom full screen, maximise/minimise, drag-resize — UI: dashboard More menu / component hover — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/dashboard-components)
- **ZD-88** Starred dashboards: max 15 per department; Popular / Telephony / Time Based / Zia dashboards cannot be starred — UI: dashboard icon > Star — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/articles/dashboard-components)
- **ZD-89** Dashboard style (colour, gradient, border, background) not editable; dashboards refresh manually — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/reports-and-dashboards/articles/faqs-zoho-desk-dashboards)

### 10.14 Dashboard / count APIs
- **ZD-90** Dashboards API: closed, created, on-hold, reopened, unresolved ticket counts; response times; resolution times; total response count — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Dashboards_Getcreatedticketscount)
