- **ZD-95** Analytics integration default config API — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#AnalyticsIntegration_CreateDefaultAnalyticsIntegrationConfig)
- **ZD-96** Microsoft Power BI integration: workspace selection, Fabric API (500,000 records/module/sync) or Power BI REST API (1,000,000 records/sync, lookup relations preset); max 75 fields per module; daily sync 6 am admin TZ; delete stops push — UI: Setup > Marketplace and Integrations > Microsoft > Microsoft PowerBI — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/analytics-and-reports/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-microsoft-powerbi)
- **ZD-97** Custom Dashboards extension: Overview + up to 10 report charts (max 11), choose profiles/departments — UI: top band > Extensions > Custom Dashboards — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/analytics-and-reports/articles/custom-dashboards-extension-for-zoho-desk)
- **ZD-98** Customer Insights extension: open vs closed donut, avg FRT, response, resolution, happiness % per contact — UI: ticket detail > Extensions — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/analytics-and-reports/articles/customer-insights-extension-for-zoho-desk)
- **ZD-99** Ticket Insights extension: conversations donut, incoming/outgoing threads, comments, avg handling time, usage web (tasks, approvals, comments, attachments, time entries) — UI: ticket detail > Extensions — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/analytics-and-reports/articles/ticket-insights-extension-for-zoho-desk)
- **ZD-100** Ticket Status Lifecycle extension: status timeline, who changed it, time per status, filter by state — UI: ticket detail > Extensions — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/analytics-and-reports/articles/ticket-status-lifecycle-extension-for-zoho-desk)
- **ZD-101** Microsoft Sentiment Analysis extension: sentiment of latest email, per-sentence (View More), other threads/drafts via More > Sentiment Analysis for Ticket Thread — UI: ticket detail right panel — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/analytics-and-reports/articles/microsoft-sentiment-analysis-for-zoho-desk)
- **ZD-102** Radar mobile app cross-ref: real-time stats/dashboards for managers and agents (see section ZM) — UI: Radar app — Ed: All — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)

### Gaps / thin docs
- No dedicated help article found for the Community dashboard, Telephony agent availability report, or chart creation on custom reports (chart types for reports only described via dashboard Report component).
- Customer happiness predefined report: fields/filters not documented.
- Edition for the individual static reports (Incoming Threads, Reopened, Response/Resolution Time, SLA Tickets, Ticket Stages) is not in the pricing table; only Lifecycle, Agent availability and Agent performance rows exist.
- Limit for number of custom report folders, max rows in tabular reports, and report export record caps for custom reports are not documented (only static report caps: 2,000 / 50,000).
- Conflicting notes: standard-dashboards page says "only Summary reports" can be added; components page says summary and matrix.
- Workforce Management FAQ is conceptual (no extra product functions beyond HQ and existing reports).


---

## 11. Organisation settings (ZO)

Edition note: Company details, logo, currency locale, portal name, spam detection, the UI language (27 languages) and personal preferences are in all editions. Business hours and holiday lists: Express/Standard/Professional get 1 each, Enterprise gets 100 each (Free: none). Departments: Professional 10 (private departments count), Enterprise 50 plus an add-on of up to 450 more (500 in total). The all-departments view, department signatures and department-specific products are Professional+. Custom domain mapping and SSL are Express+. Field translations (multilingual) and agent idle timeout are Professional+. Keyboard shortcuts are Express+. Happiness ratings are Express+ on the pricing page, but the article says Standard+. User limit: Free 3, Express 5, Standard and above unlimited. Light agents: Standard/Professional as an add-on, Enterprise 50 free.

### 11.1 Setup structure and navigation
- **ZO-01** The Setup (gear) page groups settings into categories. The help docs show these paths: Organization (Company, Rebranding, Departments, Business Hours / Holiday Lists, Products, Customer Happiness, Agents, Gamescope), Customization (General Settings, Languages, Personal Settings, Notifications), Personalization (Preferences, Workstation), Automation, Channels and Developer Space (APIs). Older articles show the same pages under "General" (Setup > General > Business Hours / Rebranding / Customer Happiness) — UI: Setup — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/getting-started/articles/admin-guide-to-get-started-with-zoho-desk)
- **ZO-02** The admin onboarding checklist runs in this order: #1 rebrand (logo, portal name, domain mapping, multi-branding), #2 departments, #3 business hours and holiday lists, #4 email channel, … #7 roles and profiles, #10 data import — UI: help article (guide only) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/getting-started/articles/admin-guide-to-get-started-with-zoho-desk)
- **ZO-03** The portal name sets the default addresses: the support email is `support@<portal>.zohodesk.com` and the Help Center is `<portal>.zohodesk.com/portal` — UI: Setup > Organization > Rebranding — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/getting-started/articles/admin-guide-to-get-started-with-zoho-desk)

### 11.2 Company details
- **ZO-04** Company Information page with these fields: Company name, Logo, Employee count, Description, Phone, Mobile, Fax, Website, Primary contact (Super Admin), full Address and Currency locale. Needs admin privileges — UI: Setup > Organization > Company > Edit (upper right) > Save — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/adding-company-detail)
- **ZO-05** Required fields: Company name, Primary contact and Currency locale — UI: Setup > Organization > Company (edit form) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/adding-company-detail)
- **ZO-06** Currency locale: chosen by country under Primary Information. Defaults to US dollars ($). Org-wide. Sets the currency used for support costs in the Time Entry module (the symbol shown on time entries) — UI: Setup > Organization > Company > Edit > Currency Locale — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/adding-company-detail)
- **ZO-07** The currency is locked once it has been set and Zoho Finance is integrated. Changing it later needs Zoho support — UI: Setup > Organization > Company — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/organization-settings/articles/faqs-organization-setting)
- **ZO-08** Company logo rules (from the FAQ): square ratio (e.g. 90x90), at most 2 MB, .jpeg/.jpg/.png. These differ from the rebranding logo rules in ZO-37 — UI: Setup > Organization > Company — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/organization-settings/articles/faqs-organization-setting)
- **ZO-09** API: get or update the organization; list all or accessible organizations; set the default organization (for multi-org users) — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Organizations_Getorganization)
- **ZO-10** API: get, update or delete the organization logo and the organization favicon — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Organizations_Updateorganizationlogo)
- **ZO-11** API Locales: list all countries, languages and time zones (feeds the locale/time zone pickers) — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Locales_Listalltimezones)

### 11.3 Primary contact (Super Admin)
- **ZO-12** Primary contact = the person who set up the account. Receives all subscription emails (upgrade/downgrade, card changes, cancellation). Has full portal access as a support administrator — UI: Setup > Organization > Company — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/billing-and-subscriptions/articles/managing-the-primary-contact-of-your-help-desk)
- **ZO-13** Changing the primary contact: only administrators can do it. The drop-down lists only active users with the default Administrator profile. The new contact becomes Super Admin and is shown on Company Information — UI: Setup > Organization > Company > Edit > Primary Contact > Save — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/adding-company-detail)

### 11.4 Business hours
- **ZO-14** Business hours list with New Business Hour (or "Add Business Hour" when none exist). Admin permission required. Limits: Free 0, Express/Standard/Professional 1, Enterprise 100 — UI: Setup > Organization > Business Hours — Ed: Express+ (multiple: Enterprise) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-15** Business hour form: Name (e.g. "New York Office"), Time Zone drop-down, Holiday List (new or existing), Operational Hours — UI: Business Hours > New Business Hour — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-16** Three operational-hours modes: Always (24x7), Specific Working Hours (the same hours on chosen weekdays) and Custom (different hours per day) — UI: New Business Hour > Operational Hours — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-17** Custom hours allow several separate time blocks in one day (e.g. 9–14, 17–20, 22–01) and overnight shifts that cross midnight into the next day — UI: Operational Hours > Custom — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-18** Time-block editor: drag the top or bottom of the green bar to change start/end. The X icon on a block removes those hours from the day — UI: Operational Hours grid — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-19** Business hours drive SLA due dates, approvals, workflows, blueprints and time-based rules (escalations and due times) — UI: used by SLA / Blueprint / Supervise rules — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-20** SLA in hours counts only business hours. Example: Mon–Fri 9–18, Sat 9–13, Sun off, 50 h SLA, ticket at Mon 10:00 → due next Mon 11:00 — UI: SLA due date — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-21** SLA in days counts business days and ignores hour length. A short Saturday counts as a full day. Examples: Mon 10:00 → Tue 10:00; Mon 22:00 → Tue 18:00; Fri 22:00 → Sat 13:00; weekend after-hours → Mon 18:00 — UI: SLA due date — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-22** Edit and delete from the list. You cannot delete a business hour until the SLAs and time-based rules that use it are dissociated — UI: Business Hours list (hover actions) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-23** Deactivate a business hour with the hover On/Off toggle, then Save. It moves to the Inactive list — UI: Business Hours list > toggle — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-24** API Businesshours: create, get, list, update and delete business-hour sets; Get Associated Automation Rules (SLA, Supervise, Blueprint usage) — UI: API — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Businesshours_GetAssociatedAutomationRules)

### 11.5 Holiday lists
- **ZO-25** Holiday Lists page (a sub-menu of Business Hours) with New Holiday List. Limits: Express/Standard/Professional 1, Enterprise 100 — UI: Setup > Organization > Business Hours > Holiday Lists — Ed: Express+ (multiple: Enterprise) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-26** Holiday list form: Name, Business hours toggle (pick up to 2 business hours), Holiday Occurrence = Recurring Holiday or Year-Specific Holiday. Year-specific allows the current year or the next two — UI: New Holiday List — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-27** Calendar entry: click a date, type a holiday name, Add. Several dates can be selected as a range for extended holidays. The list page shows the category (Recurring / Year-Specific) for each holiday — UI: New Holiday List > calendar — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-28** Recurring holidays repeat on the same date each year (e.g. 1 Jan, 25 Dec). The article says the recurring list "must be updated each year" — UI: Holiday Occurrence — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-29** Association limits: a business hour can have at most 2 holiday lists, and one holiday list can serve more than one business hour (up to 2 when set from the list form). Recurring and year-specific lists can both be attached — UI: Business hour form / Edit Holiday List > Associate Business Hours — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-30** Once the type is chosen and the list is linked to business hours, its dates cannot be changed (guards dependent SLAs and rules) — UI: Edit Holiday List — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-31** Holidays count as outside business hours. SLAs, Blueprints and time-based rules skip them when computing due dates and escalations — UI: (behaviour) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-32** You can also create a holiday list inline from the business hour form (Toggle Associate Holiday List > Create New / Associate Existing > Save Holiday List) — UI: New Business Hour — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-33** Delete one holiday: click it in the calendar > Delete > Save — UI: Setup > Organization > Holiday Lists — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-34** Deactivate a holiday list with the hover toggle Off > Save. Linked business hours then no longer pause on those holidays — UI: Holiday Lists page — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/setting-business-hours-and-holidays)
- **ZO-35** API HolidayList: create, get, list, update and delete — UI: API — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#HolidayList_CreateHolidayList)

### 11.6 Rebranding (logo, favicon, portal name)
- **ZO-36** The company logo replaces the Zoho Desk logo for agents (agent interface) and for customers (default Help Center). Change or Delete it by clicking the logo. Needs Support Administrator permission — UI: Setup > Organization > Rebranding > Company Logo > Change > Choose File — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/rebranding-the-zoho-desk-account)
- **ZO-37** Logo/favicon file rules: up to 2 MB; best size 200x40 px; .jpg/.jpeg/.png/.gif — UI: Rebranding > Company Logo / Favicon — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/rebranding-the-zoho-desk-account)
- **ZO-38** Logo link-back URL: where customers go when they click the logo in the Help Center (e.g. the company homepage) — UI: Rebranding > Company Logo page — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/rebranding-the-zoho-desk-account)
- **ZO-39** Favicon upload. Shown in the browser address bar/tab; change or delete by clicking the icon — UI: Rebranding > Favicon section — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/rebranding-the-zoho-desk-account)
- **ZO-40** Change portal name: at least 5 characters. Takes effect at once; the old URL stops working and has no redirect. The browser must be refreshed. A host-mapped Help Center URL is not affected — UI: Setup > Organization > Rebranding > Portal Name > Change Portal Name > Save — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/rebranding-the-zoho-desk-account)
- **ZO-41** Rebranding settings apply only to the agent interface and the default Help Center. Brands get separate help centres through multi-brand (= departments) — UI: Rebranding — Ed: Enterprise for multi-brand — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/rebranding-the-zoho-desk-account)

### 11.7 Domain mapping
- **ZO-42** Map a custom subdomain (e.g. support.company.com) so the Help Center is served there and agents reach `/support` on it. Needs Support Administrator permission — UI: Setup > Organization > Rebranding > Domain Mapping > Map Domain (setup wizard) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/support-domain-mapping)
- **ZO-43** Prerequisite: a CNAME at the registrar pointing to desk.cs.zohohost.com (EU: .eu, IN: .in). DNS changes are not instant — UI: external DNS — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/support-domain-mapping)
- **ZO-44** Wizard step 1, Add Domain: domain URL of at most 46 characters, then Next — UI: Map Domain wizard — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/support-domain-mapping)
- **ZO-45** Step 2, Verify ownership: CNAME method (add the generated code `zb********` as a CNAME host pointing to desk.cs.zohohost.*) or HTML method (download zoho-domain-verification.html, place it in the site root, click Verify). CNAME check fails behind Cloudflare, which must be paused — UI: Map Domain wizard > Verify — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/support-domain-mapping)
- **ZO-46** Step 3, Get SSL: Zoho installs a free SSL certificate within up to 3 business days. You cannot upload your own or a wildcard certificate — UI: Map Domain wizard > GET SSL — Ed: Express+ (SSL certificate row Express+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/support-domain-mapping)
- **ZO-47** Apply Domain on a verified domain > Apply > Visit Domain > Reload. Only one domain can be applied unless multi-brand is on. desk.zoho.com then redirects to the mapped domain — UI: Domain Mapping list > Apply Domain — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/support-domain-mapping)
- **ZO-48** Reset domain (hover > Reset icon > confirm) goes back to the default URL. A domain mapped to a multi-brand Help Center cannot be reset — UI: Domain Mapping list — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/support-domain-mapping)
- **ZO-49** Delete domain (verified or not) with the hover Delete icon. An applied domain must be reset first; a multi-brand domain must be unlinked or its Help Center deleted first — UI: Domain Mapping list — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/support-domain-mapping)
- **ZO-50** Lock-out recovery URL: `zohodesk.com/support/<portalname>/DomainMapping.do` lets you fix a wrong domain. Third-party cookies must be enabled (Safari) — UI: recovery URL — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/support-domain-mapping)
- **ZO-51** API DomainMapping: add, verify, apply, list and reset the current domain. API SupportEmailDomain: get/update the support email address subdomain — UI: API — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#DomainMapping_Adddomain)

### 11.8 Departments
- **ZO-52** Department list with Active/Inactive tabs and New Department. Admin permission required. Each department has its own agents, mailboxes, chat, forums, web forms, social channels, automation, SLAs and business hours — UI: Setup > Organization > Departments — Ed: Professional+ for multiple — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/departments/articles/managing-departments-in-help-desk)
- **ZO-53** Department limits: Professional 10 (private ones count), Enterprise 50 (one Enterprise article says "unlimited"; pricing says 50) — UI: Departments — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZO-54** Add Department form: Department Name, Display Name in Help Center, Logo (identifies the department in the Help Center), Display in Help Center checkbox (untick = private department), Associate Agents, Description, then Configure Channels (now or later) — UI: Departments > New Department — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/departments/articles/managing-departments-in-help-desk)
- **ZO-55** Private departments are for internal ticketing and are hidden from customers in the Help Center — UI: Add/Edit Department > Display in Help Center — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/departments/articles/managing-departments-in-help-desk)
- **ZO-56** Edit a department (pencil icon): change any field, switch public↔private, use "Change logo" — UI: Departments list > Edit — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/departments/articles/managing-departments-in-help-desk)
- **ZO-57** Departments cannot be deleted or restored, only disabled — UI: Departments — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/organization-settings/articles/faqs-organization-setting)
- **ZO-58** Default department: created at sign-up and marked with a default sign. It cannot be renamed, edited, deleted, disabled or replaced as default, and it owns the default support address. To stop it creating tickets, turn off email fetching on that address (Setup > Channels > Email) — UI: Departments list — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/organization-settings/articles/faqs-organization-setting)
- **ZO-59** Disable department: hover toggle > choose an active department to receive the agents and support mailbox > "Transfer and Disable". Tickets, articles, contracts, products, activities, workflows and SLAs of a disabled department become inaccessible, and its web forms must be regenerated — UI: Departments list > toggle — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/departments/articles/managing-departments-in-help-desk)
- **ZO-60** Disable rules: tickets cannot be moved (only agents and email). The transfer cannot be undone. Agents who are not transferred and have no other department see "You do not have access to any other department". A department with a Help Center needs the Help Center deleted first. A department used in assignment rules must be removed from them first — UI: Disable dialog — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/organization-settings/articles/faqs-organization-setting)
- **ZO-61** Re-enable from the Inactive tab (hover toggle). Old tickets are kept, but agents must be associated again — UI: Departments > Inactive tab — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/departments/articles/managing-departments-in-help-desk)
- **ZO-62** Add agents to an existing department: Agents > +Add > Associate Agent > pick name — UI: Setup > Organization > Departments — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/organization-settings/articles/faqs-organization-setting)
- **ZO-63** Department add-on: Enterprise and equivalent bundles (Zoho One, CRM Plus, ServicePlus — offline for bundles). Up to 450 extra (500 total). $7/dept/month monthly or $6 annual. Bought from profile > Subscription > Upgrade Agent/Add-ons. The "After Addition" column counts active departments plus the add-on. Downgrading from Enterprise cuts to the plan limit — UI: Zoho Store — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/departments/articles/managing-departments-in-help-desk)
- **ZO-64** All Departments view: the department drop-down next to search in Tickets > All Departments lists tickets from all departments the user can access — UI: Tickets top bar > department switcher — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/organization-settings/articles/faqs-organization-setting)
- **ZO-65** Department scoping: Tickets, KB (through categories), Activities and Products are per department; Contacts and Accounts are not. A ticket's department is chosen at creation or changed by Move/clone ("you cannot edit the department of an existing ticket" except by moving) — UI: ticket create / ticket detail > Move — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/organization-settings/articles/faqs-organization-setting)
- **ZO-66** Department-based signatures: in Preferences > Add Signature, pick a department and write an HTML signature (image insert allowed). Save's drop-down "apply to all departments". There is an on/off toggle. Not applied to forwarded emails — UI: Setup > Personalization > Preferences > Add Signature — Ed: Professional+ (department-based) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/updating-user-preferences)
- **ZO-67** Department-specific products: pick the department in the Products list before New Product. Edit/delete per department. Needs Product (View) and Ticket (Update). Products are linked to tickets only from the ticket detail page — UI: Setup > Organization > Products — Ed: Professional+ (department-specific handling) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/adding-and-associating-products-with-ticket)
- **ZO-68** Products list: 10 per page by default, up to 50 (choice remembered). Sort by department, owner, name, code, unit price, created or modified time, oldest/newest. Mass Update/Delete (to Recycle Bin). Predefined and custom views. Detail tabs: Attachments, Contacts, Accounts, Tickets (All/Open/Overdue) — UI: Setup > Organization > Products — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/company-settings/articles/adding-and-associating-products-with-ticket)
- **ZO-69** API Departments: add, update, get, list, list mine, count, get by IDs, check duplicate names, enable/disable, upload/get/delete logo, associate/dissociate agents, list agents and associable agents — UI: API — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Departments_Adddepartment)

### 11.9 Help desk language and field translations
- **ZO-70** Agent UI language: 27 languages (English, Chinese CN/TW, Spanish, French, Russian, Italian, Danish, Swedish, Hindi, Tamil, Vietnamese, Arabic, Thai, Japanese, German, Turkish, Portuguese BR, Dutch, Polish, Bahasa Indonesia, Romanian, Telugu, Hebrew, Korean, Norwegian, Odia). Set per user and applied across all portals that user can access — UI: Setup > Personalization > Preferences > Language — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/updating-user-preferences)
- **ZO-71** Multilingual field translations: translate field names and picklist values into up to 20 languages. Shown in ticket create/edit forms, ticket properties, criteria pickers and the happiness rating — UI: Setup > Customization > Languages (Multilingual page) — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/adding-translations-using-zoho-desk-s-multilingual-capability)
- **ZO-72** Add languages (top right) > pick a language > Add. The language shows with status "Yet to translate" — UI: Languages > Add languages — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/adding-translations-using-zoho-desk-s-multilingual-capability)
- **ZO-73** Export (Customize the export file > Visibility): "Displayed for both agents and customers" (fields, picklists, happiness), "Displayed only for agents" (fields, picklists, layouts, reports, happiness, departments) or "Displayed for customers: Happiness Rating". Downloads a key=value text file whose first line is the language code (must not be edited) — UI: Languages > Export icon — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/adding-translations-using-zoho-desk-s-multilingual-capability)
- **ZO-74** Import the translated file (Import button at top). The same export/edit/import cycle updates translations and picks up new fields — UI: Languages > Import — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/adding-translations-using-zoho-desk-s-multilingual-capability)
- **ZO-75** Export History and Import History tabs, newest first. Import History shows success or failure — UI: Setup > Customization > Languages > Export History / Import History — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/adding-translations-using-zoho-desk-s-multilingual-capability)
- **ZO-76** Turn off one language (search, then the status toggle) or all of them (master Multilingual toggle at top; fields then show untranslated) — UI: Languages page — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/adding-translations-using-zoho-desk-s-multilingual-capability)

### 11.10 Billing and subscriptions
- **ZO-77** Only the primary contact (Super Admin) can manage subscriptions. Opened from the account photo > Subscription (or Upgrade), which opens the Zoho Store in a new tab — UI: profile panel > Subscription — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/billing-and-subscriptions/articles/managing-your-agent-seats)
- **ZO-78** Upgrade paths: Free→Standard/Professional/Enterprise, Standard→Professional, Professional→Enterprise. Store flow: Select Plan > Monthly/Yearly + number of agents > total > Continue > Review Order > Confirm > pay by card or PayPal. Prorated difference is billed at once; billing starts on the upgrade day; leftover trial days are lost; renews automatically. Trial is 15 days (extend by request) — UI: Zoho Store — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/billing-and-subscriptions/articles/upgrading-your-zoho-desk-edition)
- **ZO-79** All agents must be on one edition (no mixing Professional and Enterprise seats) — UI: Zoho Store — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/billing-and-subscriptions/articles/upgrading-your-zoho-desk-edition)
- **ZO-80** Downgrade paths: Enterprise→Professional, Professional→Standard, any→Free. Allowed any time and repeatedly. No data is deleted, but features above the new plan stop working and are hidden. No automatic refund (prorated refund on request). Optional Share Feedback — UI: Zoho Store > Upgrade/Downgrade plan > Downgrade > Confirm — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/billing-and-subscriptions/articles/downgrading-your-zoho-desk-edition)
- **ZO-81** Licence (agent) seats: Add (prorated cost for the rest of the cycle > Continue > Make Payment) and Reduce (deactivate agents first > Downgrade > Confirm). Free has a fixed 3 seats. Offline purchases go through upgrade@zohocorp.com. User limit: Free 3, Express 5, Standard+ unlimited — UI: Zoho Store > Agents Add/Reduce — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/billing-and-subscriptions/articles/managing-your-agent-seats)
- **ZO-82** Light agents (internal users who can read tickets and add private comments but not reply): add-on on Standard/Professional, 50 free on Enterprise plus an add-on — UI: Zoho Store / Agents — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZO-83** Update credit card: Store > Your Store Details > Payment Method > Change > Update > OK. An expired card fails payment and can move the account to Free — UI: Zoho Store — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/billing-and-subscriptions/articles/update-your-credit-card-on-file)
- **ZO-84** Free plan contents: 3 agents, 1 support email plus web form and Help Center, priority SLAs with 1 escalation level, 2 macros, private KB, canned templates, form layout and tabs, default profiles and roles, mobile and Radar apps, 24x5 email support — UI: (plan) — Ed: Free — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/billing-and-subscriptions/articles/which-is-my-ideal-zoho-desk-plan-free-professional-or-enterprise)
- **ZO-85** Enterprise extras (from the plan article): live chat, multi-brand help centres, cross-department reports, blueprints, scheduled reports, contracts, role-based sharing, 50 free light agents, unlimited email aliases, 50 web forms, 30 workflow/time-based rules and macros per dept/module, 30 assignment rules, 20 SLAs, 50 profiles, 250 roles. Price shown: $45/agent/month, about 22% saving yearly — UI: (plan) — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/billing-and-subscriptions/articles/is-the-enterprise-plan-in-zoho-desk-ideal-for-us)
- **ZO-86** API credits (pointer): daily pool of Free 5,000; Express 25,000 + users×100; Standard 50,000 + users×250; Professional 75,000 + users×500; Enterprise 100,000 + users×1000; plus add-on credits. UI/mobile actions are free. Usage shown under Setup > Developer Space > APIs — UI: Setup > Developer Space > APIs — Ed: All (dashboard Express+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/apidashboard/articles/working-with-api-credits)
- **ZO-87** API License: Get License Plan Details and Get All License Feature Details (edition and feature limits). API AutomationFeatureCount gives per-feature usage counts — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#License_GetLicensePlanDetails)

### 11.11 General settings (idle timeout, spam detection)
- **ZO-88** Agent idle timeout: when on, an agent with no keyboard, mouse or app activity for N minutes becomes Offline, so no new tickets are assigned to them. Applies to every agent in the org. Default 5 minutes. Admin permission required — UI: Setup > Customization > General Settings > Idle Timeout toggle + minutes > Save — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/general-settings/articles/configuring-idle-timeout-for-agents)
- **ZO-89** API agentAvailability: get/update the availability configuration, get current availability, list online/offline agents — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#agentAvailability_UpdateAgentAvailabilityConfiguration)
- **ZO-90** Automatic spam detection: on by default and scans incoming email. Toggle under the Contacts panel — UI: Setup > Customization > General Settings > Contacts > Automatic Spam Detection — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/general-settings/articles/managing-spam-detection-settings)
- **ZO-91** Three spam options: (a) mark only suspicious tickets as spam; (b) mark tickets and their contacts as spam, so later tickets from that contact go straight to Spam Tickets; (c) mark tickets, but do not mark the contact if it has other valid tickets — UI: Automatic Spam Detection radio options — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/customization/articles/faqs-spam-detection)

### 11.12 Personal settings
- **ZO-92** My Information profile: First/Last name (last name used in customer communication), Photo (≤500 KB, JPG/PNG/GIF/JPEG, Delete allowed), Email (from the Zoho account, receives notifications), Roles & Permissions (read-only), Department(s), Channel Expert (Email, Facebook, Forums, Phone, Web, Chat, Feedback Widget, Twitter), Bio (shown in chat and forums), Phone/Mobile/extension, Language, Country, Time Zone (timestamps) — UI: Setup > Customization > Personal Settings > My Information — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/personal-settings/articles/agent-essentials)
- **ZO-93** Only the agent can change their own photo, language, country and time zone (not the admin) — UI: My Information — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/personal-settings/articles/agent-essentials)
- **ZO-94** Preferences: landing page (Tickets, Feeds, Countdown/Handshake/Status/Priority modes, Analytics, Activities, KB, Customers, Community, Chat, Social; you cannot pick a module that is unused or not permitted), keyboard shortcuts, article suggestions on reply, conversation vs tabs view, default Send vs Send & Close, name format (drag First/Last), signature, language, country locale, time zone, 12/24 h, date format with delimiter, "Shorten Date/Time Info", record age format (Years…Seconds or HH:MM:SS) — UI: Setup > Personalization > Preferences — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/updating-user-preferences)
- **ZO-95** Quick preferences (profile picture > Preferences): Appearance (light/night/auto), Theme, Display Layout, Font Size, Default Editor Font, Ticket Default Preferences, Accessibility Controls. Saved per browser and applied to Tickets, Contacts, Activities, Community, IM, Social and Analytics — UI: profile panel > Preferences — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/personalizing-zoho-desk-interface-with-preferences)
- **ZO-96** Keyboard shortcuts: off by default, enabled per user (profile > Preferences > Keyboard Shortcuts ON). Shift+? or the lower-right Shortcuts icon opens the list. Example: T + to add a ticket — UI: profile panel > Preferences — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/personal-settings/articles/viewing-keyboard-shortcuts)
- **ZO-97** Workstation: ticket-detail layout styles Standard (default), Classic, Modern, Compact, Advanced (Marketplace panel), Superior (single action band) and Custom. Preview with Sample Data; Apply this style > "Yes, proceed". Enterprise early access (by request) — UI: Setup > Personalization > Workstation — Ed: Enterprise (early access) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/personal-settings/articles/personalize-ticket-detail-view-with-workstation)
- **ZO-98** Workstation custom style: one per agent. Rename it; move or hide the info bar, ticket properties, action band and frames by double-clicking. Changing a predefined style turns it into the custom style and replaces the previous one. Edit or delete on hover ("Yes, delete") — UI: Workstation > Create Custom Style — Ed: Enterprise (early access) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/personal-settings/articles/personalize-ticket-detail-view-with-workstation)
- **ZO-99** API: Get/Update My Preferences, Get/Update My Entity Preferences, Get My Information, Upload/Delete My Photo; AgentSignatures get/update (including customized per-department signatures) — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Agents_UpdateMyPreferences)

### 11.13 Customer happiness rating (org-level settings)
- **ZO-100** Turn on happiness ratings with Get Started. Needs the Administrative (Customer Happiness Ratings) permission. A master toggle at the top turns it off — UI: Setup > Organization > Customer Happiness — Ed: Express+ (pricing) / Standard+ (article) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZO-101** Rating configuration: Rating Question; Good/Okay/Bad (Okay can be turned off); add the link to every response or only on close (close turns on the Ticket Closed notification); link as question only or question with smileys; Display Widget in Help Center; Show Agent Photo; Reopen Ticket on negative or all ratings; Enable Survey for chosen departments; Notify Ticket Owner on negative or all ratings; notify extra users (supervisors) on negative ratings — UI: Customer Happiness > Rating Configuration — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZO-102** Who can open the rating link: Anyone on the internet (default); Requesters and cc'd users (email only, not the Help Center); or End users (requester, cc and the requester's organisation; others are asked to sign up) — UI: Rating Configuration > Rating link accessible to — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZO-103** Rating rules: customers cannot opt out; one rating per response or ticket (not per customer); the link works once, so the rating cannot be changed — UI: (behaviour) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZO-104** Customise each rating option (pencil): pick a smiley or upload an image, rating text, response after rating and after feedback — UI: Customer Happiness > Edit Rating — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZO-105** Rating form language: English plus 27 translations. Order used: ticket language, then browser language, then English. Translations come from the Languages export "Displayed for customers: Happiness Rating" — UI: Setup > Customization > Languages — Ed: Express+ (translation tooling Professional+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZO-106** Placeholder position: on every response it sits below the agent signature and can be moved inside the editor; on close it sits above the signature in the "Closing a Ticket" template. The placeholder `${Cases.Customer Happiness Rating}` can be used in workflow alerts (e.g. phone tickets on close, follow-up on Bad ratings) — UI: Setup > Automation > Workflows — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/workflows/articles/configuring-automation-rules-for-happiness-ratings)
- **ZO-107** Help Center widget: Good/Okay/Bad percentages and the last 100 ratings, recalculated on every new rating — UI: Help Center (when the widget is on) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/setting-up-customer-happiness-rating)
- **ZO-108** Score shown on the ticket detail (requester score, split for this department and all departments), on the contact and account list and detail pages (Happiness Rating tab: score, positive/neutral/negative split, feedback with ticket ID, subject and agent). Chat ratings are good/bad by default for chat departments — UI: ticket detail / Customers — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/customer-happiness/articles/viewing-happiness-rating-score)
- **ZO-109** API CustomerFeedback: list all feedback, count, get content, get the placeholder link — UI: API — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#CustomerFeedback_Listallcustomerfeedback)

### Gaps / thin docs
- No help article covers the org-wide **time zone**. Time zone is set per business hour (ZO-15) and per user (ZO-92/94). The Company page lists no time zone field, so an org-level default time zone is unconfirmed.
- The **company logo size rules conflict**: the FAQ says square, 90x90, jpeg/jpg/png; the Rebranding article says 200x40 and also .gif. Whether the Company-page logo and the Rebranding logo are the same setting is unconfirmed.
- **Enterprise department limit**: the pricing page says 50 (+450 add-on) and the departments article agrees, but the older "Why Enterprise" article says "unlimited". Pricing is used here.
- The **happiness rating edition** differs: pricing says Express+, the article says Standard+.
- **Business hours limits**: pricing says Express/Standard/Professional 1 and Enterprise 100. No article says what happens at the limit (button hidden or error). Holiday-list association limits (2 each way) come only from the article.
- No article describes the **Setup home page layout** itself (category tiles or search box). The structure in ZO-01 is rebuilt from paths in other articles. Category names vary between "Organization" and "General".
- **Multilingual field translations**: the article says "up to 20 languages", the UI language list has 27 and the happiness form has 27. Which languages the field translations allow is not listed.
- **Department signatures**: only the Preferences article (signature per department) and the pricing row cover them. An admin-level department signature, separate from the agent's own, is not documented.
- **Billing**: the add-on catalogue in the Store (apart from departments, light agents and API credits) and the Store screens are third-party (Zoho Store) and not detailed. Cancellation and the payment-failure grace period are not documented.
- The **idle timeout** maximum and the allowed minute values are not documented; only the 5-minute default is.
- The **Data center location** choice at sign-up (pricing row, Privacy) has no setup UI and is left out.
- The API sections **APICredits / CreditAlert / CreditAllocation / CreditConsumption / IncreaseCredits** list no operation names in api_ops.txt (they are concept pages).


---

## 12. Users, roles and access (ZG)

Edition note: User limit Free 3 users, Express 5, Standard/Professional/Enterprise unlimited (paid per agent seat). Editable profiles: Free/Express default only (non-editable), Std 5, Pro 25, Ent 50. Roles: Free/Express default only, Std 5, Pro 25, Ent 250. Light agents: paid add-on on Std/Pro, 50 free on Ent (plus add-on beyond 50); none on Free (pricing also shows none on Express). Teams, team ownership and Field-level access control are Professional+. Data sharing (organization-level Private / Public Read Only / Public Read/Write/Delete) is Enterprise only. Agent add/deactivate/delete/anonymise and default profiles exist in all editions.

### 12.1 Agents: adding and inviting
- **ZG-01** Add a single agent — fields First Name, Last Name, Email (used as login), Departments, Role and Permission, Channel Expert, Phone, Extension, Mobile; button "New Agent" — UI: Setup > User Management > Agents > New Agent (or + icon next to Agents in the menu) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-02** Permission to manage agents — only users whose profile has Manage Agents can open the feature — UI: Setup > User Management > Agents — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-03** Department selection on new agent — multi-select; shown only when more than one department exists; current department preselected — UI: New Agent page > Departments — Ed: All (multi-dept Pro+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-04** Role and Permission quick picks — Agent / Admin / Light Agent, or Custom to pick any created role + profile — UI: New Agent page > Role and Permission — Ed: All (Custom needs editable profiles, Std+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-05** Ticket access scope when "Agent" is picked — Access all tickets / Their tickets and unassigned tickets / Access own, team, and unassigned tickets — UI: New Agent page > Role and Permission — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-06** Channel Expert (channel preferences) — multi-select of channels such as Phone, Chat, Twitter, Email — UI: New Agent / Edit Agent page > Channel Expert — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-07** Additional agent info — Bio, Desk Phone, Extension, Cell/Mobile — UI: New Agent page — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-08** Invitation email on add — agent must click the Access link within 7 days — UI: email to agent — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-09** Bulk invite agents — max 10 agents at once; rows of Name + Email; Department auto-filled; Profile + Role drop-downs; + icon adds a row; "Invite Agents"; remaining seats shown in top panel; profile choice limited by remaining licences — UI: Setup > User Management > Invite multiple agents (Multiple Agent Invitation page) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-10** Bulk-invited agents cannot be re-invited after the acceptance period lapses — stated limitation — UI: Multiple Agent Invitation — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-11** Invite acceptance flow, new Zoho user — Access link > Sign Up > create account > Continue signing in > confirm email via confirmation link — UI: Zoho Accounts sign-up — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-12** Invite acceptance flow, existing Zoho user — Access link > Continue signing in > lands in the company's Desk portal — UI: Zoho Accounts — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-13** Agent-set personal details at sign-up — language, country locale, time zone set only by the agent during sign-up — UI: sign-up — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-14** Not Confirmed view + Re-invite — unconfirmed users listed under "Not Confirmed"; hover row > Re-invite icon sends a new email; re-invite is distinct from activating — UI: Setup > User Management > Agents > Not Confirmed view — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-15** Agent list views — Active Agents, Not Confirmed, Deactivated Agents, Deleted Agents (tab) — UI: Setup > User Management > Agents (Agent List page) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-16** Edit agent — hover row > Edit icon, or open agent detail > Edit; change role/profile ("Edit Agent Information") — UI: Setup > User Management > Agents — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-17** Imported users default to Role "Manager" and Profile "Agent" — admins change them afterwards on the Agents page — UI: Setup > Import + Agents — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-18** Agent photo — upload / delete own photo; get agent photo; profile picture opens User Info panel in top bar — UI: top bar profile picture (User Info panel) + API Uploadmyphoto / DeleteMyPhoto / Getagentphoto — Ed: All (inferred) — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Agents_Uploadmyphoto)
- **ZG-19** Agent custom fields — Agents module supports custom fields in Layouts and Fields — UI: Setup > Customization > Layouts and Fields (module Agents) — Ed: All (inferred) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/understanding-fields-and-properties)

### 12.2 Agent licences
- **ZG-20** One licence per active agent — inactive users not counted; deleted agents no longer count toward the agent limit but seats stay billed until reduced — UI: Subscription — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-21** User limit per edition — Free 3, Express 5, Std/Pro/Ent unlimited; Free cannot add seats beyond 3 — UI: Subscription — Ed: All — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZG-22** Add licence seats — only Primary Contact / Super Admin; Account Photo > Subscription > Zoho Store > Add (Agents) > count > prorated cost > Make Payment — UI: Zoho Store — Ed: Paid editions — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/billing-and-subscriptions/articles/managing-your-agent-seats)
- **ZG-23** Reduce licence seats — Zoho Store > Reduce > count > Downgrade > Confirm; deactivate agents first is recommended — UI: Zoho Store — Ed: Paid editions — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/billing-and-subscriptions/articles/managing-your-agent-seats)
- **ZG-24** Agent counts (API) — Getagentscount, Getallagentscount — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Agents_Getagentscount)

### 12.3 Light agents
- **ZG-25** Light Agent default profile — read-only; cannot edit ticket fields, reply or assign; can add private comments in tickets of their department — UI: New Agent > Role and Permission > Light Agent — Ed: Std+ add-on; Ent 50 free — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-26** Light agent edition/limits — not in Free; paid add-on in paid editions; Ent includes 50 free light agents; billed separately across bundles (pricing shows "-" for Free and Express) — UI: Subscription — Ed: Std+ (Ent 50 free) — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZG-27** Light agents can — view Tickets, Accounts, Contacts, Articles, Activities, Reports, Dashboards; view Company, Products, Personal Settings (except Signature), Support Contract in Setup; private comments on Tickets/Contacts/Accounts/Activities/Articles; edit own profile; export module data via Import/Export; view all custom views and create views visible only to them; receive automation and escalation alerts — UI: across modules — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-28** Light agents cannot — add/edit/delete records in any module; public comments; see Community tab; merge/split/move tickets; view or create Global Reports; be assigned tickets or added to Teams — UI: across modules — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-29** Light agent department scoping — sees only tickets of assigned department(s); dept picker only when more than one dept — UI: New Agent page — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-30** Light agents cannot hold Read and Write field permission — only Read Only / Don't Show — UI: Setup > Layouts and Fields > Field Permissions — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/understanding-fields-and-properties)
- **ZG-31** Light agent profile via API — Getlightagentprofile — UI: API — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Profiles_Getlightagentprofile)

### 12.4 Deactivate, delete, anonymise
- **ZG-32** Deactivate agent — Active Agents view > hover > deactivate icon > "Deactivate Agent" > reassign open tickets and activities per department > "Yes, I understand. Deactivate Now"; login blocked; licence kept; agent moves to Deactivated Agents — UI: Setup > User Management > Agents — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-33** Activate deactivated agent — from Deactivated Agents view; only active agents count toward licences — UI: Agents > Deactivated Agents — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-34** Delete agent — Active Agents view > delete icon > "Delete Agent" > reassign open tickets/activities per department (or leave tickets unassigned) > "Yes, I understand. Delete Now"; agent archived (name kept on past records), moved to Deleted Agents tab; cannot be restored, re-add with same email as new agent — UI: Setup > User Management > Agents — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-35** Delete restrictions — cannot delete yourself, Primary Contact / Super Administrator, sole agent in a department, or agents who set up Zoho Cliq, Live Chat or Twilio integrations — UI: delete flow — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-36** Record ownership transfer on delete — owner of Accounts, Contacts, Products etc. can be changed to a new agent; tickets can be marked unassigned — UI: delete flow — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-37** Private list views — deleted with the agent on delete; kept (still private) on deactivate; shared views stay accessible to others — UI: module list views — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-38** Automation re-pointing on delete/deactivate — replacement agent written into round-robin and direct assignment "assign to", workflow/macro/supervise ticket-owner field updates, Notify Agents (not additional-recipient emails), SLA escalation owner condition and "whom to escalate", workflow task assignee — UI: delete flow — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-39** Affected integrations listed on deletion page — custom-function connections and integrations/extensions set up by the agent stop working and must be re-authorised by an admin — UI: delete flow — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-40** Reports/dashboards in "only me" folders — inaccessible on deactivate; deleted on delete (incl. scheduled reports) — UI: Analytics — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-41** Community, KB, web forms, feedback widget, ASAP unaffected — creator deactivated: primary contact becomes contact owner / ticket assignee; KB articles stay accessible — UI: Help Center — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-42** Anonymise deleted agent (GDPR) — Deleted Agents view > Anonymize toggle > optional Anonymous Name > "Anonymize Agent"; name, emails, phones, photo, bio erased; replaced by "Anonymous Agent" or nickname in tickets, comments, mentions, history; irreversible; anonymised agents vanish from Deleted Agents list — UI: Agents > Deleted Agents — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-43** Anonymise and delete in one step — delete flow > Anonymize Agent toggle + Anonymous Name (also used in community posts) > reassign > Delete — UI: delete flow — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZG-44** Scheduled reassignment for deactivated/deleted agents (API) — Schedulereassignmentfordeactivatedordeletedagents — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Agents_Schedulereassignmentfordeactivatedordeletedagents)
- **ZG-45** Agents API set — Addagent, Updateagent, Activateagents, Deactivateagent, Deleteconfirmedagent, Deleteunconfirmedagents, Reinviteunconfirmedagents, Anonymizedeletedagent, ListDeletedAgents, Listagents, Getagent, GetagentbyemailID, GetanagentbyZUID, GetMyInformation, Get/UpdateMyPreferences, Get/UpdateMyEntityPreferences — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Agents_Addagent)

### 12.5 Agent availability and preferences
- **ZG-46** Idle timeout — org-wide; toggle + minutes (default 5) before status becomes Offline; needs administrative permission — UI: Setup > Customization > General Settings > Idle Timeout — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/general-settings/articles/configuring-idle-timeout-for-agents)
- **ZG-47** Agent availability API — Get/UpdateAgentAvailabilityConfiguration, GetCurrentAvailability, GetOnlineAgents, GetOfflineAgents — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#agentAvailability_GetOnlineAgents)

### 12.6 Teams
- **ZG-49** Teams feature — sub-groups within a department; tickets and activities (tasks, events, calls) assignable to teams — UI: Setup > User Management > Teams — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZG-50** Create team — Team Name, Logo (colour icon or custom image), Description, members (agents, roles, roles & subordinates, other teams) > Save — UI: Setup > User Management > Teams > New Team (Add New Team page) — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/adding-and-managing-teams)
- **ZG-51** Team permission — requires Administrative (Agents and Teams) / Manage Teams permission — UI: Setup — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/adding-and-managing-teams)
- **ZG-52** Team limits — max 60 teams per department; per team max 100 agents, 50 sub-teams, 20 roles, 20 roles-with-subordinates — UI: New Team — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/adding-and-managing-teams)
- **ZG-53** Teams are department-specific — only agents of the department can be members; agents may belong to several teams within and across departments; not every agent needs a team — UI: Teams — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/adding-and-managing-teams)
- **ZG-54** Dynamic membership by role — agents added/removed from a role or moved between departments update team membership automatically — UI: Teams — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/adding-and-managing-teams)
- **ZG-55** Team logo display — ticket owner picker, manual assignment, Work Modes, activity assignment, Contact/Account associated tickets, private-comment team tagging (name only after tag), custom view visibility, Activities list (Classic and Compact) — UI: multiple — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/adding-and-managing-teams)
- **ZG-56** Team Assignment toggle — per department, on by default; when off the Teams tab is hidden in assignment and only agents are listed — UI: Setup > User Management > Teams > department drop-down > Team Assignment toggle (top right) — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/adding-and-managing-teams)
- **ZG-57** Edit team — hover > Edit > change name, logo, members, description > Save — UI: Setup > User Management > Teams — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/adding-and-managing-teams)
- **ZG-58** Delete team with reassignment — hover > Delete > pick agent/team for Open Tickets and Open Activities > "Reassign and Delete" — UI: Setup > User Management > Teams — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/adding-and-managing-teams)
- **ZG-59** Downgrade behaviour (from Pro/Ent) — new tickets/activities not assigned to teams; Teams tab hidden in auto-assignment; existing tickets keep team name — UI: Teams — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/adding-and-managing-teams)
- **ZG-60** Team notifications — email/SMS and Feed notifications on team assignment; prebuilt team notifications not editable — UI: Setup > Customization > Notifications > Notification Rules > Team Notifications; Feeds Preference — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/adding-and-managing-teams)
- **ZG-61** Teams in automation — Team as criterion and target in workflows, direct and round-robin assignment, time-based rules, SLA targets/escalation, macros — UI: Setup > Automation — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/actions/articles/using-teams-in-automation)
- **ZG-62** Team ownership and Teams queue — assign tickets to teams; queue of open tickets per team — UI: ticket owner picker, Work Mode / queue — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZG-63** Teams API — Createteam, Updateteam, Deleteteam, Getteam, Listteamsindepartment, Listteamsfromallassociateddepartments, Listassociableteams, Listassociatedteamsforarole, Listassociatedteamsofagent, Listdetailsofteammembers — UI: API — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Teams_Createteam)

### 12.7 Profiles
- **ZG-64** Profile limits — Free/Express default non-editable only; Std 5, Pro 25, Ent 50 — UI: Setup > User Management > Profiles — Ed: Std+ for custom — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZG-65** Default (non-editable) profiles — Support Administrator (all features), Agent (limited), Light Agent (read-only) — UI: Profiles list — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-66** Editable system profiles — Newbie Agent, Supervisor, Support Manager — UI: Profiles list — Ed: Std+ (inferred) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-67** Create profile by cloning — Profile Name, Clone Profile (source), Description > Save; new profiles always start as a clone — UI: Setup > User Management > Profiles > New Profile — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-68** Edit profile permissions — four groups: Module, Ticket, Administrative, Multi-department; editable by Support Administrator or users with Manage Permissions — UI: Profiles > select profile > Profile Information — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-69** Module access permissions — per standard and custom module: module visible on/off, View, Create, Update, Delete, Update/Delete All Records — UI: Profile > Module Permissions — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-70** KB permissions in profile — Manage KB (per own departments: articles/categories CRUD, recycle bin view, access settings view, sort articles; no Organize Category) and Admin Access (cross-department KB incl. restore/delete from recycle bin and access settings) — UI: Profile > Module Permissions — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-71** Custom view permissions — own views: Create/Edit/Delete, Share; shared views: Edit, Delete, Reshare; UI hides disallowed actions — UI: Profile > Module Permissions > Custom view — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-72** Ticket permissions — Send Email, Change Ownership (all / only unassigned / none), View Unassigned, Close Tickets, Review Emails, Find and Merge (up to 3 tickets), Add Followers, Add/Remove Followers, Revoke Blueprint (admins default on), Manage Tags — UI: Profile > Ticket Permissions — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-73** Administrative permissions — Comment Permissions, Manage Agents (view/add/update/delete), Manage Teams, Manage Permissions, Rebranding, Manage Modules, Helpdesk Automation, Manage Departments, Customer Happiness Ratings, Email and Ticket Templates, Administrator Dashboard, Agent Overview, Manage Layouts and Fields, End Users, Google Analytics Dashboard, Approve End User Signups, Support Channels, Manage General Settings, Multilingual, Manage Time Tracking, Webhooks (overrides module/dept permissions), Gamescope, Manage Privacy Settings — UI: Profile > Administrative Permissions — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-74** Import / Export permissions — Import Records and Export Records, each with a choice of which modules — UI: Profile > Administrative Permissions — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-75** Multi-department permissions — Global Reports and Dashboards, Access Data across Departments, Move Tickets, Share Tickets — UI: Profile > Multi-department Permissions — Ed: Pro+ (multi-dept) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-76** Comment permission off blocks commenting — when disabled agents cannot leave a comment on any record — UI: Profile > Administrative — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-77** Associate user with profile — Support Administrator or Manage Agents (Add and Update) can set profile on Edit Agent Information (default or Custom pick list) — UI: Setup > User Management > Agents > Edit — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-78** Delete profile with transfer — Delete icon > choose profile to transfer users > "Transfer & Delete"; Support Administrator, Light Agent and Agent cannot be deleted — UI: Setup > User Management > Profiles — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-79** Read-only metadata via GET APIs regardless of profile — department, team, company, agents, profiles, licences, roles, data sharing rules metadata viewable; create/edit/delete still gated — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZG-80** Profiles API — Listprofiles, Getprofile, Cloneprofile, Updateprofile, Deleteprofile, Getprofilecount, Listagentsbyprofile, Getmyprofiledetails, Getmyprofilepermissions — UI: API — Ed: Std+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Profiles_Cloneprofile)
- **ZG-81** Per-profile billing rate — time-tracking billing can use a fixed cost per profile (agent, light agent, custom) — UI: Setup > Time Tracking billing preferences — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)

- **ZG-82** Role limits — Free/Express default non-editable only; Std 5, Pro 25, Ent 250 — UI: Setup > User Management > Roles — Ed: Std+ for custom — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
