### 12.9 Data sharing rules
- **ZG-91** Organization-level data sharing per module — Private (default for all modules) / Public Read Only / Public Read/Write/Delete — UI: Setup > User Management > Data Sharing > Edit Data Sharing (Edit Default Organization Permissions) > Save — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-sharing/articles/setting-up-data-sharing-rules)
- **ZG-92** Private semantics — record owner, their superiors and admins (all records in dept) can access; role hierarchy applies only when Private — UI: Data Sharing — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-sharing/articles/setting-up-data-sharing-rules)
- **ZG-93** Support Administrator override — admin-profile users see all records in the department even when module is Private, regardless of role position — UI: data access — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-sharing/articles/setting-up-data-sharing-rules)
- **ZG-94** Child data follows record — attachments, comments and time entries visible if the record is visible; Import History always shows own and subordinates' records — UI: data access — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-sharing/articles/setting-up-data-sharing-rules)
- **ZG-95** Excluded modules — Chat, Social, Community, Reports, Dashboards not supported by org-level sharing — UI: Data Sharing — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-sharing/articles/setting-up-data-sharing-rules)
- **ZG-96** Data Sharing API — Getdatasharingrules, Updatedatasharingrules — UI: API — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#DataSharingRules_Updatedatasharingrules)

### 12.10 Field-level permissions
- **ZG-97** Field Permissions per module + profile — each field Read and Write / Read Only / Don't Show > Save — UI: Setup > Customization > Layouts and Fields > Fields Permissions — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/understanding-fields-and-properties)
- **ZG-98** Field permission validation — mandatory fields cannot be Read Only / Don't Show; field can be made mandatory only if all profiles have Read and Write; some system and time-based fields locked to read-only — UI: Field Permissions / field editor — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/working-with-custom-fields)
- **ZG-99** Preview layout per profile — preview how fields appear per profile before saving — UI: Layouts and Fields — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/understanding-fields-and-properties)
- **ZG-100** Help Center pseudo-profile — select profile "Help Center" in Field Permissions to hide ticket fields from end users — UI: Field Permissions > Profile = Help Center — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/customization/articles/faqs-customization)
- **ZG-101** Field permissions API — GetFieldPermissions, UpdateFieldPermissions; layouts tied to profiles via AssociateLayoutprofiles / GetLayoutProfiles — UI: API — Ed: Pro+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#OrganizationFields_UpdateFieldPermissions)

### Gaps / thin docs
- No agent-side "user groups" or "labels" for agents found: KB "User Groups" and Community "User Labels" are end-user (help center) features, belong to the Help Center section, not here.
- Agent photo upload UI is not described in any article; only API ops (Uploadmyphoto, DeleteMyPhoto, Getagentphoto) and the top-bar profile picture are documented. ZG-18 edition is inferred.
- Edition for editable system profiles (Newbie Agent, Supervisor, Support Manager) is not stated; assumed Std+ because Free/Express profiles are "Default (Non-Editable)".
- Light agents on Express: article says "not in Free; add-on in all paid editions" but pricing row shows "-" for Express. Conflict to verify live.
- Team limits phrasing is inconsistent in the article ("100 agents who can be part of 50 sub-teams or 20 roles" vs "100 agents, 50 sub teams, 20 roles, 20 roles with subordinates"). Use the latter, verify live.
- Data sharing in Zoho Desk docs covers only organization-wide defaults per module; no criteria-based or role-to-role custom sharing rules are documented (unlike Zoho CRM). The enterprise article mentions "control which data can be shared between peers", which maps to the role-level Share Data with Peers toggle.
- Role and profile behaviour on Free/Express (which default roles exist beyond CEO/Manager) is not documented.
- Agent "Bio", Extension and Channel Expert full option list not enumerated beyond examples.
- Exact validation/error messages (seat limit reached, duplicate email, deleting a profile with users) not documented.
- Agent idle timeout edition taken from pricing row "Agent idle timeout" (Pro+); the article itself states no edition.


---

## 13. Customisation (modules, layouts, fields, rules, templates, statuses) (ZL)

Edition note: All editions incl. Free: customise tabs (rename/hide/reorder), customise form fields, notification rules. Custom views and custom ticket statuses with status grouping are Express+. Custom fields are 10/module on Express, 50 on Std, 150 on Pro and 230 on Ent. Field dependencies and custom email templates are Std+ (Free/Express use default templates only). Professional+: formula fields (Pro 5, Ent 10 /module), ticket templates (600/dept), department-specific layouts, multilingual, custom lookups (Pro 3, Ent 5 /module & layout), picklist colour coding (Pro 5, Ent 10 /module), ticket ID customisation, field-level access control, nested picklists and ePHI labelling. Enterprise only: multiple layouts (20/dept), layout rules (50/dept), validation rules (50/dept), custom modules (10), regex, field encryption (custom, 10/module) and system/standard field encryption (Contacts, max 20).

### 13.1 Standard modules and tabs
- **ZL-01** Standard module set — Tickets, Accounts, Contacts, Products, Tasks, Calls, Events, Contracts, Time Entry (+ Agents as a record module in the API). Tasks are Std+, Calls/Events Pro+ and Contracts Ent — UI: Setup > Customization > Modules and Tabs > Manage Modules (columns Module Name / API Name / Data Storage / Last Modified By / Time) — Ed: All (per-module edition varies) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/standard-modules-and-fields-zoho-desk)
- **ZL-02** Module data scope — dept-level: Tickets, Tasks, Calls, Events, Contracts, Time Entry. Org-level: Accounts, Contacts, Products, Agents. Shown as "Department-level"/"Organization-level" and locked on standard modules — UI: Manage Modules > Edit <Module> — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/standard-modules-and-fields-zoho-desk)
- **ZL-03** Edit standard module — editable: Module Name (Plural)*, Module Name (Singular)*, Description, Module Permission* (profile chips). "Accessible To" (All Departments) and Data Storage are locked. Save/Cancel (live 5 Oct 2026) — UI: Manage Modules > click module name — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/customizing-standard-modules-zoho-desk)
- **ZL-04** Default system fields per standard module — e.g. Ticket Subject* 255, Status*, Description 32,000 chars; Account Name* 100; Contact Last Name* 200; Product Name* 50. Mandatory system fields (Department, Contact Name) cannot be edited or deleted — UI: layout editor ("Non-removable standard field") — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/standard-modules-and-fields-zoho-desk)
- **ZL-05** Standard fields: show/hide/reorder org-wide, mark required per layout, cannot be deleted or moved to Unused if basic (Department, Contact Name, Subject, Status, Email, Description, Due Date, Priority, Channel, Resolution, Owner) — UI: layout editor gear menu — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/understanding-fields-and-properties)
- **ZL-06** Organize Tabs — drag modules between "Selected Modules" and "Unselected Modules" ("Drop here to remove"), drag to reorder. Saves automatically. At least one module must stay selected. Hidden tabs are hidden for every profile — UI: Setup > Customization > Modules and Tabs > Organize Tabs — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/customizing-help-desk-tabs)
- **ZL-07** Grouped tabs — Activities groups Tasks/Events/Calls. Analytics groups Reports/Dashboards. Customers groups Contacts/Accounts (live tab bar) — UI: top tab bar — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/customizing-help-desk-tabs)
- **ZL-08** Rename Tabs/Modules — hover a card > Edit > singular and plural names (e.g. Knowledge Base > "FAQ"). Applies everywhere except custom reports and dashboards. 19 renamable cards incl. Reports, Dashboards, Analytics, Customers, IM — UI: Setup > Customization > Modules and Tabs > Rename Tabs — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/customizing-standard-modules-zoho-desk)
- **ZL-09** Tab order/rename scope — per organisation, no per-user tab order (inferred). No "More" overflow on web tab bar. Mobile shows custom modules under More and needs "Refresh Portal Metadata" — UI: top tab bar — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/customizing-help-desk-tabs)
- **ZL-10** Permission for module customisation — "Manage Modules" (profile administrative permission). "Manage Layouts and Fields" for layouts/fields — UI: Setup > User Management > Profiles — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/customizing-standard-modules-zoho-desk)

### 13.2 Custom modules
- **ZL-11** Create custom module — Module Name Plural*/Singular* (max 25 chars UI, 50 API), Description (200), Accessible To (default All Departments), Module Data Storage (Organization-level default / Department-level), Module Permission* (profiles); buttons Save / Save and Go to Layouts / Cancel — UI: Setup > Customization > Modules and Tabs > Manage Modules > New Module — Ed: Enterprise (10 modules) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/creating-custom-modules)
- **ZL-12** Custom module limits — 10 custom modules per org. No count shown in UI (live). Fields per module follow Enterprise caps (230; inferred) — UI: n/a — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZL-13** Auto-created fields — record name field (unique primary "name"), Layout, Owner, plus Department when dept-level storage. API name generated as cm_<plural> — UI: custom module layout — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/creating-custom-modules)
- **ZL-14** Single layout only — new layouts cannot be added to a custom module. The standard layout cannot be removed or deactivated — UI: Layouts and Fields > <custom module> — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/customization/articles/faqs-custom-modules)
- **ZL-15** Custom module tab, list and detail — tab shows plural name. List view has system views "All records"/"My records" and Select Columns. "+ Add <module>" button. Edit/Delete via record More icon with confirmation — UI: top tab bar > custom module — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/creating-custom-modules)
- **ZL-16** Record History tab — date/time, user, IP address, action (add/update), previous vs updated values — UI: custom module record > History — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/creating-custom-modules)
- **ZL-17** Related subtabs via lookups — a lookup to a standard/custom module auto-creates a subtab (e.g. "Related Tickets") with associate/view/create/disassociate. Widget locations: detail subtab and detail page — UI: record detail subtabs — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/modules/articles/creating-custom-modules)
- **ZL-18** Custom module immutability — cannot be deleted or deactivated after creation. Storage type cannot be changed. No delete API — UI: Manage Modules — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/customization/articles/faqs-custom-modules)
- **ZL-19** Custom module integration limits — workflows supported (Alerts, Custom Functions, Update Record). Import/export unsupported. No CRM sync. Help Center shows only ticket layouts. Data sharing and audit log apply. Recycle Bin 60 days — UI: various — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/customization/articles/faqs-custom-modules)
- **ZL-20** Mobile custom modules — iOS: view records, associate/dissociate, related subtabs. Creation only on web — UI: mobile More menu — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/custom-module/articles/custom-module-in-zoho-desk-ios)

### 13.6 Picklists, dependencies, nested, colour
- **ZL-60** Picklist values — sort alphabetical or user-defined, set default. Bulk add: Predefined Choices (days, months, continents, countries), Manual Entry (dupes removed), Import CSV. Export values CSV (API) — UI: Edit Field > "Add Values in Bulk" — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/working-with-custom-fields)
- **ZL-61** Replace Values — replaces a value in all existing records (modified time changes). Plain rename/add/delete does not change existing records — UI: gear > Replace Values — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/working-with-custom-fields)

Picklist only — UI: Layouts and Fields > Field Dependencies — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/understanding-fields-and-properties)

### Gaps / thin docs
- Ticket status: no limit on number of custom statuses found; no status API resource in api_ops (no Statuses/TicketStatus section); status colours not documented (colour coding explicitly excludes Status).
- Custom buttons: edition not in pricing rows and article "Availability" block was stripped; Tickets-only and "More actions" location only; no Buttons API resource found.
- Notification rules: no API resource (Notification API only covers delivery-failure alerts and pending approvals); SMS template limits not documented.
- Tabs article (customizing-help-desk-tabs) not in local S/txt; tab items rely on the earlier inventory and live capture (5 Oct 2026).
- Custom module field caps, lookup cap (community says 10) and layout column count (1 vs 2) not documented.
- Validation/layout rule regex: pattern length limit only given as 1,000 chars for criteria values (validation FAQ).
- Ticket ID configuration path is inferred; full detail belongs to the ticket-management section.
- Email template count limits per department and attachments in templates not documented (only 50 starred/dept).
- Multilingual (Languages) setup article not in S/txt; path from earlier community source.
- General Settings (idle timeout, spam detection) under Customization were not inventoried here (outside listed scope).


---

## 14. Data administration (ZX)

Edition note: Free has no import. Import batch limits per pricing are Express 1,000, Standard 10,000, Professional 20,000 and Enterprise 30,000. Export is in all editions (pricing shows Free/Express at "1000 / batch" and Standard+ as unlimited; the current article says up to 50,000 per export). Import history shows "Past 60 days" in every edition. Data backup is Standard+ ("1 full backup / month"). Sandbox (3) and Audit log are Enterprise only. Zwitch migration from other help desks is Express+ (the articles say all paid editions, and a trial is capped at 2 GB). Recycle bin (60 days) and Bulk Actions Log have no pricing row, and no article ties them to an edition.

### 14.1 Import (current UI)
- **ZX-01** Import wizard entry — select a department first, then Start Import. Steps: Upload Files > Module Mapping > Field Mapping > Review > Start import — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Import — Ed: Express+ (Exp 1,000 / Std 10,000 / Pro 20,000 / Ent 30,000 per batch) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-02** Import permission — requires the "Import Records" profile permission — UI: Setup > Profiles — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-03** Importable modules — Tickets, Accounts, Contacts, Products, Tasks, Events, Calls, custom modules, KB articles — UI: Upload Files > Select Modules — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-04** File format — CSV only. Upload a single .csv for one module, or a ZIP with one CSV per module (Tickets.csv, Contacts.csv …). CSV names must match the module name to auto-map — UI: Upload Files (drag and drop / Browse Files) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-05** File and row limits — ZIP up to 30 MB; per CSV, up to 10,000 rows or 30 MB. Conflicts with the per-edition batch limits in pricing (see Gaps) — UI: Upload Files — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-06** Supported / Unsupported Files tabs — non-CSV files (.xls, .pdf …) are listed under Unsupported Files — UI: Upload Files — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-07** Module mapping — files whose names don't match go to Unmapped Files; drag each onto its module, then Save — UI: Module Mapping — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-08** Import type per module — Create (new records only; existing ones are skipped), Overwrite (updates by identifier; rows with no match are ignored), Both (updates matches and creates the rest) — UI: Field Mapping > import type — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-09** Duplicate identifier field per module — Products: External Id / Name / Id. Accounts: External Id / Name. Contacts: External Id / Last Name / Email / Phone. Tickets, Tasks, Calls, Events: External Id. Articles: Translation External ID / Article Id. Choosing "None" means those records can never be updated by a later import — UI: Field Mapping > identifier — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-10** Multiple matches on update — only the first record returned is updated — UI: n/a (behaviour) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-11** Auto field mapping — a column maps automatically when its header equals the Desk field name; you can override it from the dropdown — UI: Field Mapping > Import fields — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-12** Mapping grid columns — Field in file (taken from row 1), Desk fields, Field format (e.g. DD/MM/YYYY HH:MM vs MM/DD/YYYY HH:MM), and Sample data from file (rows 2-3) — UI: Field Mapping — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-13** Parent/lookup keys — Product: ID or external ID. Account: ID, name or external ID. Contact: ID, external ID, email, last name or phone. Department: ID or name. Team: ID. Agent: ID or email — UI: Field Mapping — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-14** Required import order — Accounts, then Contacts, then Tickets, then other modules. In any other order related records fail or don't link (for example, tickets imported first don't create contacts) — UI: n/a (rule) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-15** Value rules — Tags as an array, e.g. [urgent,high priority,refund]. Article Status accepts only draft, publish or review. Ticket Owner must be an agent's email or Agent ID, not a name. Times are read as GMT, ignoring the user's time zone — UI: n/a (validation) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-16** Department targeting — import into one selected department, or into all departments with a Department column mapped on each record. Records with no Department mapped go to the default department — UI: department picker / Field Mapping — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-17** Review and Start import — shows a mapping summary; starting creates an import record that tracks progress — UI: Review — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-18** Import list columns — Import ID, Modules, Stage, Status (in progress / success / failed / waiting), Imported by, Imported time — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Import — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-19** Add data — reuses the original module and field mapping, which can't be changed. Only offered when the original import succeeded. Modules not in the original are ignored. A file whose fields don't match the original mapping goes to the error log — UI: import record > Add data — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-20** Revert import — deletes the records the import created, plus their mapped child records. Updates can't be reverted (Create = yes, Update = no, Both = partial). Only for imports that succeeded or completed with errors, and only within 2 weeks; after that the option is gone. Reverts only the latest Add Data batch — UI: import record > Revert — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-21** Error log — records that failed to import are listed under "Completed with error", and the list can be exported — UI: import record > Error logs — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-22** Domain-mapped portals — import needs third-party cookies enabled in the browser — UI: n/a — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-in-desk)
- **ZX-23** Import history retention: 60 days — pricing shows "Past 60 days" for every edition, including Free even though Free has no import. No article states a retention period — UI: Import list — Ed: All (pricing) — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZX-24** Import API — Postimports, Getimports, GetimportMappings — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Imports_Postimports)

### 14.2 Import (old UI, still documented)
- **ZX-25** Per-module import wizard — Select Module (Tickets, Contacts, Accounts, Products, Articles, Tasks, Calls, Events) > Browse > map fields > Confirm Mapping > Import. A completion email reports how many were added, updated and skipped — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Import — Ed: Standard+ per the article (pricing says Express+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-to-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-26** Restrictions — no import on Free. The option is hidden in All Departments view. Records go only to the default layout. The importing user becomes owner unless an owner-email column is mapped — UI: Import — Ed: n/a — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-to-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-27** File checklist — CSV or XLS, at most 5 MB. Not allowed: `"` except as a delimiter, `|` and `<>`. Checkbox values: True/1 or False/0. Picklist values must already exist. Row 1 must be headers. A blank line is read as end of file. Dates must be YYYY/MM/DD and date-times YYYY/MM/DD HH:MM:SS; other formats are ignored. Mandatory fields show in red — UI: Import wizard — Ed: n/a — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-to-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-28** Batch limits — Ent 30,000, Pro 20,000, Std 10,000 records per batch; split larger files — UI: Import — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-to-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-29** Duplicate handling — Skip, Overwrite, or Clone (Clone creates a second copy). Duplicates are matched on Contact = Email, Account = Account Name, Product = Product Name — UI: Import Data page — Ed: n/a — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-to-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-30** Ticket import "Update the ticket's account" toggle — sets each ticket's account to its contact's primary account; only non-archived tickets whose contact has an account — UI: Import Data page (Tickets) — Ed: n/a — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-to-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-31** Contact Overwrite option "Update account name in the existing tickets" — UI: Import Data page (Contacts) — Ed: n/a — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-to-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-32** Article import target — choose the KB section that receives the imported articles — UI: Import Data page (Articles) — Ed: n/a — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-to-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-33** Import History with Confirm / Undo — shows file name, module, user, date and time. Confirm makes the import permanent and can't be reverted. Undo removes the imported records; undoing contacts also deletes their linked imported accounts, and the reverse — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Import History — Ed: n/a — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/importing-data-to-zoho-desk)

### 14.3 Export
- **ZX-34** New Export — Module > records (All Records / Export Based on Views / Export Based on Criteria) > All or Specific fields > Time Frame > optional password > Export — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Export > New Export — Ed: All (Free/Express 1,000 per batch per pricing) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/exporting-data-from-zoho-desk-modules)
- **ZX-35** Export permission — requires the "Export Records" profile permission — UI: Profiles — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/exporting-data-from-zoho-desk-modules)
- **ZX-36** Modules — standard and custom modules, one module per export — UI: New Export > Module — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/exporting-data-from-zoho-desk-modules)
- **ZX-37** Export by view — system and custom views. Blueprint views can't be exported (Active Blueprint Tickets, All / My / My Team / Unassigned Transitions) — UI: New Export — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/exporting-data-from-zoho-desk-modules)
- **ZX-38** Export by criteria — multiple conditions allowed — UI: New Export — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/exporting-data-from-zoho-desk-modules)
- **ZX-39** Field selection — All fields, or Specific fields drawn from all of the module's layouts — UI: New Export — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/exporting-data-from-zoho-desk-modules)
- **ZX-40** Timeline and date range — available only with All Records. Based on Created, Closed, Modified or Completed time; predefined (report ranges) or custom range — UI: New Export > Time Frame — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/exporting-data-from-zoho-desk-modules)
- **ZX-41** Encrypt with Password — at least 8 characters, with an uppercase letter, a lowercase letter, a digit and one of !@#$%^&*. Only the user who set the password can see it — UI: New Export toggle — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/exporting-data-from-zoho-desk-modules)
- **ZX-42** Limits and rules — up to 50,000 records per export (use a backup for more). Date/time values are exported in GMT. Exports are per department, so the option is not available in All Departments. A started export can't be revoked — UI: Export — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/exporting-data-from-zoho-desk-modules)
- **ZX-43** Module-specific rules — Agents: All Records with all fields only (views and criteria disabled). Articles: views only (criteria disabled). Up to 5 lookups export both ID and name; any beyond that export ID only — UI: New Export — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/exporting-data-from-zoho-desk-modules)
- **ZX-44** Export history — columns Module, Modified by, Initiated time, Status (in progress / completed / failed), Export file. The download link is valid 7 days. Filters: module, initiated by, time, status. Completion is notified by email and in-app. Exports also appear in the audit log — UI: Export history page — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/exporting-data-from-zoho-desk-modules)
- **ZX-45** Older FAQ limits — "maximum of 3000 records" per CSV; for email threads, request a backup from support. Conflicts with ZX-42 — UI: Export — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/data-administration/articles/faq-on-data-administration)
- **ZX-46** Bulk export API — InitiateModuleExports, GetExportDetails, DownloadExportFile, FileStructure, Limitations — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#BulkExport_InitiateModuleExports)
- **ZX-47** Report export (CSV/XLS/PDF) — covered in the Analytics section — UI: Reports — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)

### 14.4 Bulk actions log
- **ZX-48** Bulk Actions Log — Initiated tab (in progress and queued) and Completed tab. Columns: module, action type, execution time, executor, view, department, status — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Bulk Actions Log > Bulk Action — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/performing-bulk-updates)
- **ZX-49** Mass Action log — mass replies, comments and IMs, with Initiated and Completed tabs; filter by department and action — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Bulk Actions Log > Mass Action — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/performing-bulk-updates)
- **ZX-50** Bulk queue rules — bulk means more than 50 records. At most 11 at a time (1 running, 10 queued) and one per view. A running bulk action can't be discarded. Each scheduler run handles up to 200,000 records. The log shows failure reasons (scheduler disabled, view modified, server error). The initiator and admin get an email — UI: Bulk Actions Log — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/actions-in-tickets/articles/performing-bulk-updates)
- **ZX-51** No bulk actions on archived tickets — handle them one at a time. Blueprint views allow mass actions only, not bulk — UI: ticket list — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/data-administration/articles/faq-on-data-administration)

### 14.5 Data backup
- **ZX-52** Data Backup page — Back up now (Full or Custom), Schedule backup, Backup History, Logs — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Data Backup — Ed: Standard+ ("1 full backup / month") — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/backup-and-data-archive/articles/backing-up-your-help-desk-data)
- **ZX-53** Who can — only the primary contact can create and download backups — UI: Data Backup — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/backup-and-data-archive/articles/backing-up-your-help-desk-data)
- **ZX-54** Contents — Tickets (threads, comments, time entries, attachments as secure links fetched by API), Contacts, Accounts, Activities (tasks, calls, events), Products, KB articles, Agents, Teams, Departments. Forum/Community data is not backed up. Delivered as one ZIP of CSV files — UI: n/a — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/backup-and-data-archive/articles/backing-up-your-help-desk-data)
- **ZX-55** Full backup — no further full backup for 30 days after one starts — UI: Back up now > Full Backup > Download Now — Ed: Standard+ (pricing: 1 full per month) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/backup-and-data-archive/articles/backing-up-your-help-desk-data)
- **ZX-56** Custom backup — covers records modified in a chosen range (Date modified after / until), at most 31 days; a longer range gives an error. Several custom backups are allowed within 30 days (the FAQ disagrees) — UI: Back up now > Custom Backup — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/backup-and-data-archive/articles/backing-up-your-help-desk-data)
- **ZX-57** Scheduled backup — Repeat Type: Weekly or Bi-weekly (pick a weekday), or Every Month or Quarterly (pick a date or an nth weekday), plus an end date. The first run is full and later runs are incremental. Only one scheduled backup per month — UI: Data Backup > Schedule backup > Create Backup Schedule — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/backup-and-data-archive/articles/backing-up-your-help-desk-data)
- **ZX-58** Backup encryption — Encrypt with Password, same rules as export — UI: Back up now / Schedule — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/backup-and-data-archive/articles/backing-up-your-help-desk-data)
- **ZX-59** Backup History and download window — shows a download icon, file size and days left. Backups expire and are removed 7 days after the backup date. You can also delete a backup — UI: Data Backup > Backup History — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/backup-and-data-archive/articles/backing-up-your-help-desk-data)
- **ZX-60** Stop a scheduled backup — Next Backup Schedule > Stop Backup, then confirm. A backup that is already running can't be stopped — UI: Data Backup — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/backup-and-data-archive/articles/backing-up-your-help-desk-data)
- **ZX-61** Backup Logs — Configuration, Backup Scheduled, Backup Initiated (date and time), Backup is ready to download, Download initiated, scheduled backup stopped — UI: Data Backup > Logs — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/backup-and-data-archive/articles/backing-up-your-help-desk-data)
- **ZX-62** Volume cap — 10 million records; past that, support contacts you — UI: n/a — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/backup-and-data-archive/articles/backing-up-your-help-desk-data)

### 14.6 Data archive
- **ZX-63** Automatic ticket archiving — tickets in a closed state with no activity for 120 days are archived. You can't archive manually or unarchive in bulk. Tickets with open activities or pending approvals are skipped. A new conversation or any property change unarchives a ticket — UI: n/a (system) — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)

- **ZX-65** Archived custom views — a view not opened for 60 days is archived automatically. It is deleted after 30 more days unless opened, which restores it — UI: All Views > ARCHIVED > Archived View — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/views-and-filters/articles/ticket-views-custom-list-archived-views)
