### 14.9 Sandbox
- **ZX-83** Build New Sandbox — Name, Description, and Set Permissions (Agents or Teams), then Create. Users who are granted access get an email with the access URL — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Sandbox > Build New Sandbox — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/sandbox/articles/working-with-sandbox-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-84** Sandbox count — up to 3 per portal; Build New Sandbox is disabled at 3. Confirmed: pricing also says "3" — UI: Sandbox — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZX-85** Permission — deploying needs the "Manage Sandbox" permission. Only the agents and teams granted at creation can use the sandbox — UI: Profiles / Sandbox — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/sandbox/articles/working-with-sandbox-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-86** Operations run one at a time — only one Build, Rebuild or Deploy can run across all sandboxes, and each sandbox is isolated from the others — UI: Sandbox — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/sandbox/articles/working-with-sandbox-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-87** What gets copied — all Setup configuration, departments, profiles and roles. Not copied: module records (add or import them, or use sample data), company details, channels (email, social, chat, community, phone, ASAP, web forms), Zia, Guided Conversation flows, Marketplace extensions and integrations. Default address is `username@sandbox.zohosupport.com`. Only the default help center. A "Sandbox mode" banner shows — UI: sandbox portal — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/sandbox/articles/working-with-sandbox-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-88** User mapping and restrictions — a user keeps their production role and profile if they exist in the sandbox; otherwise they get CEO / Support Administrator (from 2 Jan 2026). In a sandbox you can't add or disable agents or take a data backup. Workflow notifications go only to the primary recipient — UI: sandbox portal — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/sandbox/articles/working-with-sandbox-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-89** Change Set and deploy — select changes or Deploy All Changes to Production. Changes are validated as Qualified or Conflict; use Proceed Qualified, or View Details for conflicts. Deployable: layouts, layout rules, validation rules, field dependencies, fields, custom-module customization and automation, time tracking, module renames, templates, assignment rules, notification rules, workflows, skills, blueprint, macros, supervise, schedules. Status-to-state mapping is not deployed. One deployment at a time — UI: Sandbox > Change Set — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/sandbox/articles/working-with-sandbox-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-90** Conflict reasons — duplicate name, record deleted in live, record updated in both, a limit exceeded (e.g. field count), duplicate module — UI: Change Set > View Details — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/sandbox/articles/working-with-sandbox-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-91** Deployment Logs — listed in the production account and also recorded in the Audit Log — UI: production > Sandbox > Deployment Logs — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/sandbox/articles/working-with-sandbox-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-92** Rebuild (refresh) — pulls the latest production configuration and discards sandbox changes; it also deletes records previously copied from live — UI: Sandbox > Rebuild — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/sandbox/articles/working-with-sandbox-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZX-93** Sandbox API — CreateSandbox, GetSandbox(es), UpdateSandbox, RebuildSandbox, GetSandboxChangesets(Count), ValidateSandboxChangesets, DeploySandboxChangesets, GetSandboxDeploymentLogs — UI: API — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Sandbox_CreateSandbox)

### 14.10 Data migration (Zwitch)
- **ZX-94** New Migration — Basic Information (Migration From, Service URL, credentials or API key) > upload under Import Data From > target modules > Additional Info > Submit and Authenticate > choose the Desk portal > Accept or Reject — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Zwitch > New Migration — Ed: Express+ (all paid editions; a trial migrates at most 2 GB) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/data-migration/articles/migrate-data-from-other-help-desk-zwitch)
- **ZX-95** Who can — Support Administrator profile, or the primary contact — UI: Zwitch — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/data-migration/articles/migrate-data-from-other-help-desk-zwitch)
- **ZX-96** Sources and formats — Freshdesk: XML/JSON, URL + API key. Zendesk: JSON, URL + email + API token. Salesforce: CSV. Kayako: SQL. Intercom: CSV + access token. HappyFox: API + attachments ZIP. Help Scout: API (key, App ID/Secret). Others: CSV, other formats reviewed — UI: New Migration > Migration From — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/data-migration/articles/migrate-data-from-other-help-desk-zwitch)
- **ZX-97** Upload limits — 10 GB total, 2 GB per file, up to 5 files, one CSV per module; above 10 GB, contact support. The Freshdesk article says each ZIP must be under 5 GB — UI: Import Data From — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/data-migration/articles/migrate-data-from-other-help-desk-zwitch)
- **ZX-98** Target modules, in migration order — Agents, Accounts, Contacts, Products, Tickets (Threads, Comments and Attachments are included automatically), KB Articles (without attachments) — UI: New Migration > Modules — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/data-migration/articles/migrate-data-from-other-help-desk-zwitch)
- **ZX-99** Sample (trial) migration and two phases — a sample migration is recommended first. Phase 1 is the bulk move: a support ticket is opened and a report plus error-log ZIP is emailed. Phase 2 runs about 2 weeks later for residual records and fixes; nothing is accepted after Phase 2. You have 2 weeks to reply to the error log. Expect 3-5% failures, returned as CSV. ETA depends on the queue — UI: email / support ticket — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/data-migration/articles/migrate-data-from-other-help-desk-zwitch)
- **ZX-100** Migration History statuses — Waiting for Authentication (can be re-initiated), Submitted in Queue (the only status that can be cancelled), Completed, Canceled — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Zwitch — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/data-migration/articles/migrate-data-from-other-help-desk-zwitch)
- **ZX-101** Prerequisites — keep the source API key and account active with enough API rate limit. Turn off Zoho CRM/Salesforce integrations and IP restrictions. Agents must already exist, be active and use the same email; otherwise their tickets go to the Primary Support Administrator or stay unassigned — UI: n/a — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/data-migration/articles/migrate-data-from-freshdesk)
- **ZX-102** Freshdesk specifics — Companies become Accounts. Users become Agents, Contacts and EndUsers. Private notes become comments, public notes become threads. Solutions become KB categories, sections and articles. Can filter by group or product. The FreshDesk-Id (display-id) is kept. Custom fields are created with types inferred from the data. Threads with no channel become CUSTOMERPORTAL. Everything lands in one department. Takes at least 2 weeks — UI: Zwitch — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/data-migration/articles/migrate-data-from-freshdesk)
- **ZX-103** Zendesk specifics — upload three ZIPs (Tickets, Users, Organizations); KB comes over via API. Can filter by group or brand. The Zendesk-ID field is kept. Not migrated: tags, groups, teams, collaborators, inline images, nested custom fields. All replies arrive as "Incoming". Tickets with no assignee go to the submitting admin. Migrated agents appear on the Imported tab with no invite needed. Light agents get no tickets — UI: Zwitch — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/data-migration/articles/zwitch-data-migration-export-from-zendesk)
- **ZX-104** Markers on migrated records — "Old Ticket ID" field, "Mig Closed" status, "Is Migrated" checkbox on contacts and accounts, "ZohoDesk Migrated" tag on articles. Custom fields land in Unused Fields. Custom field labels are capped at 50 characters, and other characters are replaced with _ — UI: records / Layouts and Fields — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/data-administration/articles/zwitch-frequently-asked-questions-and-answers)
- **ZX-105** Data retention — uploaded backups and parsed files are purged within 30 days of migration confirmation — UI: n/a — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/data-migration/articles/migrate-data-from-freshdesk)
- **ZX-106** Portal-to-portal migration — take a Full or Custom Backup in the source portal. In the destination: New Migration > Internal Portal > source portal (only those where you are admin) > modules > upload > Submit and Authenticate. Needs Support Administrator in both portals — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Zwitch > New Migration — Ed: Express+ (inferred; the backup step needs Standard+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/data-migration/articles/migrating-data-between-zoho-desk-portals)
- **ZX-107** Assisted migration file specs — files named Agents_XX, Accounts_XX, Contacts_XX, Tickets_XX, Threads_XX, TicketComments_XX, Products_XX, Tasks_XX (.csv), each with a required ExtId. Times in GMT (YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS.000Z). Closed time can't be before created time. Missing ExtIds are generated by hashing. If a parent fails, its children are skipped. Multiple departments are possible via a department column. Residual import requests within 2 weeks. The import can be undone. Files are kept 30 days — UI: offline (migration team) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/import-export/articles/assisted-data-migration-guide)

### 14.11 Cross-references
- **ZX-108** Data Subject Requests (GDPR export/erase) — New Request with type Export Data or Delete Data; choose module and contact or reference ticket; Specific or All records; Fetch Data, then Submit; CSV download. Covered in the Security/Privacy section — UI: Setup > Privacy and Security > Data Subject Requests (the FAQ says Data Administration > Privacy Settings) — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/compliance/articles/addressing-data-subject-requests)
- **ZX-109** Attachment controls and other security settings under Data Administration > Security Settings — covered in the Security section — UI: Setup > Data Administration > Security Settings — Ed: see Security — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/security-settings/articles/working-with-attachments-control)

### Gaps / thin docs
- Import limits conflict. The current article says a 30 MB ZIP and 10,000 rows per CSV. Pricing says Exp 1,000 / Std 10,000 / Pro 20,000 / Ent 30,000 per batch. The old UI says 5 MB. Verify on a live account.
- Import history "Past 60 days" appears only on the pricing page (all editions, including Free, which has no import). No article confirms it, and revert is limited to 2 weeks.
- Export limit conflict: 50,000 per export (article) vs 3,000 (FAQ) vs 1,000 per batch on Free/Express (pricing). The permission is named "Export Records" in the article but "Export Data" in the FAQ.
- Backup edition: pricing says Standard+, while the FAQ says "all paid editions" and still calls the feature Beta.
- Backup rules conflict. The article allows multiple custom backups within 30 days; the FAQ blocks all backups for 30 days after a full or custom one. "One scheduled backup per month" vs "per 30 days". No restore-from-backup is documented (download only).
- Recycle bin has no edition data, and who can permanently delete is unclear (Recycle Bin permission vs Support Administrator).
- Audit log has no API resource in api_ops.txt. It sits under Privacy and Security, not Data Administration. The pricing tooltip mentions only automation and custom modules, while the article lists 38 entities.
- Data archive has no Data Administration article. Only automatic ticket archiving (120 days, not configurable per the docs) and view archiving are documented.
- Bulk Actions Log is documented only in the ticket bulk-updates article and the FAQ; no edition or retention period is given.
- Zwitch department targeting conflicts: the FAQ says one department only, while the Freshdesk key-facts table and the assisted-migration guide say multi-department is supported.
- "Trial vs full migration" is documented only as a recommendation to run a sample migration plus the 2 GB trial-edition cap. There is no Zwitch API.
- Sandbox storage limits, any limits on rebuild frequency, and whether sandbox records count against portal limits are not documented.


---

## 15. Security, privacy and compliance (ZE)

Edition note: IP range restriction (via Zoho Directory), CSP header support, DKIM/DMARC, anonymisation of agents/customers and data-centre choice are in all editions (Free included). SSL for mapped domains, attachment controls and Data Subject Requests are Express+. Help-center SAML, MFA, OIDC, federated login and ASAP JWT are Standard+. ePHI field labelling is Professional+. Custom-field encryption (10 per module) and system/standard-field encryption (Contacts only, 20 fields) are Enterprise only, as are the Audit log and help-center SMS-OTP sign-in. Encryption at rest and in transit applies to every account and has no switch.

### 15.1 Access control: IP restriction and agent sign-on
- **ZE-01** IP range restriction for agent login — add a single static IP or an IP range under Allowed IPs. After that, every login from any other IP is blocked. It is set up in Zoho Directory, not in Desk Setup — UI: directory.zoho.com > Admin Panel > Security > Security Policies > Default Policy > Allowed IPs > Add IP address — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/configuring-active-directory-sso-for-zoho-desk-agents)
- **ZE-02** Default vs granular security policy — Allowed IPs set on the Default Policy apply to all users in every Zoho app. To limit them to one group of users, add a new security policy for that group — UI: Zoho Directory > Admin Panel > Security > Security Policies — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/configuring-active-directory-sso-for-zoho-desk-agents)
- **ZE-03** Pricing row "IP range restriction (active directory)" — lets you allow-list the IPs used to reach Zoho services and denies access from other networks — UI: (see ZE-01) — Ed: All — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZE-04** Knowledge-base category visibility "Custom IP" — the category is visible only to registered users signed in from the listed IP addresses. Several IPs are allowed. Static IPs are recommended, and every IP in a network must be allow-listed (e.g. 192.158.*.*). Sections inherit the category's visibility unless the category is None. If visibility changes later, article display falls back to "Registered users" — UI: KB > Category/Section > Visibility: None / Groups / Public / Agents / Custom IP > IP Addresses field — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/knowledge-base/setting-up-and-permission/articles/organizing-your-knowledge-base-content-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZE-05** Active Directory SSO for agents through Zoho Directory Sync — syncs AD users and passwords into Zoho accounts so agents sign in with their corporate credentials. Steps: Zoho Directory > Admin Panel > Applications > Add Application > Zoho Desk > pick the account > Yes, Add — UI: directory.zoho.com (outside Desk) — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/configuring-active-directory-sso-for-zoho-desk-agents)
- **ZE-06** Agent provisioning from Zoho Directory — once AD users are synced, you add and manage agents from inside Zoho Directory — UI: Zoho Directory — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/configuring-active-directory-sso-for-zoho-desk-agents)
- **ZE-07** SAML SSO for agents (Okta, Azure AD etc.) — not documented in the Desk KB. The Desk SAML page applies only to customers ("does not apply to agents"). Agent SAML is assumed to be configured at the Zoho Accounts/Directory level (inferred) — UI: n/d — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/setting-up-saml-single-signon-for-help-center)
- **ZE-08** Two-factor authentication / MFA for agents — no Desk article. Agents sign in with Zoho Accounts, so MFA (OneAuth, TOTP, SMS) is assumed to come from Zoho Accounts / Directory security policies (inferred) — UI: accounts.zoho.com / Zoho Directory (inferred) — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/configuring-active-directory-sso-for-zoho-desk-agents)
- **ZE-09** Role-based security model — Roles (hierarchy), Teams, Profiles (module- and feature-level permissions), Data Sharing Rules and Field Level Security. Needs Support administrator + Administrative permission — UI: Setup > User Management > Roles / Teams / Profiles / Data Sharing; Setup > Customization > Layouts and Fields > Field Permissions — Ed: n/d (see Users & permissions area) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/securing-your-help-desk)
- **ZE-10** Profile permission "Manage Security Settings" — needed to open Attachment controls — UI: Setup > User Management > Profiles > Administrative Permissions — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/security-settings/articles/working-with-attachments-control)
- **ZE-11** Profile permission "Manage Privacy Settings" — grants access to process Data Subject Requests and to enable read receipts. On for Administrator by default; off for the Agent profile until an admin turns it on — UI: Setup > User Management > Profiles > Agent > Administrative Permissions — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)

### 15.2 Help-center (end-user) authentication
- **ZE-12** User Authentication page per help center — tabs for SAML, JWT, OIDC, MFA and SMS-based authentication, set separately for each help center (multi-brand) — UI: Setup > Channels > Help Center > [select help center] > User Authentication — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/setting-up-saml-single-signon-for-help-center)
- **ZE-13** SAML 2.0 SSO for the help center — fields: Remote Login URL, Remote Logout URL, Reset Password URL, Public Key (upload the X.509 certificate as a .txt file) and Algorithm (RSA SHA-256 is the only option). Needs Support Administrator — UI: Help Center > User Authentication > SAML — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/setting-up-saml-single-signon-for-help-center)
- **ZE-14** SAML service-provider values shown before Save — Help Center SAML Request URL, SAML Response URL (ACS), Entity ID (Issuer, editable to an alternative if the IdP rejects duplicates), and a Redirect URL for Microsoft Azure. You copy these into the IdP — UI: SAML page — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/setting-up-saml-single-signon-for-help-center)
- **ZE-15** SAML "Enable Signup" checkbox — lets new IdP users sign in to the help center for the first time — UI: SAML page — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/setting-up-saml-single-signon-for-help-center)
- **ZE-16** SAML rules — customers only (not agents). Remote authentication and SAML cannot be on at the same time. With SAML on, customers cannot self-sign-up or change their password in the help center — UI: SAML page — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/setting-up-saml-single-signon-for-help-center)
- **ZE-17** Disable SAML — Disable button at the top right. Users who had a password before SAML can use it again; users who signed up under SAML must reset their password — UI: SAML page > Disable — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/setting-up-saml-single-signon-for-help-center)
- **ZE-18** Documented SAML IdP recipes — Zoho Vault, Okta, OneLogin, Auth0 (.pem cert, given_name/family_name mapping), Google Workspace (logout https://accounts.google.com/logout), Microsoft Azure AD (user.mail claim, Sign SAML response, zoho.eu/zoho.in identifiers), Keycloak (Valid Redirect URIs accounts.zohoportal.com/* and desk.zoho.com/*) — UI: n/a (IdP side) — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/setting-up-saml-single-signon-for-help-center)
- **ZE-19** ADFS with SAML for the help center — Relying Party Trust with identifier zoho.com / zoho.eu / zoho.in / zoho.au by DC. Claim rules: LDAP E-Mail to Email, then transform to Name ID (Email). SHA-256 hash. SAML Logout endpoint ?wa=wsignout1.0 (POST). Export the base-64 X.509 .cer and save it as .txt. Needs Administrative permission — UI: Help Center > User Authentication > SAML — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/configuring-adfs-for-zoho-desk-with-saml)
- **ZE-20** JWT authentication for the help center — enter Remote Login URL and Remote Logout URL. Desk then generates the JWT Secret Key and the Sign-in End Point URL automatically — UI: Help Center > User Authentication > JWT — Ed: Standard+ (inferred from the help-center SSO rows) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/jwt-authentication-mechanism-for-help-center)
- **ZE-21** JWT token contract — HS256 signature. Payload claims: email, nbf, exp, jti (jti blocks token reuse until exp). Desk sends ?serviceurl= to the IdP, and the IdP returns to the sign-in endpoint with return_to=&jwt= — UI: n/a (IdP side) — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/jwt-authentication-mechanism-for-help-center)
- **ZE-22** Delete JWT authentication — Delete (bottom right) > Continue. A new secret is generated if JWT is configured again. Users then go back to self-sign-up/password, and users who joined under JWT must reset their password — UI: JWT page > Delete — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/jwt-authentication-mechanism-for-help-center)
- **ZE-23** Guest vs authenticated users — guests can read the KB, submit tickets and view community posts. Authenticated users can also track and view their tickets, view other tickets in their account, and follow, post and comment — UI: help center — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/jwt-authentication-mechanism-for-help-center)
- **ZE-24** OpenID Connect (OIDC) for the help center — Enable, then fill Provider Name, Client ID, Client Secret, Authorization URL, Access Token URL, User Info URL and Scopes, then Save. Needs Support administrator — UI: Help Center > User Authentication > OIDC > Enable — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/configuring-openid-connect-oidc-for-help-center)
- **ZE-25** Federated login for the help center — IdPs: Zoho, Google, LinkedIn, Facebook, Microsoft Azure. Each needs a Client ID and Client Secret (Zoho needs neither, only Terms + Enable, and creates an OAuth client in the Zoho Developer Console). The page shows the Authorized domain and Redirect URI to paste into the IdP — UI: Setup > Channels > Help Center > Federated Login — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/enabling-federated-login-for-help-center)
- **ZE-26** Federated login rules — no new-sign-up moderation for federated users, and they need no invite. Conditions are shown only when customer self-sign-up is enabled. Needs Help Center permission — UI: Federated Login — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/enabling-federated-login-for-help-center)
- **ZE-27** Disable a federated IdP — hover the provider > Disable > confirm — UI: Federated Login page — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/enabling-federated-login-for-help-center)
- **ZE-28** MFA for the help center — Enable MFA after accepting the Terms of Use. Works with TOTP authenticator apps (Zoho OneAuth, Google, Microsoft, Duo). Needs Support administrator — UI: Help Center > User Authentication > MFA > Enable MFA — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/multi-factor-authentication-mfa-for-help-center)
- **ZE-29** MFA lifetime — Ask every time / 180 days (default) / Custom (up to 180 days) — UI: MFA tab > Lifetime of MFA — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/multi-factor-authentication-mfa-for-help-center)
- **ZE-30** End-user MFA enrolment — sign in > OTP Authenticator > Configure > scan the QR code or enter the key > Verify > Enable MFA > Generate backup codes > Download > Continue — UI: help-center sign-in — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/multi-factor-authentication-mfa-for-help-center)
- **ZE-31** Admin "Reset MFA" for an end user — sends the user an email, and the user must set up MFA again. Also available in the API (Users_ResetMFAforPortalUser) — UI: Help Center > Users > hover > Edit > Reset MFA > Reset now — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/multi-factor-authentication-mfa-for-help-center)
- **ZE-32** Disable MFA — toggle it off. Users who already enrolled are still prompted, and new users get no MFA option — UI: MFA tab toggle — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/multi-factor-authentication-mfa-for-help-center)
- **ZE-33** SMS OTP sign-in for the help center — passwordless login: mobile number, then OTP. Also sends sign-up invitations by SMS. Indian numbers only. Needs DLT sender registration, TRAI compliance, Zoho added as telemarketer aggregator (ID 1102631350000082951) and purchased credits. Needs Help Center permission — UI: Setup > Channels > Help Center > User Authentication > SMS Based Authentication > Enable — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/help-center-auth-sms-otp)
- **ZE-34** SMS OTP setup wizard — Company Info (legal name, brand, city, state, postal code, country). Sender ID Details (Sender name, PEID 19-digit, Entity name, Sender unique ID, status, Registered by). Submit DLT files (verification + invitation templates, then map, then DLT approval) — UI: SMS Based Authentication — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/help-center-auth-sms-otp)
- **ZE-35** SMS credits — balance and usage analysis (weekly/monthly/quarterly/custom) on the Credit Details tab, plus Buy Credits. $1 = 1 credit = 500 SMS (INR 0.0020 credit per SMS). Admin gets an email when credits run out — UI: SMS Based Authentication > Credit Details > Buy Credits — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/help-center-auth-sms-otp)
- **ZE-36** SMS OTP rules — only for single help centers or a unique user base. Turning it on for one help center turns it on for all of them, and SAML, JWT and federated login are then ignored. Disabling deletes brand, sender and template data but keeps credits — UI: SMS Based Authentication > Disable — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/help-center-auth-sms-otp)
- **ZE-37** Multilingual CAPTCHA for unregistered ticket submitters — "Show captcha for unregistered users in the add Tickets form" (on by default). Image CAPTCHA in 52 languages. Audio CAPTCHA (arithmetic) in 6 languages, falling back to English — UI: Help Center > Access Settings — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/secure-help-center-access-with-multilingual-captcha)

### 15.3 ASAP widget security (CSP, trusted domains, JWT)
- **ZE-38** Content Security Policy support — on sites whose CSP has script-src, generate a nonce, add 'nonce-…' to the header, and set the same nonce attribute on the ASAP script tag. ASAP passes the nonce on to the scripts it injects — UI: ASAP setup snippet (code change on the host site) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/asap/general/articles/embedding-the-asap-add-on-on-sites-with-a-content-security-policy)
- **ZE-39** Pricing row "CSP policy header" — prevents client-side execution of malicious scripts — UI: n/d — Ed: All — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZE-40** ASAP "Secure your help widget" — authentication method Anonymous or JWT, set separately for Web, iOS and Android. With JWT you list allowed domains (max 5, comma-separated), and the widget appears only on those domains — UI: ASAP widget > Secure your help widget tab > Update — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/self-service/asap/articles/how-to-enable-security-and-privacy-settings-on-the-asap-help-widget)
- **ZE-41** ASAP enhanced JWT — the JWT secret is shown once when the add-on is created. HS256 signature. Required claims: email, email_verified, not_before, not_after (UTC in ms). Validity window ≤ 600000 ms (~10 min). The web ASAP access token expires after 1 hour. The token is returned as a plain string from a JWT callback — UI: ASAP setup > JWT — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/asap/general/articles/understanding-the-enhanced-jwt-mechanism-for-authenticating-users-in-the-asap-help-widget)
- **ZE-42** ASAP JWT error debugging — JWT Configuration Error screens (web, Android, iOS). Checks: JWT enabled, host domain matches, correct secret, token format (three dot-separated parts). nbf/iat in seconds; not_before/not_after/exp in ms. The endpoint is called from Zoho IAM, not from the browser — UI: ASAP add-on error — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/asap/general/articles/debugging-jwt-related-errors-while-configuring-the-asap-add-on)

### 15.4 Email authentication (SPF / DKIM / DMARC)
- **ZE-43** DKIM Authentication page — lists From-address domains with a Verify button. The pop-up shows Selector and TXT record values with Copy buttons. Status becomes "Verified" — UI: Setup > Channels > Email > DKIM Authentication — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/configuring-spf-and-dkim-for-your-email-domain)
- **ZE-44** SPF record values — copied from the same pop-up and published in the domain's DNS — UI: DKIM Authentication > Verify — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/configuring-spf-and-dkim-for-your-email-domain)
- **ZE-45** Prerequisite: a verified From address — New From Address (Department, From Address, Friendly Name), then a verification email — UI: Setup > Channels > Email > From Address > New From Address — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/configuring-spf-and-dkim-for-your-email-domain)
- **ZE-46** DKIM/SPF rules — not available for From addresses that send through your own SMTP. DNS changes take 24-48 h. Desk re-checks the DKIM TXT record every two days. Needs Support Administrator — UI: DKIM Authentication — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/email/articles/configuring-spf-and-dkim-for-your-email-domain)
- **ZE-47** DMARC — named only in the pricing row "DKIM/DMARC". No Desk article covers DMARC setup — UI: n/d — Ed: All — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)

### 15.5 Transport security, SSL and mapped domains
- **ZE-48** SSL certificate for a mapped support domain — after the CNAME (desk.cs.zohohost.com / .eu / .in) and ownership verification, click GET SSL. Zoho installs a certificate free within up to 3 business days. Only one domain unless multi-branding is on. Domain length ≤ 46 characters — UI: Setup > Rebranding > Domain Mapping wizard > Get SSL — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/organization-settings/rebranding/articles/support-domain-mapping)
- **ZE-49** No custom certificates — wildcard or third-party SSL certificates cannot be installed. Zoho uses Group SSL certificates, so other portals' domains show in the certificate. Disable Cloudflare proxying until the CNAME is verified — UI: Domain Mapping — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/security/articles/faqs-security)
- **ZE-50** Minimum TLS 1.2 — TLS 1.0/1.1 disabled on all UI endpoints since 15 May 2019. Applies to Help Centers, web-to-ticket forms, feedback widgets and ASAP; older browsers get an error screen. Minimum browsers: Chrome 49, Firefox 44, Safari 9, Opera 45, Edge — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/security/articles/faqs-security)
- **ZE-51** TLS needs for API clients and PhoneBridge — old Java/OpenSSL/.NET versions must be upgraded to call the API, and the PhoneBridge adapter needs Java 8 — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/security/articles/faqs-security)

### 15.6 Encryption (platform, field level, mobile)
- **ZE-52** Encryption in transit — TLS 1.2 for transfers and backups, with AES_CBC/AES_GCM 256/128-bit ciphers — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/encryption-at-zoho-desk)
- **ZE-53** Encryption at rest — application-layer AES-256. A DEK is wrapped by a KEK, and keys sit in Zoho's in-house KMS. Encrypted by default: SMTP passwords, auth tokens, ticket conversations, attachments — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/encryption-at-zoho-desk)
- **ZE-54** Full-disk encryption — in the EU, IN, AU and JP data centres (not stated for US) — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/encryption-at-zoho-desk)
- **ZE-55** Custom-field encryption toggle — "Encrypt field" in the Edit Field panel, on new or existing custom fields. Applies org-wide across departments. Toggle it off to decrypt. Needs Support administrator — UI: Setup > Customization > Layouts and Fields > module + dept > layout > field gear > Edit Properties > Encrypt field > Update > Save layout — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/encrypting-custom-help-desk-fields)
- **ZE-56** Custom-field encryption limits — max 10 encrypted custom fields per module. Types: single line, integer, email, phone, percent, decimal, date, picklist, currency, URL — UI: Edit Field panel — Ed: Enterprise (10/module) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/encrypting-custom-help-desk-fields)
- **ZE-57** Reference check before encrypting — encryption turns the value into text, so you must first remove the field from custom views, reports and automation. Desk shows a prompt listing every reference — UI: Edit Field panel prompt — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/encrypting-custom-help-desk-fields)
- **ZE-58** Background encrypt/decrypt job — a scheduler processes the records, and its duration depends on record count. Progress is sent by email — UI: email notifications — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/encrypting-custom-help-desk-fields)
- **ZE-59** Encrypted-field restrictions — not allowed as a report column, criteria, column to total or grouping. Not allowed as an automation-rule condition. Not usable in Advanced Filters, Search by Criteria or Sort. Search matches whole words only. Works in APIs and as custom-function input, and in all integrations except Zoho Analytics — UI: Reports / Rules / Filters / Search — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/encrypting-custom-help-desk-fields)
- **ZE-60** Standard (system) field encryption — Contacts module only, up to 20 standard fields, all departments, same 10 field types. Phased rollout on request (registration link). Cannot be decrypted. Lookups and Global Search need the full value — UI: Layouts and Fields > Contacts > layout > standard field > Field Settings > Edit Properties > Encrypt field — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/encrypting-standard-fields-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZE-61** Encryption vs field permissions — encryption does not change who can see a field. Pair it with Field Permissions (Read and Write / Read Only / Don't Show per profile). Mandatory fields cannot be Read Only or Don't Show. Light Agents cannot get Read and Write. Preview the layout per profile before saving — UI: Setup > Customization > Layouts and Fields > Fields Permissions — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/customization/articles/faqs-encrypting-standard-fields)
- **ZE-62** Modules that can hold custom fields (encryption candidates) — Contracts, Time Entry, Agents, Tickets, Contacts, Accounts, Products, Calls, Tasks, Events. Custom fields are department-specific — UI: Layouts and Fields > Fields List (name, data type, API name, layouts) — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/customization/articles/faqs-encrypting-custom-fields)
- **ZE-63** Mobile app database encryption (AES) — UI: Android/iOS app > Settings > Privacy and Security > Enable Encryption — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/enabling-encryption-in-android)
- **ZE-64** Mobile file encryption — attachments are encrypted once downloaded and decrypted only for temporary viewing. Files opened in third-party apps show as an unsupported format — UI: mobile app — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/enabling-encryption-in-android)
- **ZE-65** Mobile app lock — 4- or 6-digit PIN, biometric unlock (only after a PIN is set), Change PIN, "Ask for PIN" timing, hide content in recent apps, diagnostics/crash reports (email or anonymous) — UI: app > Settings > Privacy and Security > Enable App Lock — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/enabling-privacy-and-security-in-android)

### 15.7 Attachment controls
- **ZE-66** Attachment control modes — (1) allow all file types except system-restricted ones, (2) allow only specified file types, (3) restrict more file types (choose from a recommended list of commonly restricted types). Needs Manage Security Settings — UI: Setup > Privacy and Security > Attachment controls > Update — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/security-settings/articles/working-with-attachments-control)
- **ZE-67** Attachment control scope — Tickets module only. Applies to agent replies (e.g. blocking .exe) and to help-center uploads; guests and registered users get an error for restricted types. Does not apply to Social, Chat or IM attachments: once converted to a ticket the file cannot be opened in the ticket, but can still be opened in the Social module. Files uploaded before the setting are not affected — UI: ticket attachments, help-center ticket form — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/security-settings/articles/working-with-attachments-control)
- **ZE-68** Attachment preview and annotator — preview before attaching, then edit with shapes, highlight, arrows, text, blur and crop. Edit works only for files from Browse files (jpeg, jpg, png, bmp, svg, ico). Cloud sources: WorkDrive, Google Drive, Dropbox, Box, OneDrive, Evernote — UI: ticket > Attachment tab > Upload Attachment page > Edit > Done > Attach — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/data-administration/security-settings/articles/working-with-attachments-control)

### 15.8 Compliance: HIPAA / ePHI
- **ZE-69** Mark field as ePHI — adds an ePHI label beside the field in the agent UI and in the help center. Turns on Encrypt field automatically (can be turned off manually; not recommended) — UI: Setup > Customization > Layouts and Fields > layout > field gear > Edit Properties > Mark as ePHI > Update — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/compliance/articles/hipaa-compliance-guide)
- **ZE-70** ePHI toggle when creating a field — "Mark as ePHI" is among the new-field properties — UI: layout editor > new field — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/customization/layouts-and-fields/articles/working-with-custom-fields)
- **ZE-71** HIPAA BAA — request the Business Associate Agreement template from legal@zohocorp.com — UI: n/a (offline) — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/compliance/articles/hipaa-compliance-guide)
- **ZE-72** HIPAA audit trail — add/update/delete audit logs kept in the back end for up to 1 year and shared on request; module export suggested as a periodic alternative (see the Audit log area) — UI: n/a / Setup > Data Administration > Audit Log — Ed: Enterprise (Audit log row) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/compliance/articles/hipaa-compliance-guide)

### 15.9 GDPR, data subject requests and anonymisation
- **ZE-73** Data Subject Requests list — history of every DSR with its status, plus a Filter DSR Requests icon. Requests are not department-specific — UI: Setup > Privacy and Security > Data Subject Requests — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/compliance/articles/addressing-data-subject-requests)
- **ZE-74** DSR "Export Data" (right of access) — New Request > Request type Export Data > Module + Contact fields > optional Reference Ticket Id > Record Details: Specific Records (criteria) or All Records (by contact email) > Fetch Data > select > Submit. Download the CSV from the request entry — UI: Data Subject Requests > New Request — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/compliance/articles/addressing-data-subject-requests)
- **ZE-75** DSR "Delete Data" (right to erasure) — same flow, then Submit > Delete to confirm. Deletion is permanent, cannot be recovered and runs "after a while" — UI: Data Subject Requests > New Request — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/compliance/articles/addressing-data-subject-requests)
- **ZE-76** DSR API — list/get requests, export all or specific subject data, erase all or specific resources, list resources related to a subject, get count of resources, get fields and conditions — UI: API — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#SubjectAccessRequests_Listsubjectaccessrequests)
- **ZE-77** Anonymise an agent — only for deleted agents (from the Deleted Agents view via the Anonymize toggle, or during delete via the Anonymize Agent toggle). Optional one-time Anonymous Name/nickname. Scrubs ticket fields, comments, mentions and history; shows "Anonymous Agent". Irreversible, and the agent is removed from all lists — UI: Setup > User Management > Agents > Deleted Agents > Anonymize — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/agents-and-teams/articles/add-manage-desk-agents)
- **ZE-78** Anonymise deleted agent via API — Agents_Anonymizedeletedagent — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Agents_Anonymizedeletedagent)
- **ZE-79** Delete or anonymise a help-center end user (GDPR) — the Delete End User window lets you show the user as Anonymous or under a Nickname. Profile data is purged and contact details scrubbed from tickets. Immediate and irreversible. Invited users cannot be anonymised (delete the invitation instead). Admins or agents with End Users permission — UI: Setup > Channels > Help Center > Users > Delete — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/support-channels/help-center/articles/managing-the-customer-portal)
- **ZE-80** Anonymise portal user via API — Users_Anonymizeuser (and Users_RemovePortalUser) — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Users_Anonymizeuser)
- **ZE-81** GDPR data access for users — agents see their own profile under Personal Settings > My Information. End users see their profile, tickets, comments and attachments under My Information / My Tickets in the help center — UI: as stated — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/compliance/articles/zoho-desk-eu-data-protection)
- **ZE-82** Data rectification — users can edit all personal data except the email address (the unique identifier). Email changes go through a request to Zoho — UI: profile edit — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/compliance/articles/zoho-desk-eu-data-protection)
- **ZE-83** Data portability — per-module CSV export, plus a free one-time bulk back-end export on request — UI: module export (see Data administration area) — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/compliance/articles/zoho-desk-eu-data-protection)
- **ZE-84** Controller/processor roles and certifications — the customer is the data controller and Zoho Desk the processor. ISO 27001 and SOC 2 Type 2 cited. No advertising use of data — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/compliance/articles/zoho-desk-eu-data-protection)
- **ZE-85** Read receipts listed under PRIVACY — read-recipient tracking for email replies. Turning it on needs Manage Privacy Settings (cross-ref Ticket replies area) — UI: n/d here — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)

### 15.10 Data residency and retention
- **ZE-86** Data-centre location chosen by sign-up domain — desk.zoho.com US, .eu EU, .in IN, .com.au AU/NZ, .com.cn CN (JP mentioned for full-disk encryption). DC migration on request, up to 5 business days, no downtime expected — UI: sign-up domain — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/compliance/articles/zoho-desk-eu-data-protection)
- **ZE-87** Recycle Bin retention — module data deleted in the UI stays 60 days in the Recycle Bin and can be restored. Deleting from the bin schedules removal within 24 h. Backups are kept 90 days — UI: Recycle Bin — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/data-retention)
- **ZE-88** Retention matrix — settings/configuration deletes are immediate. Audit logs: 2 years. Backup files: 7 days. Portal deletion on immediate request: 30 days. Account close or inactive: disabled at 120 days, deleted at 180. DC migration: 30 days. App/access logs: 90 days. Desk audit-log scheduler and admin activity logs: 20 days. Backups: +90 days — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/data-retention)
- **ZE-89** Inactive-user policy and account termination — unpaid accounts inactive for 120 days may be terminated with notice and a backup option. Activity is counted per service. After account termination, data is removed from the active DB at the six-monthly clean-up and from backups 3 months later — UI: n/a — Ed: Free (unpaid) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/data-retention)
- **ZE-90** Accidental deletion and integration data — Zoho cannot recover data a customer deletes by mistake. Data already sent to integrated services cannot be deleted from those services — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/data-security/articles/data-retention)

### Gaps / thin docs
- Agent-side SAML SSO and agent 2FA/MFA (ZE-07, ZE-08) have no Desk KB article. They are inferred to live in Zoho Accounts / Zoho Directory. The Desk SAML page says it applies to customers only.
- IP restriction is documented only through Zoho Directory (ZE-01/02). No Desk-native allowed-IP setting for agent login was found, and no help-center-wide IP restriction either (only KB "Custom IP" category visibility, ZE-04).
- DMARC appears only as a pricing row; there is no setup article. SPF and DKIM share one verification pop-up.
- The CSP article covers only the ASAP nonce. There is no doc on Desk's own CSP headers or on CSP for the help center/web forms.
- JWT for the help center has no pricing row of its own. The "Standard+" edition is inferred from the help-center SSO rows (the ASAP JWT row is Standard+).
- Session timeout, password policy, login history and agent session management are not documented in the Desk KB (assumed to be Zoho Accounts features).
- The HIPAA guide (1-year back-end audit logs) conflicts with the data-retention table (audit logs 2 years, Desk audit-log retention 20 days). The GDPR article still says audit logs are "coming soon". The GDPR article also gives 60-day retention while the retention article lists several periods.
- Encryption at rest: full-disk encryption is listed for EU/IN/AU/JP only, and the US DC status is not stated. CN appears in the hosting table but has no encryption statement.
- Attachment controls: the list of system-restricted file types and the exact help-center error text are not given. File-size limits are not covered here.
- The standard-field encryption article says Contacts only and up to 20 fields. The "System field encryption" pricing row does not give limits. The phased-rollout status is unclear.
- DSR: no limits documented (records per request, export retention, which modules qualify). The UI list of selectable modules is not given.
- SMS OTP: the FAQ article is placeholder text saying details are unconfirmed. The main article covers India/DLT only, with no OTP validity, resend or rate-limit details.

## 16. Time tracking, billing, contracts and products setup (ZB)

Edition note: Time entry and manual ticket time tracking are Standard+ (not in Free/Express). Automatic ticket time tracking, activity time tracking and billing preferences are Professional+. Contracts and support plans (and "Management in SLA" for contract SLAs) are Enterprise-only; Schedules are Enterprise-only, 10/department and 250 active per account. Products (product-based ticket tracking, contact/account association, product owner, custom views) are Standard+; product custom fields are Std 50 / Pro 150 / Ent 230; department-specific product handling is Professional+. Contracts and Time Entry custom fields (Enterprise) are capped at 20 string fields and 5 of each other type.

### 16.1 Time tracking setup (Setup > Customization > Time Tracking)
- **ZB-01** Time Tracking setup page — per department (department drop-down at top of page) — UI: Setup > Customization > Time Tracking — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZB-02** Permission "Manage Time Tracking" — controls access to the setup page; enabled by default for Administrator profile; lets users track time on tickets, tasks and billable entries — UI: Setup > Roles and Profiles > profile permissions — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/user-management-and-security/roles-and-profiles/articles/managing-user-profiles)
- **ZB-03** Disable time tracking per department — toggle "Disable Time Tracking" — UI: Setup > Customization > Time Tracking — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZB-04** Manual time tracking mode — agent starts/stops the timer on ticket detail; this is the default mode — UI: Setup > Customization > Time Tracking > Manual — Ed: Standard+ (pricing "Manual ticket time tracking") — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZB-05** Automatic time tracking mode — time logged automatically when the agent performs selected actions — UI: Setup > Customization > Time Tracking > Automatic — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZB-06** Auto-tracked action: Reply — reply, reply all, forward; selected by default and mandatory (cannot be unselected) — UI: Time Tracking > Automatic > actions — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZB-07** Auto-tracked action: Draft a reply — counted only when the agent clicks "Save Draft" — UI: Time Tracking > Automatic > actions — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZB-08** Auto-tracked actions: Field update (without editing the ticket), Approval (create/edit/delete), Comment (add/edit, public and private), Attachment (add/remove), Resolution (add/edit/delete) — admin selects all or specific actions — UI: Time Tracking > Automatic > actions — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZB-09** Auto-tracked action: Remote assistance — time in a remote session; requires Zoho Assist add-on — UI: Time Tracking > Automatic > actions — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZB-10** Exclusion — automatic tracking does not apply to replies/updates made by workflow automation or AI Agents; agents can enter time and price manually instead — UI: n/a (behaviour) — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZB-11** Option "Pause the timer when agents stay idle for 30 seconds" — pauses after 30 s idle, resumes automatically when the agent resumes work — UI: Time Tracking > Automatic options — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZB-12** Option "Hide the timer for agents" — timer hidden on ticket detail but keeps tracking; logs still visible in the Time Entry tab — UI: Time Tracking > Automatic options — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
- **ZB-13** Option "Agents can edit their time spent on the ticket" — after an auto-logged action, a 10-second review window lets the agent submit, adjust or ignore the time; window shown only when this option is on — UI: Time Tracking > Automatic options; review pop-up on ticket — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/ticket-management/time-tracking/articles/setting-up-time-tracking-and-billing-preferences-in-tickets)
