### Gaps / thin docs
- No article documents a "mandatory time entry on ticket close" setting (ZB-17 kept as n/d). No auto-start-on-open or pause-on-leaving-the-ticket option is documented either; the only automatic pause is the 30-second idle pause in automatic mode.
- The time-tracking article's "Availability" table did not survive text extraction, so editions come from the pricing rows only.
- Where hourly rates are entered per agent/profile (exact form, currency, default) is not described. The Billing History page's columns are not described.
- Invoice status: only "Total invoiced hours" is documented. How an entry is marked invoiced (a UI field or the Zoho Books/Invoice integration) is not described. The "Ticket Charge Type" values come from the standard-fields table, not from a dedicated article.
- No dedicated article for a timesheet or Time Entry list view (cross-agent timesheet, filters, export). Reports list only "Tickets By Time Entry".
- Call/Event time entry APIs are not separate resources in api_ops (only Task). Call and event time entries appear only in ticket-history events.
- Contract renewal (as opposed to the expiry notification) and contract-based ticket assignment/routing beyond SLA selection are not documented. Charging for tickets over the credit limit is described only as a business option, with no UI.
- "Schedules" is filed under support-contract-schedules but is general automation, not tied to contracts, and may duplicate the automation section.
- The FAQ says customers can see their remaining credits, but no Help Center UI for this is documented.


---

## 17. Integrations and marketplace (ZI)

Edition note: Free and Express have no integrations except G Suite (all editions) and Zoho Assist (1 free technician in every edition). Almost every Zoho and third-party integration (CRM, Bigin, Salesforce, Projects, BugTracker, Jira, Slack, MS Teams, Google Chat, Cliq, Analytics, Finance, FSM, Campaigns, SMS, YouTube, Play Reviews, Translate, Lens, Meeting, SalesIQ, PageSense, Office 365) needs Standard or higher. Telephony/PBX, private extensions and custom actions via extensions need Professional or higher. Public extensions, Zoho Flow and Zapier work in Standard, except for features that Standard does not have. Most setup screens need Administrator or the "Manage Marketplace" permission.

### 17.1 Marketplace and extension framework
- **ZI-01** Marketplace home: library of Zoho and third-party apps with **All extensions** and **Installed extensions** tabs. Filters: category (customer service, ecommerce, finance, analytics...), edition, price (free/paid) and rating. — UI: Setup > Marketplace > All — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/general/articles/navigating-the-marketplace-guided-tour)
- **ZI-02** Deployment-type filter and button label: Zoho Platform extension = "Install"; built-in Zoho integration = "Activate"; API-built third-party = "Visit Website". — UI: Setup > Marketplace — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/general/articles/navigating-the-marketplace-guided-tour)
- **ZI-03** Top-band Marketplace icon. The first click opens a carousel (shown only once) with three options: agent productivity apps (Customer Insights, Parent-child, Print...), connect favourite apps (TeamViewer, Slack, Jira, YouTube) and "Build your own extension" (opens Developer Space). — UI: top band — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/general/articles/navigating-the-marketplace-guided-tour)
- **ZI-04** Permission "Manage Marketplace" is needed to access, install and uninstall extensions, including the starter pack. — UI: Setup > Profiles — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/general/articles/navigating-the-marketplace-guided-tour)
- **ZI-05** Installed-extension filters: Installed Extensions, My Installed Extensions, Pending configuration and updates, Active / Inactive. — UI: Setup > Marketplace > Installed — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/general/articles/navigating-the-marketplace-guided-tour)
- **ZI-06** Extension starter pack: recommended extensions appear as install placeholders in their natural locations. On by default; switch it in Manage Extensions. — UI: Setup > Integration > Marketplace > Installed Extensions > Manage Extensions — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/general/articles/navigating-the-marketplace-guided-tour)
- **ZI-07** Starter-pack placeholder locations: Customer Insights (ticket detail eyebar), Ticket Insights (marketplace icon at top right of ticket detail), Print Tickets (ticket More menu > Print), Geolocation (ticket subtab and top band), My Followed Tickets (top band), Checklist (bottom band). Each has an Install button that opens the install page. — UI: ticket detail / top band / bottom band — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/general/articles/navigating-the-marketplace-guided-tour)
- **ZI-08** Drag and drop to reorder extensions inside a placeholder (needs Manage extension permission). — UI: placeholders — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/general/articles/navigating-the-marketplace-guided-tour)
- **ZI-09** Public extensions: build, host and sell on the Zoho Desk marketplace. — UI: Marketplace / Developer Space — Ed: Standard (excludes extensions using non-Standard features), Pro, Ent — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZI-10** Private extensions: build custom extensions and host them only for your own org. — UI: Developer Space / Marketplace — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZI-11** Custom actions via extensions: an installed extension can register actions that then appear in workflow-rule actions. Cross-ref: automation, Custom Actions Gallery. — UI: Setup > Automation > Workflows > Actions — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZI-12** Widget locations used by extensions: top band (`desk.topband`), ticket detail right panel (`desk.ticket.detail.rightpanel`, opened with the Extensions icon), ticket detail subtab (left icon panel), ticket More menu, bottom band and Blueprint transition. — UI: ticket detail, top band — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/developer-extensions/articles/faqs-developer-extensions)
- **ZI-13** Widgets in Blueprint: extension widgets can be added as an operation in a Blueprint "during" transition. Needs Manage Marketplace to install and Helpdesk Automation to add. Only extensions with a Blueprint location are listed. Widgets are department-specific and cannot be set as mandatory. — UI: Blueprint transition > During — Ed: Professional+ (inferred, Blueprint) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/blueprint/articles/a-guide-to-integrate-widgets-in-blueprint)
- **ZI-14** Integration settings are grouped by provider: Setup > Integrations (also called Marketplace) > Zoho / Microsoft / Google / Others / Marketplace. — UI: Setup — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/general/articles/extend-your-zoho-desk-with-integrations-and-add-ons)

### 17.2 Zoho CRM
- **ZI-15** Set up the CRM integration: Integrate > pick the CRM company from a dropdown > Authorize. Needs a Desk Administrator who is also a CRM admin, and a paid Desk plan. — UI: Setup > Integrations > Zoho > Zoho CRM — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-16** If the Desk admin is not a CRM admin: "Use another user's portal link" > enter the CRM admin's email > Request Access. An invitation email goes out (can be resent). The Configuration Details tab appears after the CRM admin approves. — UI: CRM Authentication page — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-17** Contacts/accounts sync. Sync type: Two-way (default) or one-way CRM→Desk. Optional matching on secondary email (this overwrites the Desk primary email with the CRM value). Choose CRM account/contact list views, map fields including custom fields, then Start Sync; Edit later. — UI: Zoho CRM > Configuration Details — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-18** Products sync: Configure on Products > Two-way or CRM→Desk > choose departments > optional "Also sync the product's customer (contact and account) associations" (works only with two-way sync on products AND on contacts/accounts) > map fields > Start Sync. Product attachments and tickets are not synced. — UI: Zoho CRM > Products > Configure — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-19** Sync rules: the first sync is a bulk import/export that can take hours. Only records created or modified in the last 1 year are synced. Duplicate check uses contact email, account name and product name. Contacts sync even with no email. Sync triggers on create, update, delete and mass update on either side. — UI: n/a — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-20** CRM users are auto-added to Desk as Light Agents. They can be @mentioned in tickets and are notified through CRM Signals. "@CRM Contact Owner" notifies the contact's owner in CRM. — UI: ticket comments — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-21** Delete preferences. On CRM delete: Always delete in Desk / Delete only if no associates (otherwise unmap) / Never (unmap only). On Desk delete: Always / Never. Deleted records go to the Recycle Bin. Associates: Contacts = tickets, time entries, activities, attachments; Accounts = tickets, contacts, contracts, attachments; Products = attachments. — UI: Zoho CRM > Configuration Details > Preferences — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-22** Profile-level CRM permissions: a "Visible" checkbox per profile, an Accounts-or-Contacts toggle, and Full / Restricted access for Contact/Account Information, Potentials, Notes and Activities (Calls, Events, Tasks each selectable). — UI: Zoho CRM > Permissions tab — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-23** Sync History shows date, source→target (Desk→CRM or reverse), record type (Contacts/Accounts/Products) and counts added / updated / failed. — UI: Zoho CRM > Sync History — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/integration/articles/faqs-zoho-desk-integration-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-24** Failure Log per record type with failure time and reason, and a "Try Again" re-sync. Messages include: "CRM ID is already mapped with another Support Entity", "Text value is given for an Integer field", Mandatory Field Missing, Missing Parameter, Incorrect API parameter. — UI: Zoho CRM > Failure Log — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-25** Pause Sync / Resume: no data exchange while paused. — UI: Zoho CRM Integration page — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-26** Delete integration: More (...) > Delete Integration > confirm. After that, sync stops, CRM info is no longer visible and Handshake Mode is lost. — UI: Zoho CRM page > More — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-27** CRM info inside tickets: a CRM logo/icon on the left of the ticket, below the contact name, shows the CRM profile, potentials, notes and activities. Also shown on contact/account detail pages. The match uses the ticket sender's email. — UI: ticket detail left panel; contact/account detail — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/integration/articles/faqs-zoho-desk-integration-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-28** System views "Contacts mapped with CRM" / "Accounts mapped with CRM", plus a list of records not yet mapped. — UI: Customers module views — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/integration/articles/faqs-zoho-desk-integration-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-29** Handshake Mode: ticket columns grouped by CRM customer type: Customers, Opportunities, Contacts/Leads, New. — UI: Tickets > Handshake mode — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/integration/articles/faqs-zoho-desk-integration-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-30** Switch one-way to two-way later via Edit > "Enable Two-way Sync". Changing a field mapping affects only records created or modified afterwards. — UI: Zoho CRM > Sync Settings > Edit — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/integration/articles/faqs-zoho-desk-integration-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-31** Email notification when the integration completes, with added / updated / ignored counts and skip reasons. — UI: email — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/integration/articles/faqs-zoho-desk-integration-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-32** When the trial expires or the org downgrades to Free, sync pauses and CRM data is hidden on both sides. It resumes after upgrading. — UI: n/a — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/integration/articles/faqs-zoho-desk-integration-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-33** CRM side: a "Zoho Desk" related list in CRM records, with a refresh button. Users in both apps can create and reply to tickets from CRM; CRM-only users can only view (priority, due date, assignee). — UI: Zoho CRM record > Zoho Desk related list — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/integration/articles/faqs-zoho-desk-integration-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-34** Limits: CRM custom modules cannot be synced (custom-function workaround through CRM support). The CRM client portal and the Desk help center are separate. — UI: n/a — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/integration/articles/faqs-zoho-desk-integration-with-zoho-crm)
- **ZI-35** Mutual exclusion: if Zoho CRM is integrated, the Bigin and Salesforce integrations are restricted, to prevent record loops. — UI: Setup > Integrations — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/zoho-desk-for-bigin-integration)

### 17.3 Bigin and Salesforce
- **ZI-36** Bigin integration in 3 steps (authenticate, contacts/accounts, products). Standard fields come pre-mapped. Two-way (default) or Bigin→Desk. Product sync per department. Shows potentials, notes and activities beside the ticket, supports Handshake Mode, and shows Desk tickets inside Bigin. — UI: Setup > Integrations > Zoho > Bigin — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/zoho-desk-for-bigin-integration)
- **ZI-37** Salesforce prerequisites: Desk Standard/Pro/Enterprise; Salesforce Enterprise, Unlimited, Performance, Professional (with API) or Developer edition; Salesforce admin credentials. — UI: Setup > Integrations > Others > Salesforce — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/zoho-desk-for-salesforce-crm-integration)
- **ZI-38** Salesforce sync: accounts and contacts only, two-way (default) or Salesforce→Desk, pre-mapped standard fields. First sync takes about 30–60 min. Deletes are NOT propagated, and updating a Salesforce record that was deleted in Desk re-creates it. — UI: Salesforce > Configuration Details — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/zoho-desk-for-salesforce-crm-integration)
- **ZI-39** Salesforce data inside tickets (potentials, notes, activities, customer profile on contact/account), with profile-level permissions. — UI: ticket detail; Salesforce > Permissions — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/zoho-desk-for-salesforce-crm-integration)

### 17.4 Zoho Projects and Zoho BugTracker
- **ZI-40** Zoho Projects setup: needs the Support Administrator profile permission. One Projects portal per Desk org. Departments are mapped to projects. — UI: Setup > Integrations > Zoho > Zoho Projects — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/project-management/articles/setting-up-zoho-projects-integration)
- **ZI-41** From a ticket: create Projects tasks and submit issues. Task subject, description and due date sync in real time. Comments are shared. Issue assignee, status and due date show inside the ticket. The linked ticket opens from the issue in Projects. Tasks/issues can be delinked. — UI: ticket detail — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/project-management/articles/setting-up-zoho-projects-integration)
- **ZI-42** Projects config: "Issues" checkbox under Zoho Projects Modules (hidden if BugTracker is integrated). "Configure Actions – Issues" toggles choose notifications and comment-sync direction. Disabling keeps settings; deleting removes ticket actions. — UI: Zoho Projects > Configuration; More > Delete — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/project-management/articles/setting-up-zoho-projects-integration)
- **ZI-43** BugTracker prerequisites: Desk Standard+, plus BugTracker Enterprise or Projects Enterprise with the BugTracker add-on. Uses a ZSC Key (Setup > Developer Space > ZSC Key). Departments map to all projects by default (Edit to change). Portal can be changed or the integration deleted. — UI: Setup > Integrations > Zoho > Zoho BugTracker — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/project-management/articles/configuring-zoho-bugtracker-integration)
- **ZI-44** File a bug: More Options on a conversation thread > File a bug. Available only in departments mapped to a project; filing from the latest thread is recommended. "Link to bug" attaches an existing bug. A ticket can link to only one bug. — UI: ticket thread > More options — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/project-management/articles/filing-a-ticket-as-bug)
- **ZI-45** Bug Information icon in the ticket's left tab panel: Bug ID (opens BugTracker if permitted), Subject, Assignee, Status. Close, Re-open and Delink actions. Comments and status updates sync both ways. — UI: ticket detail left panel — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/project-management/articles/filing-a-ticket-as-bug)

### 17.5 Atlassian Jira
- **ZI-46** Jira setup: Cloud or On-premise (on-prem URL must be HTTPS and reachable from the internet). Needs a Jira admin. OAuth access token used for the trust link. Paid editions only. — UI: Setup > Integrations > Others > Jira — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/project-management/articles/configuring-zoho-desk-for-jira-integration)
- **ZI-47** Optional Jira custom field "Zoho Desk Ticket" (Text Field, multi-line) added to project screens, which shows the Desk ticket link in Jira. — UI: Jira Admin > Issues > Custom Fields — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/project-management/articles/configuring-zoho-desk-for-jira-integration)
- **ZI-48** Sync actions and department-to-project mapping are auto-configured, all actions on by default, and can be customised. — UI: Jira integration page — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/project-management/articles/configuring-zoho-desk-for-jira-integration)
- **ZI-49** File a Jira issue from one thread (More Actions on a customer response) or for the whole ticket (top-bar More Actions). Defaults: summary = ticket subject, description = email content. Link an existing issue by key (e.g. ZDK-100). One Jira issue per ticket. Editing Reporter needs the "Modify Reporter" permission in Jira. — UI: ticket detail — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/project-management/articles/reporting-a-bug-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZI-50** Jira icon at the upper left of the ticket: Issue ID, Priority, Status, with Close and Delink actions. Comments sync both ways (only those posted after linking, and only if the option is on). Agents are notified on Jira status changes and comments. — UI: ticket detail — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/project-management/articles/reporting-a-bug-in-zoho-desk)

### 17.6 Chat and collaboration tools
- **ZI-51** Slack: install "Slack For Zoho Desk" from Marketplace > choose the "Post to" channel. Multiple Slack workspaces can be added ("Your Slack Workspaces" tab), each with department→channel mapping and a choice of notified events (ticket closed, status change, happiness rating...). — UI: Setup > Integrations > Marketplace > Slack — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/collaboration/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-slack)
- **ZI-52** Slack slash commands fetch ticket info. Replies are private to the user who ran the command. Non-admin users must log in to Desk on first use. From Slack you can also create tickets, comment, reply, get Blueprint transition reminders and receive ticket DMs. — UI: Slack — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/collaboration/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-slack)
- **ZI-53** MS Teams "Zoho Desk" tab in a channel (installed from Apps or Teams). Actions: ticket detail, public/private comment, reply / reply all / forward, change status, assign to agent or team, followers, tags, edit properties, log time. Only Teams users who are Desk users, within their Desk profile permissions. — UI: MS Teams channel tab — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/collaboration/articles/setting-up-microsoft-teams-integration)
- **ZI-54** MS Teams channel bot and personal bot: "@Zoho Desk set portal & department"; commands Customize notification, Find a ticket, Show recent tickets, Show my recent tickets, Show tickets due today, Show overdue tickets, Add ticket; "help" lists commands. Create/update notifications go to the channel or personal bot. — UI: MS Teams > Posts — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/collaboration/articles/setting-up-a-bot-in-microsoft-teams)
- **ZI-55** Google Chat: add Zoho Desk to a Chat Space; `/zohodesk_tickets` command to view, create, assign, comment on and update ticket status. Personal Chat settings control per-user notifications. Only users added in Desk, within their profile permissions. — UI: Google Chat space — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/collaboration/articles/setting-up-google-chat-integration)
- **ZI-56** Zoho Cliq: org channel `#desk` with a notification bot (status updates, comments, escalations). The ticket owner is not notified of their own changes. Departments can map to Cliq channels (all default to #desk; section hidden when there is only one department). Admin preferences; supports multiple portals. — UI: Cliq; Setup > Integrations > Zoho > Cliq — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/collaboration/articles/zoho-cliq-integration)
- **ZI-57** Cliq quick actions from DM notifications (reply, change status, assign, comment). Consolidated summary: open / due / resolved counts, CSAT and top-3 contributors. Search tickets by ID or customer email. Only admins invite agents. — UI: Cliq — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/collaboration/articles/zoho-cliq-integration)
- **ZI-58** Zoho Mail integration: work with Desk tickets from Zoho Mail. — UI: Zoho Mail — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/collaboration/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-zoho-mail)

### 17.7 Identity and suite integrations
- **ZI-59** G Suite / Google Workspace: add G Suite users as agents (assigned CEO role + Agent profile by default, within the licence limit; extra users are deactivated after trial until licences are bought, then re-invite from Setup > Agents > Inactive). Import G Suite contacts into Contacts. New sign-ups get a 15-day Enterprise trial. — UI: Setup > Marketplace > Google — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/collaboration/articles/enabling-zoho-desk-for-google-apps)
- **ZI-60** Office 365: on by default for orgs that sign in with O365. Add O365 users as agents instantly (no verification), with SSO. — UI: Setup > Integrations > Microsoft; Setup > Agents — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/sales-and-marketing/articles/setting-up-zoho-desk-for-office-365-integration)

### 17.8 Analytics
- **ZI-61** Zoho Analytics advanced reports. Not in Free; admin only; one Analytics account per Desk. Choose modules (Tickets and Contacts are mandatory) and a sync schedule (daily at a set time, or None = sync once). Settings are locked during the initial transfer. Includes a 15-day Analytics trial, drag-and-drop reports and dashboards, sharing, scheduled emails, export to PDF/HTML/Excel/CSV/image, and embedding. — UI: Setup > Integrations > Zoho > Zoho Analytics — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/analytics-and-reports/articles/advanced-helpdesk-reports-with-zoho-analytics)
- **ZI-62** Microsoft Power BI integration for custom support metrics. — UI: Marketplace — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/analytics-and-reports/articles/integrating-zoho-desk-with-microsoft-powerbi)
- **ZI-63** Analytics extensions: Custom Dashboards; Customer Insights (tickets received, response/resolution time, happiness % in the ticket eyebar); Ticket Insights (responses sent/received, comments, average handling time); Ticket Status Lifecycle; Microsoft Sentiment Analysis (scores each ticket's sentiment). — UI: ticket detail / Marketplace — Ed: Standard+ (inferred, marketplace) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/analytics-and-reports/articles/customer-insights-extension-for-zoho-desk)

### 17.9 Finance, inventory, billing, field service
- **ZI-64** Zoho Books or Zoho Invoice (one at a time, admin only). The org list shows only orgs where you are an admin. Create and view invoices and estimates inside tickets and change their status. Three-level profile permissions for invoice/estimate actions. — UI: Setup > Integrations > Zoho > Zoho Books / Zoho Invoice; ticket left panel — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/finance/articles/setting-up-zoho-finance-suite-integration)
- **ZI-65** Zoho Inventory: view and create invoices and sales orders for a contact (with status) from tickets, contacts and accounts. Optional product sync into a chosen department ("Also sync your Zoho Inventory Products" toggle), with Pause Sync, Edit mapping and a Sync History tab. Profile permissions for invoices, sales orders and contacts. — UI: Setup > Integrations > Zoho > Zoho Inventory — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/finance/articles/setting-up-zoho-inventory-integration)
- **ZI-66** Zoho Billing (formerly Subscriptions): Zoho Billing icon on the left of the ticket, with tabs Overview (unused credits, receivables, addresses), Activity, Subscriptions (amount, status, invoices) and Invoices (filter: this ticket or all for the contact). Also on contact, account and ticket forms. — UI: ticket detail left panel — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/finance/articles/setting-up-zoho-subscriptions-integration)

### Gaps / thin docs
- No help article for **Zoho FSM**, **Zoho Flow** (only a one-paragraph mention), **Zoho Voice** or a Zoho Books-only article; FSM and Flow come from pricing only.
- Docs say both "Setup > Integrations" and "Setup > Marketplace" for the same screens; the current UI label was not confirmed.
- Zoho Assist and Lens trial lengths conflict inside each article (15 vs 30 days, 15 vs 90 days).
- The full list of extension widget locations (Marketplace developer manifest) is not in the local text; only `desk.topband` and `desk.ticket.detail.rightpanel` are confirmed. The others are taken from where the help articles say widgets appear.
- Slack slash-command names, the full Slack notification event list and Google Chat command options are shown only as images in the source.
- Third-party extensions (ZI-82..ZI-104) have no edition in the docs; they are assumed to follow marketplace availability (Standard+).
- Exact Jira sync-action toggles and the Finance permission level names were not captured in detail.

## 18. Developer space (API, webhooks, SDKs, extensions) (ZV)

Edition note: the REST API is in every edition, metered by daily credits: Free 5,000; Express 25,000 + 100/user; Standard 50,000 + 250/user; Professional 75,000 + 500/user; Enterprise 100,000 + 1,000/user; plus add-on credits up to 5M. The API dashboard, usage matrix and usage alerts are Express and higher. Webhooks are Professional (5 active) and Enterprise (10 active), max 20 per org including inactive. Mobile SDK is Professional+. Deluge custom functions are Enterprise only on the pricing page. Concurrency limits: Free/Trial 5, Express 10, Standard 10, Professional 15, Enterprise 25.

### 18.1 REST API conventions and authentication
- **ZV-01** REST API root `desk.zoho.com/api/v1`. JSON only (the old XML/JSON APIs used portal name and authtoken). Changes are announced on the Zoho Desk Developer APIs forum. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Introduction)
- **ZV-02** Mandatory headers: `Authorization` and `orgId` on every endpoint except `/organizations`. With OAuth org binding, `orgId` may be omitted, but if sent it must match the token's org, otherwise the error is `OAUTH_ORG_MISMATCH`. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#OAuthOrgIdBind)
- **ZV-03** Auth header formats: `Zoho-oauthtoken {token}` or `Bearer {token}`. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#MakingAPICalls)
- **ZV-04** OAuth 2.0: register the app in the Zoho API console. Flows for server-based, client-based, mobile/desktop, limited-input devices and self client. Access token lifetime is in `expires_in` (1 hour); an optional refresh token gives renewal. No per-token rate limit. — UI: API console — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#OauthTokens)
- **ZV-05** Organisation binding: a token is bound to one Desk org at consent. A user with several portals picks the org on the consent screen. — UI: OAuth consent — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#OAuthOrgIdBind)
- **ZV-06** OAuth scopes: `Desk.tickets.ALL/READ/WRITE/UPDATE/CREATE/DELETE`, `Desk.contacts.READ/WRITE/UPDATE/CREATE` (contacts + accounts), `Desk.tasks.ALL/READ/WRITE/CREATE/UPDATE/DELETE`, `Desk.basic.READ/CREATE` (orgs, agents, departments), `Desk.search.READ`, `Desk.events.ALL/READ/WRITE/CREATE/UPDATE/DELETE` (event subscriptions), `Desk.articles.READ/CREATE/UPDATE/DELETE`. Multiple scopes are comma-separated. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#OAuthScopes)
- **ZV-07** Data-centre base URLs: US desk.zoho.com, IN .in, AU .com.au, CA zohocloud.ca, SA .sa, JP .jp, CN .com.cn, EU .eu, SG .sg, UAE .ae. Use `api_domain` from the token response (never hard-code). Multi-DC must be enabled in the API console. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#DataCenterEndpoints)
- **ZV-08** HTTP methods: GET (retrieve), POST (create / actions), PUT (update), PATCH (partial update), DELETE. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#HTTPMethods)
- **ZV-09** Pagination with `from` (offset, default 1) and `limit` (default 10, max 50 unless an API says otherwise). Responses are wrapped as `{"data":[...]}`. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Pagination)
- **ZV-10** `include` query param (comma list) returns related resources in one call, e.g. owner and assignee with tickets. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Include)
- **ZV-11** Conventions: ignore undocumented fields/enums (they may be experimental or deprecated). Record fields are dynamic because of per-profile field-security filtering. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Conventions)
- **ZV-12** Compatibility: backward compatibility is kept. Minor deprecations (field, URL, param) get a forum notice and a transition period. Only major changes bump the version in the URL. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Compatibility)
- **ZV-13** OpenAPI Specification 3.1.0 for all Desk APIs (JSON/YAML), hosted on GitHub. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#OAS)
- **ZV-14** `featureFlags` request header (e.g. `multiLayout,ticketTeam`) shown in API examples to opt into feature-specific response shapes. — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Pagination)
- **ZV-15** HTTP status codes: 200, 201, 204, 400, 401, 403, 404, 405, 413, 415, 422, 429, 500. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Errors)
- **ZV-16** Machine-readable `errorCode` values: UNAUTHORIZED, INVALID_OAUTH, SCOPE_MISMATCH, OAUTH_ORG_MISMATCH, FORBIDDEN, LICENSE_ACCESS_LIMITED (with `feature` AGENTS/LIGHT_AGENTS/TEAM and `editionType` FREE/EXPRESS/STANDARD/PROFESSIONAL/ENTERPRISE/ULTIMATE), URL_NOT_FOUND, METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED, RESOURCE_SIZE_EXCEEDED, TOO_MANY_REQUESTS, and others. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Errors)
- **ZV-17** Authtoken deprecation and migration API (`authtooauth`). Web-based apps: submit a form and get support verification first. Self-client apps: migrate directly with scope and `soid=ZohoSupport.<orgId>`. Old authtokens are deleted 24 h after migration. Custom functions move to Connections. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/rest-apis/articles/deprecating-support-for-authtokens)
- **ZV-18** Migration table from the old XML/JSON APIs (addrecords / updaterecords / getrecords for Requests, Contacts, Accounts, Tasks, TimeEntry, Contracts, Products, Solutions) to REST equivalents. Portal name is replaced by orgId (from Get all organizations). — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/rest-apis/articles/migrating-from-xml-json-apis-to-our-new-rest-apis)
- **ZV-19** Further documentation sections (headings only, no operations parsed): BulkUpload, BulkDownload, HelpCenterApps, MailConfigurations, Criteria. — UI: API — Ed: n/d — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument)

### 18.2 API credits, concurrency and rate headers
- **ZV-20** Daily API credits per edition. Base / per-agent (light agents excluded): Free 5,000/0; Express 25,000/100; Standard 50,000/250; Professional 75,000/500; Enterprise and Zoho bundles (One, CRM Plus, Service Plus) 100,000/1,000. Plus add-on credits. — UI: Setup > Developer Space > APIs — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/apidashboard/articles/working-with-api-credits)
- **ZV-21** Credits are charged only for external calls (integrations, extensions, custom apps, SDKs, backend). The Desk web UI, mobile app and Radar UI do not consume credits. — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/apidashboard/articles/working-with-api-credits)
- **ZV-22** Credits reset daily (00:00–23:59:59 in the data-centre timezone); unused credits expire. When exhausted, new requests are denied, API-based automations may stop, and threshold alerts fire. — UI: n/a — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/apidashboard/articles/working-with-api-credits)
- **ZV-23** Add-on credits: super admin only, paid plan only. Buy at checkout or as a standalone add-on (Zoho Store), or through support / account manager. 1,000–5,000,000 per transaction, max 5M in total. Monthly price per 1,000 daily credits: first 25k $4.20, next 75k $1.80, next 150k $1.50, above 250k $0.75. — UI: Zoho Store — Ed: Express+ (paid) — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#IncreaseCredits)
- **ZV-24** Credit consumption model. Fixed: 1 credit per single-record fetch. Bulk write: update many = 1 credit per 2 records; trash = 6/record; restore from recycle bin = 6/record; generic action = 1/record; 50 credits by default if no IDs are given. Bulk fetch by range: ≤2,000 = 3; ≤10,000 = 5; ≤50,000 = 10; ≤100,000 = 50; above that = 100. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#ConcurrencyLimit)
- **ZV-25** Concurrency limit (simultaneous active OAuth calls per org, shared by all clients and tokens; not counted for UI traffic): Free/Trial 5, Express 10, Standard 10, Professional 15, Enterprise/Zoho One/CRM Plus 25. Exceeding it returns `TOO_MANY_REQUESTS` "The maximum number of Concurrent API Calls that can be made has been exceeded." — UI: API Details page — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/apidashboard/articles/working-with-api-credits)
- **ZV-26** Rate response headers: `X-Rate-Limit-Request-Weight-v3` (credits used by this call), `X-Rate-Limit-Remaining-v3` (credits left today), and `Retry-After` (seconds) on HTTP 429 once the daily cap is hit. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#RateLimitResponseHeader)

### 18.3 API dashboard, usage matrix and alerts
- **ZV-27** API Details page: daily usage split into base / variable / additional credits, % used today, API plan for the edition, org ID, credits allotted for the day, concurrency limit. — UI: Setup > Developer Space > APIs — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/apidashboard/articles/working-with-api-credits)
- **ZV-28** API Dashboard: paid editions; needs the Helpdesk Automation permission. The Custom Function component shows only in Professional and Enterprise. Also available in the Radar app. — UI: Setup > Developer Space > API > API Dashboard — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/apidashboard/articles/working-with-api-dashboard)

Most components switch between donut, table, bar and pie. — UI: API Dashboard — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/apidashboard/articles/working-with-api-dashboard)
- **ZV-30** Dashboard behaviour: manual refresh (whole dashboard or per component). Tables show the top 1,000 rows and charts the top 10. "View All" turns a chart into a table. — UI: API Dashboard — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/apidashboard/articles/working-with-api-dashboard)

- **ZV-32** Default usage emails to Support Administrators at 80% and 90% of the limit. — UI: email — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/apidashboard/articles/working-with-api-dashboard)
- **ZV-33** API Usage Alerts: on first visit, three default alerts are created (80/90/100%) for the primary contact and support-admin agents; their creation is written to the Audit Log. Needs the Helpdesk Automation permission. — UI: Setup > Developer Space > API > API Usage Alerts — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/apidashboard/articles/working-with-api-usage-alerts)
- **ZV-34** New Alert fields: Name (max 100 chars), Description (max 1,500), Alert Threshold (50–100%), Email Template (fixed, not editable), Recipients (max 50: agents, profiles, primary contact, or external emails that are not validated). Created enabled; max 5 active alerts; edit and delete on hover. — UI: API Usage Alerts > New Alert — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/apidashboard/articles/working-with-api-usage-alerts)
- **ZV-35** Global Enabled toggle for usage alerts (on by default). Turning it off leaves individual alerts showing "enabled" but they stay inactive. — UI: API Usage Alerts — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/apidashboard/articles/working-with-api-usage-alerts)

### 18.4 Ticket History API
- **ZV-36** `GET api/v1/tickets/{ticket_Id}/History`. Params: `from` (default 0), `limit` 1–50 (default 10), `eventFilter` (CommentHistory, TimeEntryHistory, AttachmentHistory, ApprovalHistory, AssignmentRuleHistory, NotificationRuleHistory, WorkflowHistory, MacroHistory, SuperviseHistory, SLAHistory, BlueprintHistory, TaskHistory, CallHistory, EventHistory), `agentId`, `fieldName` (API name). Filters combine with AND; results are newest first. — UI: API (mirrors ticket History tab) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/rest-apis/articles/get-ticket-history-api-explained-in-detail)
- **ZV-37** History entry shape: `eventName`, `eventTime`, `actor{id,name,type}`, `source`, `eventInfo[]` and `actorInfo[]` of `{propertyName, propertyValue, propertyType, systemProperty}`; `propertyValue` type depends on `propertyType`. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/rest-apis/articles/get-ticket-history-api-explained-in-detail)
- **ZV-38** History event names: TicketCreated/Updated/Deleted/Restored/Cloned/MergedMaster/SplitParent/SplitChild/Archived/Escalated/Shared/ShareModeUpdated/ShareRevoked; ThreadAdded/Deleted/Resent; Comment Added/Edited/Deleted; Approval Sent/Approved/Rejected; Followers and Tags Added/Removed; Attachment Added/Updated/Deleted/Restored/DeletedRB; TimeEntry Added/Updated/Deleted/Restored; SecondaryContacts Added/Updated; OvershotDueTime; Blueprint Applied/Revoked/TransitionPerformed/StateMoved; CustomFunctionExecuted; NotificationSent; Call/Event/Task Added/Updated/Deleted/Restored plus their comment and time-entry events; BugCreated/Linked/Delinked; InvoiceCreated; EstimateCreated; SalesOrderCreated. — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/rest-apis/articles/events-supported-in-get-ticket-history-api)
- **ZV-39** TicketCreated variants. Customer via channel: source = Email, Chat, OfflineChat, HelpCenter, Forum, Twitter, Facebook, FeedbackWidget, WebForm or CustomChannel. Agent via submit form: source null. Agent import: source "Import". — UI: API — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/rest-apis/articles/events-supported-in-get-ticket-history-api)

### 18.5 Webhooks
- **ZV-40** Webhooks push an HTTP POST to a third-party URL when events happen. Org-wide: max 20 webhooks (active + inactive); active limit Professional 5, Enterprise 10. — UI: Setup > Developer Space > Webhooks — Ed: Pro 5 active, Ent 10 active — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/webhooks/articles/setting-up-webhooks-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZV-41** Ticket events: Add; Update specific fields with/without previous value (max 5 fields); Update with/without previous value; Delete; Incoming / Outgoing / All threads; Add / Update comment; Add / Update / Delete attachment; Approval add; Approval update with/without previous value. — UI: New Webhook > Choose Event — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/webhooks/articles/setting-up-webhooks-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZV-42** Other modules: Contacts, Accounts, Agents, Tasks, Time Entry, KB Category, KB Section (Add, Update ±previous value, Delete); Departments (Add, Update only); Articles (also Translation add/update/delete and Feedback add). — UI: New Webhook — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/webhooks/articles/setting-up-webhooks-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZV-43** New Webhook form: Name, URL to Notify (callback), Description, Choose Event (module + event + department; "+" adds more events), and "Test Run" posts a sample payload. Special characters are not allowed in Name, URL and Description. — UI: Developer Space > Webhooks > New Webhook — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/webhooks/articles/setting-up-webhooks-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZV-44** Restrictions: only public URLs with no authentication (no basic auth, OAuth or API-key headers). Payload-in-URL notify URLs are not supported; data goes in the POST body. — UI: n/a — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/automation/articles/faq-webhooks)
- **ZV-45** Webhook list: MODULE-EVENT column shows the event count (click for the list); per-row Test Run, Edit and Delete; active toggle; filters Modules, Events, Active, Inactive. — UI: Webhooks list — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/automation/webhooks/articles/setting-up-webhooks-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZV-46** "With previous value" payloads carry old and new values; "without" carries only new values. Source ID (UUID) lets the listener correlate payloads or ignore its own changes. — UI: n/a — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/automation/articles/faq-webhooks)
- **ZV-47** Webhooks created by an extension cannot be edited or deactivated in Desk. They are declared in the extension's `resource.json` (`subscriptions` such as Ticket_Add, Ticket_Update {includePrevState}, Ticket_Thread_Add, Ticket_Comment_Add/Update, plus `ignoreSourceId`). — UI: extension — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/developer-extensions/articles/faqs-developer-extensions)
- **ZV-48** Event-subscription webhook API (scopes `Desk.events.*`) for programmatic subscriptions. — UI: API — Ed: Professional+ (inferred) — [src](https://desk.zoho.com/DeskAPIDocument#Webhook)
- **ZV-49** IM webhooks: automate event-driven tasks between messaging apps and workflows (cross-ref Channels / IM). — UI: Instant Messaging — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)

### 18.6 Connections, custom functions and best practices
- **ZV-50** Connections: needs the Helpdesk Automation or Manage Marketplace permission. "My Connections" lists your own connections; Create Connection opens "Pick Your Service" (predefined) or "Custom Services" (custom auth type and details). — UI: Setup > Developer Space > Connections — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/connections/articles/setting-up-connections-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZV-51** Connection fields: Connection Name, a unique Connection LinkName (used in Deluge integration tasks and invokeUrl), optional scopes. "Create and Connect" > authorise > summary page. The same connection works for widgets and functions via `connectionLinkName`. — UI: Create Connection — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/connections/articles/setting-up-connections-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZV-52** Deluge custom functions (cross-ref Automation section). — UI: Setup > Automation / Developer Space — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)
- **ZV-53** Best practice for functions: null-check fields; reuse data already fetched; use `include`; check current values before updating (e.g. already closed). `sendmail` is capped at 2,500/day and is not logged in the thread, so use `sendReply` instead. Use feature flags to skip automation; read values from function arguments. — UI: n/a — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/best-practices/articles/optimal-utilization-of-custom-functions-and-apis)
- **ZV-54** Best practice for APIs: use `updateMany` for bulk updates; update several attributes in one call; use the latestThread API; call the upload API once per file (bulk upload of the same file gives only one ID). — UI: n/a — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/developer-space/best-practices/articles/optimal-utilization-of-custom-functions-and-apis)

### 18.7 Extensions developer platform (Sigma)
- **ZV-55** Build functions in Sigma (sigma.zoho.com/workspace > Functions > Add Function) with the Deluge Script Builder. The function's return type must be String. — UI: Sigma — Ed: Standard+ (public) / Pro+ (private) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/developer-extensions/articles/faqs-developer-extensions)
- **ZV-56** Extension lifecycle triggers: extension_installed, _uninstalled, _on_run, _on_stop, _disabled, _enabled, _updated, _upgraded. — UI: Sigma — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/developer-extensions/articles/faqs-developer-extensions)
- **ZV-57** Function versioning when attaching to an extension; `${LATEST}` always uses the newest version. One function can be reused in many extensions. — UI: Sigma — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/developer-extensions/articles/faqs-developer-extensions)
- **ZV-58** Test and debug: Extension Details > Functions tab > choose Organization > Add Arguments > Save & Execute. Syntax errors are highlighted on Save; runtime errors on Save & Execute. — UI: Sigma — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/developer-extensions/articles/faqs-developer-extensions)
- **ZV-59** Multi-DC extensions: publishing takes about 2–3 weeks. Outbound domains go in plugin-manifest `whiteListedDomains`; widget `other_dc_urls` per DC (EU/IN/AU/CN/JP). Worldwide visibility needs an acknowledgement; partner extensions are US-DC only until requested. `service_domain` / `sigma_domain` keys are available in functions. — UI: plugin-manifest.json — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/developer-extensions/articles/faqs-developer-extensions)
- **ZV-60** Widget `location` values in the manifest, e.g. `desk.topband` and `desk.ticket.detail.rightpanel`, each with name, logo, icon and url. Blueprint-transition location: see ZI-13. — UI: plugin-manifest.json — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/faqs/developer-extensions/articles/faqs-developer-extensions)
- **ZV-61** ZSC Key (used by the BugTracker integration). — UI: Setup > Developer Space > ZSC Key — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/integrations-and-marketplace/project-management/articles/configuring-zoho-bugtracker-integration)

### Gaps / thin docs
- The pricing page lists Deluge custom functions as Enterprise only, but the API dashboard shows a Custom Function component in Professional too. The edition gate for custom functions needs checking.
- Webhook delivery details (retry policy, timeout, payload signing) are not documented. Webhooks only accept unauthenticated URLs.
- Express API dashboard availability: the article says "all paid editions" and the pricing page says Express+. These agree, but which components appear in Express was not stated beyond "Standard shows all except Custom Function".
- The full list of extension widget locations (sigma developer docs) is not in the local text; only two location keys are confirmed.
- The list of `errorCode` values was truncated after RESOURCE_SIZE_EXCEEDED, so it is only partial (TOO_MANY_REQUESTS comes from the concurrency section).
- The API reference sections BulkUpload, BulkDownload, HelpCenterApps, MailConfigurations, Criteria and BulkExport "Limitations" had no parseable operations or text in the local copy.
- Operation names in 18.9 are the anchor slugs from the API document (spaces removed), not verified one by one. Per-operation credit weights and required scopes are not listed.
- SDK version numbers come from the first entries in the release notes as of capture and may change.


---

## 19. Agent workspace, personalisation, collaboration and search (ZW)

Edition note: All PERSONALIZATION rows (night mode, display layout, font controls, reply editor font, reply button behaviour, landing page, article auto-suggestion, default send behaviour, multi-language, date/time format) and all ACCESSIBILITY rows are in every edition. Quick search/advanced search, peek view and response drafts are Free+; keyboard shortcuts, table view and agent queue are Express+; search facets, work modes, real-time list/detail updates, real-time starred-view counts and Team Feeds are Standard+. Gamescope, agent idle timeout, teams queue and agent collision detection/chat/reply avoidance are Professional+. Workstation (ticket detail styles) is Enterprise early-access on request. Preferences are stored per browser.

### 19.1 Preferences: appearance and display
- **ZW-01** Quick Preferences panel — opens from profile picture > User Info panel > Preferences; applies to Tickets, Contacts, Activities, Community, IM, Social, Analytics; stored per browser — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/personalizing-zoho-desk-interface-with-preferences)
- **ZW-02** Appearance — Light, Night, Auto (follows browser/device) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/personalizing-zoho-desk-interface-with-preferences)
- **ZW-03** Appearance sub-options — Light: "Make Left Panel Light"; Night: "Make it Darker" — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/personalizing-zoho-desk-interface-with-preferences)
- **ZW-04** Appearance Quick Switcher on the bottom bar — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/personalizing-zoho-desk-interface-with-preferences)
- **ZW-05** Color themes — Red, Blue, Yellow, Green, Orange — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/personalizing-zoho-desk-interface-with-preferences)
- **ZW-06** Display Layout — Adaptive (default), Focused, Full Width — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/using-accessibility-controls)
- **ZW-07** Display font type — Zoho Puvi (default), Lato, Roboto, Vazirmatn (Arabic/Persian mixed script), OpenDyslexic — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/personalizing-zoho-desk-interface-with-preferences)
- **ZW-08** Display font size — preset sizes; finer slider in Accessibility Controls — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/personalizing-zoho-desk-interface-with-preferences)
- **ZW-09** Default editor font — for reply editor, ticket description, Add form, Snippets, Send as Email — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/accessibility-and-preferences/articles/personalizing-zoho-desk-interface-with-preferences)

## 20. Zia and AI (ZZ)

Edition note: Content analysis is in all editions (incl. Free). Generative AI is Express+ as BYOK (ChatGPT / DeepSeek / Azure OpenAI); native Zia LLM (Qwen) is Professional+ (pricing: "BYOK & Native"; comparison article says native is free for Enterprise). Summary, Insights, Reply/Writing assistance, Generate content and Article generation are Express+. Answer Bot, Sentiment, Auto Tags, Zia notifications and Thread keywords are Standard+. Field predictions and Anomaly predictions are Professional+. Zia Actions, Deluge assistant bot, Zia dashboard, pre-built AI agents (US/IN DC), IM message suggestions and IM reply assistance are Enterprise. DC availability differs per feature (US, EU, IN, AU, JP, CA, CN, UAE; Zia Actions only US/CA/EU/IN/AU; Qwen not in CN).

Common sources: pricing = https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html (ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE - ZIA, Intelligence, Anomaly, AI AGENTS, HELP DESK AUTOMATION).

### 20.1 Zia enablement, permissions, data handling
- **ZZ-01** Master Zia toggle (org-wide, applies to all departments/portals); then per-department feature toggles — UI: Setup > Zia (toggle at top) — Ed: per feature — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/intelligence/articles/understanding-zia-auto-tags)
- **ZZ-02** Profile permissions: "Zia" (use/configure Zia features) and "Zia - Administrative" (auto tags, thread keywords); GenAI config needs Zia enabled in profile — UI: Setup > User Management > Profiles — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/intelligence/articles/zia-generated-thread-level-keywords-for-tickets)
- **ZZ-03** Zia capabilities overview with editions, DCs and language master sheet (Insights, sentiment, auto tag, keywords, notifications, anomaly, field prediction, summary, GenAI, answer bot, reply/writing assistance, content analysis) — UI: n/a — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/overview/articles/understanding-zia-s-capabilities)
- **ZZ-04** Native Zia LLM privacy: hosted on Zoho servers in the customer's DC, no data retention beyond generation, never trained on customer data (only KB), GDPR — UI: n/a — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/setting-up-the-generative-ai-services-zia-open-source-model-chatgpt-deepseek)
- **ZZ-05** ChatGPT data handling: masks IP, email, contact name, phone, IBAN, card numbers and any PII/PHI-marked fields; attachments, account/contact info and to/cc/bcc not sent; PII typed inside threads/comments not masked; GDPR + HIPAA — UI: n/a — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/integration-of-zia-with-openai-chatgpt-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZZ-06** DC availability: GenAI (ChatGPT) US/EU/IN/AU/JP/CA/UAE; native Zia US/EU/IN/AU/JP/CA/CN/UAE per pricing (Qwen article: US/IN/AU/EU/JP/CA); DeepSeek CN only — UI: n/a — Ed: n/d — [src](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)

### 20.2 Generative AI services setup
- **ZZ-07** Choose one GenAI service for the whole org: Zia (Qwen, 30B default), ChatGPT (OpenAI key), Azure OpenAI, or DeepSeek (CN); switching applies to all departments — UI: Setup > Zia > Generative AI > Services — Ed: Express+ (BYOK), Professional+ (native) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/setting-up-the-generative-ai-services-zia-open-source-model-chatgpt-deepseek)
- **ZZ-08** ChatGPT model choice: GPT-3.5 Turbo (0125), GPT-4o (default), GPT-4 Turbo, GPT-4o Mini, GPT-4.1 Mini, GPT-4.1, GPT-5.1, GPT-5.4 Mini, GPT-5.4; pricing shown per model; pay-as-you-go OpenAI key — UI: Generative AI > General preference — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/setting-up-the-generative-ai-services-zia-open-source-model-chatgpt-deepseek)
- **ZZ-09** API key ownership: only the admin who configured the OpenAI key can edit it (else contact support); disable/re-enable keeps the key — UI: Generative AI toggle — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/integration-of-zia-with-openai-chatgpt-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZZ-10** Azure OpenAI: Resource URL, Deployment Name, Model, API Key, accept pricing notice, Configure; Zia Actions not supported with Azure — UI: Services > Azure OpenAI > Enable — Ed: Express+ (BYOK, inferred) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/setting-up-the-generative-ai-services-zia-open-source-model-chatgpt-deepseek)
- **ZZ-11** DeepSeek BYOK for China DC; Answer Bot not in Chinese so Reply Assistance gives no KB suggestions for Chinese tickets — UI: Setup > Zia > Generative AI — Ed: Express+ (BYOK) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/setting-up-the-generative-ai-services-zia-open-source-model-chatgpt-deepseek)
- **ZZ-12** Feature preferences per department: Insights, Summary, Writing Assistance, Reply Assistance (independent per department; service common to org) plus org toggles Article Generation, Zia Actions, Deluge Assistant Bot — UI: Generative AI > Feature Preferences > Department — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/setting-up-the-generative-ai-services-zia-open-source-model-chatgpt-deepseek)
- **ZZ-13** Generation preference for Insights and Summary: Generate on click / Auto generate (on ticket open) / Dynamic (default, follows agent's last behaviour) — UI: Feature Preferences > Configure — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/setting-up-the-generative-ai-services-zia-open-source-model-chatgpt-deepseek)
- **ZZ-14** Reply Assistance source option (ChatGPT): Open-domain data, Knowledge Base articles, or both; KB first then open domain; KB option needs Answer Bot trained + ≥1 published article — UI: Feature Preferences — Ed: Express+ (KB mode Standard+) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/integration-of-zia-with-openai-chatgpt-in-zoho-desk)
- **ZZ-15** Zia vs ChatGPT comparison: no key vs key; KB-only vs KB+open domain; data stays in Zoho vs sent to OpenAI; free vs token billing — UI: n/a — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/comparing-zia-and-chatgpt-in-desk)
- **ZZ-16** Languages: GenAI 33–35 languages; summary translation; Answer Bot 29 (pricing says 30) — UI: n/a — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/answer-bot/articles/ai-based-self-service-with-answer-bot)
- **ZZ-17** Disable Zia auto-suggestion that opens in iBar on Reply All — UI: Setup > Personalization > Preferences > Auto Suggestions — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/integration-of-zia-with-openai-chatgpt-in-zoho-desk)

- **ZZ-18** Zia Insights: Sentiment (green positive / orange neutral / red negative), Tone (formal, casual…), Key Topics; uses incoming threads — UI: Ticket detail > Ticket Properties > Zia Insights — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/ai-powered-services-for-tickets-insights-summary-reply-generator-writing-assistance-using-zia)
- **ZZ-19** Ticket Summary: bullet summary of latest 30 conversations (5 with ChatGPT per overview), conversation-type filter (incoming, outgoing, forwarded, public, private comment), each conversation ≥50 words, Regenerate, Translate — UI: Ticket detail > Summary — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/ai-powered-services-for-tickets-insights-summary-reply-generator-writing-assistance-using-zia)

- **ZZ-23** Writing Assistance: select sentence(s) in reply editor, rewrite by tone, length, language — UI: Reply / Reply All editor > select text > Writing Assistance — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/ai-powered-services-for-tickets-insights-summary-reply-generator-writing-assistance-using-zia)

### 20.4 Article generation (Ticket to Article / IDP)
- **ZZ-27** Ticket to Article: select 1–10 conversations (incoming, forwarded, agent replies, public/private comments) of one ticket; creates KB draft with default agent-only visibility to edit (meta, SEO) and publish — UI: Ticket detail > Zia > Ticket to Article > Generate Article — Ed: Express+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/generative-ai/articles/intelligent-document-processing-generate-articles-from-ticket-conversation)

- **ZZ-36** Answer Bot for agents: chat from ticket; shows article permissions of sources; auto-opens on Reply All; "No bot is created" error when no department bot — UI: Ticket detail > iBar > Zia > Answer Bot — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/answer-bot/articles/ai-based-self-service-with-answer-bot)
- **ZZ-37** Answer Bot for customers: help center, websites, landing pages via ASAP script; iOS/Android via Mobile SDK — UI: ASAP / Help Center — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/answer-bot/articles/ai-based-self-service-with-answer-bot)

### 20.6 Intelligence: sentiment, auto tags, thread keywords
- **ZZ-41** Native Sentiment Analysis on latest incoming thread (Positive/Neutral/Negative from key phrases); separate from GenAI Insights; used in automation and Zia dashboard; 12 languages; model upgrade (Update button; old model deprecated 6 Jun 2026) — UI: Setup > Zia > Intelligence > Sentiment Analysis — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/overview/articles/analyze-business-performance-and-improve-decision-making-with-zia-dashboard)
- **ZZ-42** Ticket Auto Tags: clusters keywords from history, tags new tickets and each customer reply, keeps earlier tags; per-department toggle; English only — UI: Setup > Zia > Intelligence > Ticket Auto Tags — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/intelligence/articles/understanding-zia-auto-tags)
- **ZZ-43** Auto Tag training needs ≥3,000 tickets from email, help center, web forum, community, social (chat, IM, phone, custom channels excluded); can enable earlier but no tagging; Manage Auto Tags shown only above 3,000 — UI: same — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/intelligence/articles/understanding-zia-auto-tags)
- **ZZ-44** Manage Auto Tags: Change Tag Prediction (rename/pick tag), Delete Tag, Set Auto Tags for a cluster; clusters not editable; manual agent tags not used for training — UI: Setup > Zia > Ticket Auto Tags > Manage Auto Tags — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/intelligence/articles/understanding-zia-auto-tags)
- **ZZ-45** Auto tags shown under Key Information; History shows tag action as admin; usable as workflow criteria — UI: Ticket detail > Properties / History — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/intelligence/articles/understanding-zia-auto-tags)
- **ZZ-46** Thread-level keywords: Zia keywords per customer reply; auto-on when Zia enabled for the dept (only new tickets), off only by disabling Zia for dept; English only; hover # next to contact name; workflow criterion "keyword contains" — UI: Ticket detail thread # icon — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/intelligence/articles/zia-generated-thread-level-keywords-for-tickets)

### 20.7 Predictions: field prediction, anomaly, notifications
- **ZZ-47** Field Predictions: predict system/custom picklist values and ticket owner; picklist must be in default layout; dept needs ≥500 tickets (500 per value recommended); max 20 predictions per department; max 50 values; each value ≥10 tickets to retrain — UI: Setup > Zia > Field Predictions > Create New Field Prediction — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/prediction/articles/zia-field-predictions)
- **ZZ-48** Prediction model: Trainable (training criteria, learns from corrections; 80/20 train/test; accuracy score) or Ready to use (pre-trained, instant) — UI: Prediction Setup > Choose AI model — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/prediction/articles/zia-field-predictions)
- **ZZ-49** Prediction behaviour: run on Ticket creation, Customer reply or both; Update field automatically vs Suggest value for agent review; confidence score threshold (70 recommended); workflows fire only if their trigger is Create/Customer reply — UI: Prediction Setup — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/prediction/articles/zia-field-predictions)
- **ZZ-50** Enable each prediction after training completes; list shows accuracy and training status; <70% must be annotated before enabling — UI: Field Predictions list toggle — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/prediction/articles/zia-field-predictions)
- **ZZ-51** Prediction Playground: test subject/description or a ticket; shows predicted values with confidence (red below, green above); Reset; only for models with accuracy >0% — UI: Field Predictions > More > Playground — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/prediction/articles/zia-field-predictions)
- **ZZ-52** Ticket Annotation: tabs Trained, Unannotated, Annotated (Annotated / Overridden filters); bulk value update; Retrain Zia > Confirm — UI: Field Predictions > predictor — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/prediction/articles/zia-field-predictions)
- **ZZ-53** Import tickets for retraining: optional criteria, Fetch Tickets, per-value counts (max 500 per value), import status in Zia Notification Alerts — UI: Field Predictions > Annotate > Unannotated > Import — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/prediction/articles/zia-field-predictions)
- **ZZ-54** Add to Prediction from a ticket with chosen values — UI: Ticket detail > More > Add to Prediction — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/prediction/articles/zia-field-predictions)
- **ZZ-55** Anomaly prediction per department for incoming and/or outgoing: threshold 5 / 25 / 50 tickets, or custom criteria (volume band 6–25, 26–50, 51–75, 76–100, >100 × deviation 10/25/50/100%/dynamic; both must match); business-hours notification toggle; trained on last 30 days — UI: Setup > Zia > Anomaly — Ed: Professional+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/prediction/articles/anomaly-prediction-for-incoming-and-outgoing-tickets)
- **ZZ-56** Zia Notifications panel: Anomalies and Alerts (Answer Bot training, auto-tag creation, annotated status, import status); red icon on bottom bar; kept 15 days; recipients Agents / Teams / Roles / Roles and Subordinates — UI: Setup > Zia > Intelligence > Zia Notifications; bottom bar icon — Ed: Standard+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/zia/overview/articles/analyze-business-performance-and-improve-decision-making-with-zia-dashboard)

- **ZZ-70** Digital agent behaviour: listed wherever agents are listed, always offline (round robin needs "assign to offline agents"), cannot do blueprint transitions, text only (no attachments/images), GPT-4o Mini, no data stored, disable only (no delete), loop risk with automation — UI: assignment rules, workflows — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/agentic-ai/articles/agentic-ai-in-desk-support-specialist-and-resolution-expert)

- **ZZ-72** Disable / downgrade: disabled agents stop acting but past actions stay; on downgrade agents stay assigned but do nothing (reassign via custom view + mass action) — UI: Zia Agents > disable — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/agentic-ai/articles/agentic-ai-in-desk-support-specialist-and-resolution-expert)

### Gaps / thin docs
- No REST API operations for Zia features (predictions, sentiment, summaries, answer bot) found in the API op list.
- Edition conflicts: pricing lists Answer Bot as Standard+, a ChatGPT FAQ says KB reply assistance needs Answer Bot "only available in the Enterprise plan"; summary scope 30 vs 5 conversations with ChatGPT; Answer Bot 29 vs 30 languages; GenAI 33 vs 35 languages; summary translation 15 vs 35 languages; Zia Agent credits 3,000 vs 8,000/25,000; agent model GPT-4o Mini vs "ChatGPT Mini 4.1".
- Edition of Azure OpenAI, IM right-panel features, IM translation and GC GenAI block not stated.
- Tone label list and Insights language coverage not fully documented; Zia Agent Studio/Store limits beyond the pricing row not in Desk help.
- Sentiment Analysis setup steps (department toggle, languages list) only described indirectly.
