## 21. Mobile apps and Radar (ZM)

Edition note: The pricing page lists "Zoho Desk app" (the agent app for iOS and Android) and "Radar app for Zoho Desk" as Y in all editions (Free to Enterprise). On-Demand Mobile Apps and the Mobile App Gallery are Enterprise-only paid add-ons. Only a help desk administrator can request them, and each is priced per project. Mobile SDK (for building your own apps) is Professional+. Inside the apps, each feature still depends on the matching web feature and its edition (blueprint, custom modules, Zia GenAI, WhatsApp/IM). Most configuration (views, macros, blueprints, custom buttons, templates, IM channels, portals) can only be done on the web. The apps use that configuration and pick up changes through "Refresh Portal Metadata". [pricing](https://www.zoho.com/desk/pricing-comparison.html)

### 21.1 Agent app: account, sign-in, settings
- **ZM-01** Sign up from the app (iOS+Android): Sign Up opens a zoho.com form (full name, email, password, phone), then an OTP to the phone, then Create Portal (company name, portal URL, phone). This creates a Free account. Upgrading to Standard/Pro/Ent is only possible on the web, during or after the 15-day trial. Android also offers Gmail sign-up — UI: launch screen > Sign Up — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/getting-started/articles/signing-up-for-zoho-desk-account-in-mobile-android) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/get-started/articles/signing-up-for-zoho-desk-account-in-ios)
- **ZM-02** Sign in (iOS+Android): email + password. Agents can sign in only after an admin adds them on the web and they accept the invite email. Android also allows Google; iOS allows Apple, Google or LinkedIn. Sign-in lands on Ticket views — UI: launch screen > Sign in — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/getting-started/articles/logging-in-to-your-zoho-desk-account-in-mobile-android) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/get-started/articles/signing-in-to-your-zoho-desk-account-in-ios)
- **ZM-03** Sign out with a confirmation prompt (iOS+Android). On iOS the agent can set a passcode from the sign-out pop-up — UI: Android Hamburger > Settings > Power icon; iOS More > Settings > Power icon — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/getting-started/articles/logging-out-of-your-zoho-desk-account-in-mobile-android) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/get-started/articles/signing-out-of-your-zoho-desk-account-ios)
- **ZM-04** Close/delete the Zoho account from the app (Android): re-enter the password on accounts.zoho.com, review the linked apps and orgs, pick a reason and add feedback, then Close Account. Free accounts only: a paid account must be cancelled or downgraded first. The flow resumes if the app is closed partway. The email can be reused afterwards — UI: Settings > Privacy and Security > Close Account — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/deleting-the-zoho-desk-account)
- **ZM-05** Portal and department switching (iOS+Android): view the portals the user belongs to and switch between them. Portals and departments can only be created on the web. On iOS, use Ticket Views > Change, which also has a department search — UI: Android Settings > portal name; iOS Ticket Views > Change — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/selecting-portal-preference-in-android) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/changing-portals-and-departments-in-ios)
- **ZM-06** Portal access restriction for mobile: admins can limit which portals an agent can open in the mobile app, matching web access. Documented for iOS — UI: n/d (setting location not in text) — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/account-setup/articles/setting-portal-access-restriction-in-ios)
- **ZM-07** Refresh Portal Metadata (iOS+Android): pulls web admin changes into the app, such as signatures, validation rules, layout rules, blueprints, custom modules, formula fields and attachment restrictions. Some options, such as Auto CC, are not covered — UI: Settings > Refresh Portal Metadata — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/selecting-portal-preference-in-android) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/account-setup/articles/personalizing-desk-account-in-ios)
- **ZM-08** Synchronize time tracking preferences (manual vs auto) between web and app (iOS+Android) — UI: Settings — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/selecting-portal-preference-in-android)
- **ZM-09** Clear search history (Android) — UI: Settings — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/selecting-portal-preference-in-android)
- **ZM-10** Clear cache (Android): two options, Reset user images and Clear cached attachments, confirmed with OK — UI: Settings > Clear Cache — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/clearing-the-cache-from-the-desk-account-in-android)
- **ZM-11** Reset app (Android): removes all preferences (theme, language, fonts) and the user data stored on the device — UI: Settings > Reset App > OK — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/resetting-the-android-app)
- **ZM-12** Default attachment download setting (Android): Always / Only on WiFi / Never — UI: Settings — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/downloading-attachments-in-android)
- **ZM-13** Agent profile, view-only (iOS+Android): name, email, photo, role/permissions, phone, mobile, extension, language, location, departments, teams, channel expert, bio. Tabs: Tickets (open/on hold/closed), Time Entry (total time and cost), Happiness Rating. Performance (first response, average response, average resolution, happiness) for a chosen date range. Edits only on the web — UI: Settings > View Profile — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/viewing-agent-profile-in-mobile-android) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/account-setup/articles/viewing-agent-profile-in-ios)
- **ZM-14** In-app Zoho Help Centre (iOS+Android): browse the Zoho KB and Community, and submit a request with attachments. Android can also post to Community as a question, discussion, idea or problem, with attachments, preview and save-as-draft — UI: Settings > Help — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/accessing-the-help-centre-in-android) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/account-setup/articles/accessing-the-help-center-in-ios)
- **ZM-15** Feedback (iOS+Android) with attachments and optional system logs and a diagnostic report (ZUID, user agent, device/app version, WebView, notification, encryption, DB and push details). On Android, About also shows the app version and a Rate app option — UI: Settings > Feedback — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/viewing-app-details-in-android) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/account-setup/articles/giving-feedback-in-ios)
- **ZM-16** Legal documents (Android): open source licenses, terms of service, privacy policy — UI: Settings > About — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/viewing-documents-related-to-zoho-desk-in-android)

### 21.2 Agent app: security and privacy
- **ZM-17** Database encryption (iOS+Android): AES encryption of on-device data, can be switched on or off at any time — UI: Settings > Privacy and Security > Enable Encryption — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/enabling-encryption-in-android) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/account-setup/articles/enabling-encryption-in-ios)

- **ZM-19** App lock (iOS+Android): 4- or 6-digit PIN, entered twice to confirm, with Change PIN and Ask for PIN timing. On iOS the user is signed out after 6 failed attempts — UI: Settings > Privacy and Security > Enable App Lock — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/enabling-privacy-and-security-in-android) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/account-setup/articles/enabling-privacy-and-security-in-ios)

- **ZM-21** Show or hide app content in the recent-apps screen (Android); Send diagnostics reports toggle (iOS) — UI: Privacy and Security — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/setting-up-your-account/articles/enabling-privacy-and-security-in-android) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/account-setup/articles/enabling-privacy-and-security-in-ios)

- **ZM-28** Siri Shortcuts (iOS): suggested shortcuts My Open Tickets, My Overdue Tickets, Add Comment, Add Resolution. Tap + to add one and record a custom phrase — UI: Settings > Siri Shortcuts — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/account-setup/articles/siri-shortcuts-in-ios)
- **ZM-29** Quick actions on long-press of a ticket (iOS): move, close/reopen, mark read/unread, assign, share URL, mark as spam, delete — UI: ticket list long-press — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/quick-actions-in-tickets-module-in-ios)

### 21.4 Agent app: ticket list, views, filters
- **ZM-30** Ticket list views (Android): Classic (list) and Table. In Table view the columns can be customised and sorted ascending or descending, and edits sync to the web at once — UI: Tickets home — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/setting-ticket-views)
- **ZM-31** Work modes (iOS+Android): Status (custom statuses supported; iOS calls it Classic mode), Handshake (Customers / Contacts or Leads / New inquiries; needs the CRM integration), Countdown (time left until due), Priority (High/Medium/Low/Other) — UI: Tickets view mode switch — Ed: All (Handshake needs CRM) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/ticket-work-modes-in-mobile) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/ticket-work-modes-in-ios)
- **ZM-32** Starred views (iOS+Android): star/unstar existing views and reorder them; on iOS, swipe a view left and choose Star. Custom views cannot be created on mobile — UI: Views list — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/starring-ticket-views-in-ios)
- **ZM-33** Filter tickets on several fields (iOS+Android). iOS limits: 50 values per field, 250 values in total. Saved filters are private to the agent (iOS max 100) and can be renamed or deleted (with confirmation) — UI: Filter icon > Filter / My saved filters — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/sorting-and-filtering-tickets) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/filtering-and-sorting-tickets-in-ios)
- **ZM-34** Sort tickets (iOS+Android); sort options not listed in the text — UI: Filter icon > Sort — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/sorting-and-filtering-tickets) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/filtering-and-sorting-tickets-in-ios)
- **ZM-35** Search tickets by typing or by voice (Android voice input goes through Google) — UI: Search — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/searching-for-a-ticket)
- **ZM-36** Zia sentiment (Android): overall sentiment shown next to each ticket in the list, and positive/negative/neutral per incoming response, with keyword aspects, in the ticket detail — UI: ticket list / ticket detail — Ed: n/d (Zia) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/sentiment-analysis-in-mobile)

### 21.5 Agent app: ticket create and actions
- **ZM-37** Add ticket (iOS+Android): department, layout (iOS), contact, account, email, phone, subject, rich-text description, status, due date, priority, product, attachments. Android can apply a ticket template (More > Templates) that pre-fills fields. Refresh Layout picks up web layout changes. Mark as unassigned, or assign an agent — UI: Add (+) — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/adding-tickets-and-assigning-agents) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/adding-tickets-and-assigning-agents-in-ios)
- **ZM-38** Send as Email on create (Android): emails the ticket description to the contact. Must be enabled on the web first — UI: Add Ticket > More > Send as Email — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/sending-ticket-details-as-email-in-android)
- **ZM-39** Due date (calendar picker, Done), status and priority, set on create or edited later (iOS+Android) — UI: Add Ticket / tap the field on the ticket — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/setting-ticket-preferences) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/setting-ticket-preferences-in-ios)
- **ZM-40** Ticket actions (iOS+Android): edit, move to another department, apply a macro (macros are created on the web), add followers, send for approval, add tags (with Zia tag suggestions), marketplace extensions (only those installed on the web), Zia GenAI (iOS top band) — UI: ticket detail > More / top and bottom bands — Ed: All (depends on the feature) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/ticket-actions) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/ticket-actions-in-ios)
- **ZM-41** Ticket properties panel (iOS+Android): properties, Zia insights (tone, summary, key topics), contact/account info, created time, department, owner, status, due date, tags, language, priority, channel, product. History can be filtered to responses, activities, comments, approvals or automations. Also shows linked articles (search, share, paste into reply), a timeline of the contact's/account's tickets, and marketplace — UI: ticket detail > Hamburger/Properties — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/viewing-ticket-properties) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/viewing-ticket-details-in-ios)
- **ZM-42** Conversation layouts (iOS): Conversation (threads and comments together, told apart by icon), Threads only, Comments only — UI: ticket detail — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/ticket-detail-view-in-ios)
- **ZM-43** Ticket subtabs (iOS+Android): Resolution (one per ticket; edit, delete, notify the customer), Time entry (billable rates), Attachments, Activities, Marketplace, custom modules (iOS), Parent-child (iOS) — UI: ticket detail subtabs — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/viewing-ticket-subtabs) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/viewing-ticket-subtabs-in-ios)
- **ZM-44** Formula fields, view-only (Android). The feature is in early access, and new fields appear after Refresh Portal Metadata — UI: ticket fields — Ed: n/d (early access) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/viewing-formula-field-in-android)
- **ZM-45** Custom buttons (created on the web) run from the ticket's More menu: open other pages/apps, fill forms, run custom workflows (Android) — UI: ticket > More — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/using-custom-buttons-in-android)
- **ZM-46** Blueprint (iOS+Android): view blueprint info and status, perform transitions (iOS: tap the status), revoke the blueprint. Blueprints are created on the web only — UI: ticket status > blueprint icon — Ed: n/d (web blueprint edition) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/blueprint-in-mobile) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/viewing-blueprint-in-ios)
- **ZM-47** Parent-child tickets (iOS): associate, disassociate and close. Tickets must be in the same department. A parent can have up to 200 children; a child has only one parent. Set up on the web — UI: ticket subtab — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/associating-tickets-as-parent-child-in-ios)
- **ZM-48** Bulk actions on up to 50 tickets (iOS+Android): merge (max 5; single-department view only; choose the master ticket and which fields to keep, then confirm), assign (same department), close (also in All Departments), delete (kept in the recycle bin for 60 days), mark as spam, update. Android supports list and table view — UI: multi-select toolbar — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/bulk-actions-in-mobile) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/performing-bulk-actions-in-ios)

### 21.6 Agent app: replies, comments, attachments
- **ZM-49** Reply (iOS+Android): Reply, Reply all (secondary contacts added as CC), Forward, Split ticket. Send, Send and close, or Send and update (set status). iOS can also save a draft — UI: Reply icon / thread More — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/replying-to-tickets) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/replying-to-tickets-in-ios)
- **ZM-50** Scheduled reply (iOS+Android): presets 10 min / 1 h / 4 h / 12 h, custom duration, or a specific date and time with time zone; Schedule, or Schedule and Close. Once scheduled: reschedule, edit, send now, discard draft, clear schedule, pin the scheduled reply, marketplace extensions. Must be enabled per department on the web — UI: compose > Send dropdown > Scheduled Reply — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/scheduling-replies-to-tickets-in-android) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/scheduling-replies-to-tickets-in-ios)
- **ZM-51** Snippets, templates and KB articles in replies (iOS): view own and shared snippets, insert them, sort by name or by created/modified time. All are created on the web — UI: compose > More > Snippets / Templates / Articles — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/snippets-templates-and-articles-in-ios)
- **ZM-52** Comments (iOS): rich text (bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, font colour, highlight, bullets, alignment), @mentions, inline images added on the web, ticket hyperlinks. The default public/private type is set on the web and synced — UI: ticket > Comment icon — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/adding-comments-in-tickets-in-ios)
- **ZM-53** Pin comments and threads (Android): Visible for all agents (orange pin) or Visible for me (grey pin). Up to 3 of each, so 6 pins per ticket. An admin must enable pinning on the web. Unpin from More or from the pinned item — UI: thread/comment More > Pin — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/pinning-comments-and-threads)
- **ZM-54** Attachments on tickets, replies, comments and activities. Android: gallery, camera, audio, scribble, file (including Google Drive), scan, and a public/private toggle. iOS: camera, voice, files — UI: Attachment icon — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/attaching-a-file) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/attaching-a-file-in-ios)
- **ZM-55** Document scan (Android): manual or auto-capture with auto-crop, or convert an image from the gallery. Output is JPG only; PNG is converted to JPG automatically — UI: Attachment > Scan — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/scanning-and-attaching-documents-android)
- **ZM-56** Restricted attachment types (iOS+Android): the app enforces the org's allowed/blocked file types after a metadata refresh. Settings are made on the web — UI: attachment picker — Ed: Express+ (pricing row "Attachment controls") — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/android/ticket-management/articles/restrict-the-attachment-type-based-on-the-organization-settings-in-mobile-android) / [iOS](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/ios/ticket-management/articles/restrict-the-attachment-type-based-on-the-organisation-settings-in-mobile-ios)

- **ZM-73** Radar overview: companion app with real-time help desk stats. Managers/admins see stats for all agents; agents see only their own — UI: Radar app — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/radar-app-for-managing-your-help-desk)

- **ZM-76** Dark mode and App Defaults — UI: Setup > Themes > Dark Mode; Setup > App Defaults — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/faqs/articles/how-do-i-personalize-radar-to-suit-my-requirements-and-style)
- **ZM-77** Ticket handling in Radar: reply with templates/snippets, comments, resolution, history, time entries; More menu: revoke blueprint, mark as spam, assign, move, delete. Agents can also edit their own profile (My Info > Edit Profile) — UI: Menu > Views > ticket — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/faqs/articles/how-can-support-agents-use-the-radar-app-effectively)
- **ZM-78** Static dashboards: Overview, Ticket status, Customer happiness, SLA, Call, Phone agent availability, Blueprint, Zia. Components can be set to a time frame — UI: Dashboards > Static folder — Ed: All (data depends on web edition) — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/static)
- **ZM-79** Other dashboards: Knowledge Base, Instant Messaging, API, Community — UI: Dashboards > Other folder — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/overview-of-dashboards-in-radar-part-2)
- **ZM-80** Standard reports in Radar (nine in total), for example Agent availability (login/logout times, hours worked, session data) — UI: Reports — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/und)
- **ZM-81** Create custom dashboards: needs the Reports and Dashboards permission. Choose or create a folder; dashboard visibility follows the folder. Add components from Report Analytics (matrix and summary reports only; tabular not supported), Gallery, or Custom Views. Choose a chart type, set grouping for stacked bar, percentage bar and multi-line charts, preview, Add — UI: Dashboards > + — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/creating-dashboards-in-the-radar-app)
- **ZM-82** Global dashboards (iOS+Android): data from all departments together; default "Tickets by department". Save a new dashboard in the Global Dashboards folder to make it global; any other folder makes it department-level — UI: Dashboards > Global Dashboards — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/understanding-global-dashboards-in-radar)
- **ZM-83** Agent details page: a fixed Quick Information card (name, role/profile, availability, all/open/overdue requests, happiness rating) plus customisable cards (agent info, ticket status, response times, CSAT) — UI: Agents > agent — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/customizing-the-agent-card-in-radar)
- **ZM-84** Contacts card: enable it from the Radar Store. Contact views, and a contact detail with tickets, interactions and activities. Needs the Customers permission — UI: Menu > Contacts — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/contacts-card-in-radar)
- **ZM-85** Accounts card: account views, tickets, activities, interactions, products, attachments, time entries. Needs the Customers permission — UI: Menu > Accounts — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/accounts-card-in-radar-2-6-2026)
- **ZM-86** Activities card: tasks, calls and events in one view; filter, sort, view details, manage — UI: Menu > Activities — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/activities-card-in-radar)
- **ZM-87** Custom modules in Radar: enable each module in the Radar Store; view and update records according to profile and module permissions — UI: Menu — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/custom-modules-in-radar)
- **ZM-88** My Pins: pin tickets, customers, activities, dashboards, and ticket or activity views to the Radar home; grid or list layout; up to 12 items; no special permission needed — UI: Home > My Pins — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/pinning-items-to-the-radar-home)
- **ZM-89** Exception alerts: set benchmarks (for example average first response time, handling time, happiness, FCR) and get notified when a metric deviates. Daily Toast sends daily ticket stats. Needs push notifications — UI: Setup > exception handling — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/setting-up-exception-alerts-in-radar)
- **ZM-90** Radar permissions (support administrators only): for each default (Administrator, Agent, Light Agent) or custom profile, switch features on or off. Groups: Insights, Ticket management tools, Radar-exclusive (for example Daily toast), Developer space. Everything is on by default except text and video content protection — UI: Setup > Radar Permissions — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/setting-up-radar-permissions)
- **ZM-91** Audit Log card: date and time, details, performed by, IP address, department, entity, action, sub action — UI: Menu > Audit Log — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/audit-log-in-radar)
- **ZM-92** Radar privacy and security: app lock with biometrics or a 4-digit PIN; sign-out after 6 failed attempts; locks as soon as the app is closed unless Ask for Pin is changed; content protection — UI: Setup > Privacy and Security — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/understanding-privacy-and-security-settings-in-radar)
- **ZM-93** Use Device Timezone toggle; by default Radar uses the Desk account/user time zone — UI: Setup > Use Device Timezone — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/making-radar-follow-your-device-s-time-zone)
- **ZM-94** Multiple languages: Radar follows the device language automatically and falls back to English if that language is not supported — UI: device setting — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/multiple-language-support)
- **ZM-95** Zia GenAI in Radar: ticket and thread summary (last 30 conversations; at least one conversation of 50+ words), regenerate, translate, reply generation. Needs Zia GenAI enabled on the web — UI: Views > ticket > Zia — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/using-zia-generative-ai-in-radar)
- **ZM-96** Apple Intelligence (iOS, supported iPhones only): Writing Tools (proofread, rewrite, summarize, compose) in the reply editor, comment editor, team feeds and feedback; Image Playground for the profile photo — UI: editor > Writing Tools — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/working-with-apple-intelligence-in-radar)
- **ZM-97** Siri Shortcuts in Radar: "Hey Siri, show <widget>" / "open <insight>" — UI: Radar Siri setup — Ed: All — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/configuring-siri-shortcuts-in-the-radar-app)
- **ZM-98** Zoho Lens from a ticket: live video session with a remote expert who can annotate. The session summary and screenshots are attached to the ticket automatically. An admin must enable the integration — UI: ticket > Zoho Lens — Ed: n/d — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/radar/general/articles/zoho-lens-in-radar)

### 21.10 On-demand apps, App Gallery, mobile experience
- **ZM-99** On-demand mobile apps: custom apps built on the Desk mobile SDK, priced by build hours. Enterprise only — UI: request from within Zoho Desk — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/on-demand-mobile-app/articles/understanding-on-demand-mobile-apps)
- **ZM-100** On-demand legal terms: needs an active Enterprise subscription; only an Account Administrator can submit a request, which must include a clear description. These terms apply in addition to the Zoho ToS/MSA — UI: n/a — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/on-demand-mobile-app/articles/legal-terms-for-on-demand-mobile-apps)
- **ZM-101** Mobile App Gallery: curated apps by industry, function and role; iOS and Android; rebranded (name, logo, colours, splash screen, icon) and customisable. Enterprise add-on, priced per app, requested by an admin — UI: n/d — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/mobile-app-gallery-1/articles/understanding-the-mobile-app-gallery)
- **ZM-102** Grievance redressal app (gallery): citizens file complaints with images, anonymously or after OTP phone login; track status; read FAQs and announcements. Officers who sign in with agent credentials get a ticketing workspace — UI: gallery app — Ed: Enterprise — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/mobile-app-gallery-1/articles/grievance-redressal-app)
- **ZM-103** Mobile experience overview: prebuilt apps (Zoho Desk app, Radar), on-demand custom apps, and SDKs (pricing row "Mobile SDK" is Professional+) — UI: n/a — Ed: All / SDK Pro+ — [src](https://help.zoho.com/portal/en/kb/desk/mobile-apps/mobile-experience/articles/mobile-experience-in-zoho-desk)

### Gaps / thin docs
- Many articles rely on screenshots or videos and their step text is cut short: manual and voice search steps, adding a resolution, iOS account search, where the iOS portal access restriction is set, iOS notification settings.
- Platform coverage is uneven. iOS docs do not cover swipe customization, clear cache, reset app or the encryption toggle path. Android docs do not cover parent-child, snippets/templates in reply, comment formatting or the home widget. Parity between the platforms on these is not confirmed.
- Radar: the full list of the nine reports, the exception metrics list, the supported language list, the permission needed for the Audit Log card and the full Radar Store card list were not fully extracted from the text read.
- Edition gating of features inside the apps (sentiment, blueprint, IM/WhatsApp, custom modules, Zia GenAI, Zoho Lens, Audit Log) is not stated in the mobile docs; it follows the matching web feature (shown as n/d).
- No mobile-specific API operations are documented.
